and the reason why that projection piece has to be so tightly bound to the lower foundations is because rendering media is where all your energy and money gets burned if the underlying logic slips. if a character loses an arm or drops an iron key in scene one and then magically uses both hands to pick it up three scenes later the audience checks out and every single gpu cycle spent generating those frames was completely wasted. so you cant just let scenes float around as loose text files waiting for a human script supervisor to catch a continuity leak. the ledger has to stop acting like a passive wiki and start behaving like an automated compiler toolchain. you have to treat the entire fictional reality like software where every single fact is an upstream source dependency and every written scene is a downstream build artifact. that way when a fact mutates in the lore the engine immediately knows which downstream scenes are broken and dirty before anything ever gets sent to render. to pull that off in sqlite we have to ground the contraption into a concrete schema where three tables handle the facts the scenes and the exact pinned versions linking them together:


The ledger being described is the **Facts Ledger** (also referred to as the **Canonical Narrative Facts Ledger**, **Stage A: Canonical Narrative Facts**, or **CANON_DB** within the **Minimal Validated Core**).

### Key Details & Context

* **Primary Purpose**: It serves as an immutable, low-overhead relational database (implemented in local SQLite or Cloudflare D1) that acts as the single source of truth for narrative continuity and physical reality before downstream media generation, AI text expansion, or GPU rendering occurs.


* **The "Build System" Model**: It functions analogously to tools like `make` or `Bazel`, treating atomic canonical facts as upstream source dependencies and authored scenes as downstream compiled artifacts. When an upstream fact's version increments (`NEW.version > OLD.version`), an automated SQL trigger evaluates the staleness predicate against `scene_fact_dependencies` and marks downstream scenes as `is_dirty = 1` (`status = 'dirty'`), revoking their certification.


* **Distinction from the Event Ledger**:
* **The Event Ledger (`event_log`)**: The append-only, irreversible historical log recording causality, state transitions, and actions ("what happened and why") via pure functional reduction ($S_{n+1} = \mathcal{R}(S_n, E_n, \sigma_n)$).


* **The Facts Ledger (`facts`, `scenes`, `scene_fact_dependencies`)**: The materialized relational working view representing ground-truth physical reality ("what is true right now") used for cache invalidation, dependency pinning, and blast-radius tracking.




* **Schema Implementation**: It is formally established in the project's Stage A flat schema migration (`migrations/0001_phase0_flat_schema.sql`) across the `facts`, `scenes`, and `scene_fact_dependencies` tables.

**Yes, exactly.** The fundamental architectural thesis of the project is to treat a long-running, serialized fictional universe as a **software codebase** governed by a deterministic build system (like `make`, `Bazel`, or a compiler toolchain).

Instead of relying on human script supervisors or fallible memory across 10 years and hundreds of episodes, narrative continuity is converted into a **Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) and cache-invalidation problem**:

---

### 1. The Core Metaphor: Narrative as a Build Graph

* **Canonical Facts are Upstream Source Code:** Ground-truth realities—such as an amputated limb, an item held in a specific pocket, or a character's secret—are stored as discrete, versioned nodes in a relational ledger (SQLite / Cloudflare D1).


* **Scenes and Episodes are Downstream Build Targets:** Scenes explicitly pin the exact version of the facts they consume (`depends_on: [fact_the_pilot_hand_injury@v1]`).


* **Cache Invalidation ($O(1)$ Blast-Radius Sweeps):** When an author updates a past event or character state (e.g., a duel in Episode 2 escalates a wound from version `v1` to `v2`), an automated database trigger sweeps downstream dependency edges and immediately marks affected scenes as `is_dirty = 1`.


* **Incremental Re-rendering:** Like rebuilding only modified files in a program, the pipeline only halts and flags the specific downstream scenes, audio stems, and JSX composition templates linked to that mutated fact, leaving the rest of the multi-season catalog untouched.



---

### 2. The $T_0 = \$0.00$ Economic Firewall

Generative AI models (LLMs, neural voice synthesizers, diffusion models) are treated as **untrusted, stateless rendering workers** with zero authority over physical reality.

If an LLM hallucinates an amputee climbing a ladder or picking up a weapon with a severed hand, waiting to catch that error after rendering wastes hundreds of dollars in GPU hours and video compositing. By compiling the screenplay through somatic and logistical invariant linters, contradiction checks happen at compile time ($T_0 = \$0.00$), preventing broken scripts from ever reaching a GPU or voice synthesizer.

---

### 3. The Counter-Hazard: "Epistemological Calcification"

While the compiler achieves flawless **Downward Consistency** (forcing every generated line in Episode 80 to obey established lore), the documentation highlights a critical architectural danger: **The Silent Narrative Prison**:

* **The Loss of Human Friction:** In a traditional writers' room, lossy human memory forces writers to re-read and debate old bibles. If a throwaway plot point written under deadline in Episode 2 turns out to be boring or restrictive, human writers naturally renegotiate or forget it.


* **The Calcification Trap:** Because an automated compiler never forgets and never forces writers to re-read older lore, a casual note (e.g., *"the culvert valve requires a bronze key"*) is enforced with machine perfection forever. As subsequent episodes pile on dependent edges ($k_{\text{in}} = 1 \to 12 \to 45$), the fact becomes an unchangeable structural obstacle that strangles future dramatic avenues.



To prevent this, the compiler pairs downward consistency with **Upward Scrutiny**:

1. **The "Still Good" Protocol:** The system monitors graph centrality ($k_{\text{in}}$) and flags high-blast-radius facts that haven't been reviewed in $>60$ days, asking the human showrunner: *"Does this constraint still serve the drama?"*.


2. **Tri-State Standing Lifecycle:** Facts are staged through states (`ACTIVE_ENDORSED`, `FLAGGED_FOR_REVIEW`, and `DEPRECATED`) so writers can debate and retcon lore in a liminal holding state without instantly breaking or re-rendering all active downstream production lines.

In this architecture, **Downward Consistency** is the mathematical and programmatic guarantee that every downstream narrative artifact—every generated screenplay line, Dialogue Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), actor kinetic action, inventory state, audio stem, and visual composition plate—strictly adheres to declared canonical facts ($B \implies A$).

The compiler achieves flawless downward consistency by treating narrative canon not as a passive wiki or prompt guideline, but as **executable source code within a deterministic, multi-pass compilation pipeline** governed by pure functions, linear satisfiability solvers, and relational dependency build graphs.

---

### 1. The Legislative Source of Truth & The Deletion Principle

To enforce absolute consistency across dozens of episodes, canonical reality cannot reside in transient runtime memory, mutable database tables, or stochastic LLM contexts.

* **Physical Anchoring via Byte-0 Markdown Dossiers:** World truth is strictly declared in version-controlled Markdown dossiers stored under physical series paths (e.g., `/characters/*.md`, `/locations/*.md`, `/possessions/*.md`, `/grievances/*.md`).


* **The Byte-0 Frontmatter Law:** Every canonical source file must start its YAML frontmatter strictly at offset Byte 0 (`0x2D 0x2D 0x2D` / `---`). The presence of any leading whitespace, newline, or UTF-8 Byte Order Mark (BOM) causes the compiler to abort immediately at Stage $T_{-1}$.


* **The Refined Deletion Principle:** Downstream runtime databases (such as Cloudflare D1, Durable Objects, or SQLite caches) have zero semantic authority. If all databases, caches, compiled JSON files, and cloud workers are wiped, the entire state of the universe is reconstructed bit-for-bit from the raw Markdown dossiers and the append-only event ledger via a pure mathematical fold:



$$\text{State}_N = \text{fold}(\text{apply}_v, \, \text{State}_0, \, [E_1, E_2, \dots, E_N], \, \sigma)$$





* **Hermetic Series Scoping (`instanceDir` Isolation):** When the compiler (`compileLoreInstance`) is invoked for a specific series slug, its in-memory entity index is strictly isolated to `series/<series-slug>/`. A character in one series attempting to reference an item or room in another series immediately fails compilation, preventing cross-series continuity leaks.



---

### 2. The Three-Pass Offline Compilation Gauntlet (`scripts/compile.ts`)

Before any downstream engine can boot, `scripts/compile.ts` executes an offline, three-pass validation and sealing gauntlet at zero compute cost ($T_0 = \$0.00$). The compiler is a **pure function**: it makes zero network calls, maintains no cross-execution state, and bans ambient system clocks (e.g., `Date.now()`, `performance.now()`).

```text
/characters/*.md ──┐
/grievances/*.md ──┼─► [ PASS 1: SHAPES & SCALARS ] ─► [ PASS 2: RELATIONAL INTEGRITY ] ─► [ PASS 3: DETERMINISTIC SEAL ]
/locations/*.md  ──┤       (Zod Schemas & Basis Pts)          (Foreign Keys & DFS DAG)         (RFC 8785 JCS & H_root)
/possessions/*.md──┘                                                                                      │
                                                                                                          ▼
                                                                                              compiled/bible-state.json

```



#### Pass 1: Structural Shapes, Scalar Bounds & Basis-Point Quantization

* **Strict Zod Schema Enforcement:** Frontmatter is extracted via `gray-matter` and evaluated against closed, rigid TypeScript schemas (e.g., `CharacterSchema`, `GrievanceSchema`, `LocationSchema`, `PossessionSchema`). Any un-schemaed keys or duplicate entity IDs throw an immediate `DUPLICATE_ID` or fatal exit.


* **Basis-Point Quantization ($0 \text{ to } 10{,}000\text{ bp}$):** To eliminate cross-platform floating-point divergence (IEEE 754 variances between x86 and ARM), floating-point numbers are prohibited in canonical schemas. All severities, acoustic modifiers, friction scores, and probabilities are stored as integer basis points ($0 \le x \le 10{,}000$, where $10{,}000 = 1.0$).


* **Exhaustive Banker's Rounding (Round-Half-Even):** Chained scalar calculations enforce integer arithmetic with strict left-associativity $\text{Scale}(\text{Scale}(A, B), C)$:



$$\text{Scale}(A, B) = \text{RoundEven}\left(\frac{A \times B}{10{,}000}\right)$$



Let $P = A \times B$, $Q = \lfloor P / 10{,}000 \rfloor$, and $R = P \pmod{10{,}000}$. The tie-breaking rule at $R = 5{,}000$ checks the least significant bit of $Q$:



$$\text{Scale}(A, B) = \begin{cases} Q & \text{if } R < 5{,}000 \\ Q + 1 & \text{if } R > 5{,}000 \\ Q & \text{if } R = 5{,}000 \text{ and } Q \pmod 2 = 0 \quad (\text{even}) \\ Q + 1 & \text{if } R = 5{,}000 \text{ and } Q \pmod 2 \neq 0 \quad (\text{odd}) \end{cases}$$






#### Pass 2: Relational Integrity, Foreign-Key Sweeps & DAG Acyclicity

* **Referential Closure & Dangling Pointer Elimination:** The compiler constructs an in-memory cross-reference index. Every equip slot (`logistical.held`, `worn`, `carried`, `cached`) must resolve to a valid file in `possessions/`. Every participant in `participants_primary` must resolve to an active file in `characters/`. Every spatial portal must be reciprocal. Any dangling pointer halts the build with an immediate `RELATIONAL_INTEGRITY` Hard Fail.


* **Three-Color Depth-First Search (DFS) Cycle Detection:** When grievances spawn child grievances upon reaching max escalation (`spawns_on_max_escalation`), causal recursion loops ($A \to B \to C \to A$) would throw runtime narrative engines into infinite simulation loops. The compiler models escalation as a directed graph $\mathcal{G} = (\mathcal{V}, \mathcal{E})$ and tracks three distinct node colors:


* **WHITE (0):** Unvisited node.


* **GRAY (1):** Actively on the current recursion call stack.


* **BLACK (2):** Fully verified acyclic subtree.


* If traversal encounters an outgoing edge targeting a **GRAY** node, a back-edge is mathematically proven; the compiler halts with `CYCLE_DETECTED` and dumps the exact cycle path to `stderr`. Permitted confluent structures (diamond dependencies where two separate conflicts unlock the same terminal climax) are validated and preserved.





#### Pass 3: Deterministic Sealing & Dual-Emission Snapshots

* **RFC 8785 Canonical JSON Scheme (JCS):** All dictionary and object keys at every nested level are sorted lexicographically by UTF-16 code units / UTF-8 byte order to eliminate V8 object-property order non-determinism. Array ordering is strictly preserved because arrays encode causal timelines and chronological beats.


* **Unicode Normalization Form C (NFC):** All text strings undergo `str.normalize('NFC')` to eliminate visual-equivalence divergence between precomposed characters and combining sequences across different operating systems.


* **Merkle Root Digest ($H_{\text{root}}$):** The compiler computes the canonical 64-character SHA-256 hash across the canonicalized byte stream:



$$H_{\text{root}} = \text{SHA-256}\Big(\text{RFC8785\_Canonicalize}(\text{bible-state.json})\Big)$$





* **Atomic Sibling Inode Renames:** To prevent concurrent processes (e.g., test daemons, UI runners) from reading torn pages or half-written buffers, output is written to a temporary sibling file (`compiled/bible-state.json.tmp_[UUIDv7]`) on the same physical filesystem and atomically committed via `fs.renameSync`.


* **Dual-Emission Snapshots:** The compiler atomically writes two files:


1. `compiled/<slug>/bible-state.json`: The un-timestamped canonical active HEAD consumed by downstream runtimes.


2. `compiled/<slug>/bible-state_<TIMESTAMP>.json`: An immutable WORM archive stamped with a Windows-safe ISO 8601 timestamp (`YYYY-MM-DDTHH-mm-ssZ`) committed to Git for exact historical reproducibility.





---

### 3. The Multi-Tier Compile-Time Invariant Gauntlet ($T_0 = \$0.00$)

When candidate screenplays or scenes are generated, they must pass through a deterministic five-tier invariant ladder before touching any expensive downstream model (audio synthesizer, diffusion renderer, or video compositor):

```text
[ TIER 0: BOUNDARY & LINT ]       ──► 7-bit ASCII, zero-token regex, no runtime Markdown ($0.00)
│ Pass
▼
[ TIER 1: DEEP-FREEZE IMMUTABILITY ] ─► Recursive Object.freeze(); memory tampering kills runtime
│ Pass
▼
[ TIER 2: SOMATIC SAT & MOTOR AST ]──► Linear arithmetic: slots <= limbs; banned kinetic verbs mask
│ Pass
▼
[ TIER 3: EPISTEMIC HORIZON ]      ──► Ignorance-by-Omission; taboo masks against BeliefRecord
│ Pass
▼
[ TIER 4: MERKLE DRIFT & MUTEX ]   ──► Assert H_rootUI == H_rootLedger; 2PC entity lockouts

```



* **Tier 0 (Boundary & Zero-Token Lint):** Static checks enforcing pure 7-bit ASCII telemetry ($0\text{--}127$). Forbids any runtime Markdown parsing; all downstream services are forced to consume pre-compiled JSON snapshots.


* **Tier 1 (Deep-Freeze Defensive Immutability):** Ingested JSON payloads are wrapped via recursive `deepFreeze<T>()`. If a runtime simulation engine or worker script attempts to mutate an in-memory canonical baseline (e.g., `char.logistical.held.push(...)`), the JavaScript runtime immediately throws an unhandled `TypeError`, instantly killing the illegal mutation.


* **Tier 2 (Somatic SAT & Dual-Field AST Motor Linters):**
* Linear arithmetic verifies somatic equipment limits against anatomical capacity:



$$\sum_{s \in \text{slots}} s.\text{occupies\_hands} \le 2.0 - \text{hand\_impairment\_penalty}$$





* The compiler requires scenes to be structured as a **Dual-Field AST** (pairing prose with explicit machine verbs, e.g., `["LIMP", "REACH_LEFT"]`).


* It performs an $O(1)$ set-intersection check against the character's compiled `banned_kinetic_verbs` mask (e.g., `["climb", "bimanual_lift"]`). If an amputee character is described climbing a ladder, the compiler halts before any tokens or frames are generated.




* **Tier 3 (Epistemic Firewall & Ignorance by Omission):** Enforces negative context constraints over character dialogue. Spoken tokens are checked against forbidden propositions derived from the character's active `BeliefRecord`:



$$\text{Spoken Tokens} \cap \text{Forbidden Propositions} = \emptyset$$



A character structurally cannot speak secrets or react to world events that have not been registered in their subjective knowledge horizon.


* **Tier 4 (Cryptographic Drift & Entity Mutexes):** Asserts that the active UI state hash matches the ledger's canonical Merkle root digest ($H_{\text{rootUI}} \equiv H_{\text{rootLedger}}$). If a concurrent process has updated the ledger, the candidate scene is blocked until re-synchronized.



---

### 4. Relational Dependency Pinning & Reactive Invalidation

Downward consistency across multi-season series is maintained in SQLite / Cloudflare D1 via an explicit build graph modeled after software compilation tools (`make` or `Bazel`).

```text
  [ facts table ] ─────────► [ scene_fact_dependencies ] ◄───────── [ scenes table ]
  (Atomic canonical claims)   (Pins scene_id to fact_id & v_pinned)    (status: 'clean'|'dirty')
             │                                                                   ▲
             └─────── [ UPDATE facts SET version = NEW.version ] ────────────────┘
                      Propagate Mutation Trigger: SET status = 'dirty' WHERE pinned_version < current_version

```



* **Relational Schema Entities:**
1. `facts` table: Atomic claims containing `id`, `claim_text`, `scope` (`CHARACTER`, `LOCATION`, `LOGISTIC`, `LAW`), and a strictly increasing integer `version` (`NEW.version > OLD.version`).


2. `scenes` table: Prose and AST containers holding `id`, `episode_num`, `scene_num`, and certification `status` (`clean`, `dirty`, `reviewed`).


3. `scene_fact_dependencies` table: Junction edges binding `scene_id` to `fact_id`, `entity_type`, and the exact `pinned_version` active during authoring.




* **Reactive Database Triggers (`propagate_fact_mutation`):** When an upstream canonical fact is modified (e.g., a duel in Episode 2 changes a character's limb status from `v1` to `v2`), an atomic SQLite trigger fires instantly:



$$\text{WHERE } \text{scene\_fact\_dependencies.pinned\_version} < \text{facts.version} \implies \text{UPDATE scenes SET status = 'dirty', is\_dirty = 1}$$





* **$O(1)$ Transitive Closure Hashing ($H_{\text{dep\_transitive}}$):** To eliminate expensive recursive graph walks at render time, the compiler precomputes the deep Directed Acyclic Graph across entities ($\text{character} \to \text{grievance} \to \text{location} \to \text{prop}$) and embeds a 64-character SHA-256 fingerprint into storage. Downstream orchestrators verify continuity in $\le 1.1\text{ ms}$ via an $O(1)$ string-equality check, halting invalid pipelines before dispatch.


* **Deterministic Scene Fingerprints:** Every downstream scene artifact binds an immutable composite hash:



$$\text{SceneHash} = \mathcal{H}\left(\text{AST} + \sum_{i=1}^{k} \mathcal{H}(\text{Dependency}_i) + \text{Assets} + \text{CompilerVersion}\right)$$






---

### 5. Separation of Compiler Purity from Statistical NLP Linters

A critical architectural discovery in the system is that statistical NLP libraries (such as spaCy, Stanza, or transformer-based parsers) rely on platform-specific floating-point arithmetic and versioned neural weights.

* **The Architectural Hazard:** If an NLP parser were placed directly inside `compile.ts`, running CI on ARM versus x86—or upgrading a minor library dependency—could alter parse trees and cause builds to fail without a single character of Markdown changing, violating determinism.


* **The Structural Separation:**
1. `scripts/compile.ts` evaluates **only** exact Zod schemas, integer basis points, foreign keys, and graph acyclicity. It is completely pure and bit-identical across all architectures.


2. All fuzzy prose and semantic analysis is relegated to a separate linter stage (`scripts/lint-prose.ts`) that runs **outside the cryptographic sealing loop**. The resulting `bible-state.json` artifact remains completely unpolluted by statistical models.





---

### 6. Continuous Integration & Tamper Firewalls

To guarantee that no untrusted LLM, compromised edge worker, or negligent human developer can bypass downward consistency, CI enforces a battery of automated tripwires:

1. **The Zero-Drift Tripwire (`git diff --exit-code compiled/`):**
Immediately after running compilation in CI, the pipeline asserts zero git status diffs. This provides a dual-vector firewall:


* *Uncompiled Source Edits:* Flags when an author modifies frontmatter in a source Markdown dossier but forgets to run `compile.ts`.


* *Spoofed Passes:* Flags when an untrusted agent directly hand-edits `compiled/bible-state.json` to spoof a green test without satisfying source constraints; re-compilation clobbers the manual edit, triggering a diff that aborts the build.




2. **The Trusted Overlay of Build Machinery:**
In CI runners, the build environment dynamically overlays the immutable compiler scripts (`scripts/compile.ts`), schemas (`schemas/`), test harnesses, and build configurations (`package.json`, `tsconfig.json`) directly from `origin/main` before running verification. An agent pull request attempting to relax Zod scalar bounds or bypass invariant linters is neutralized before execution.


3. **Automated Negative Controls:**
Adhering to the **Universal Negative-Control Law** (a validation gate never observed failing has not been proven to work), CI suites continuously run synthetic mutations—injecting circular grievance edges, out-of-bounds scalars, and dangling foreign keys—to assert that the compiler halts with non-zero exit codes.



Through this exhaustive pipeline—from Byte-0 source constraints and 3-pass algebraic graph verification to reactive SQL invalidation triggers and deep-frozen in-memory execution—the compiler guarantees that every downstream episode operates with **100% mathematical adherence to established lore at $T_0 = \$0.00$**.

Based on the architectural specifications and status ledgers for **`000.goblin-lore`** (also designated across the specifications as `goblin-bible`, `001.lore`, and mapped downstream to `@snailicid3/consumer-npm`), here is the comprehensive breakdown of what has been implemented so far and the exact technical components that still need to be built to achieve **flawless Downward Consistency** ($B \implies A$ at $T_0 = \$0.00$).

---

### I. The Architectural Mandate: What "Flawless Downward Consistency" Requires

In this pipeline, **Downward Consistency** means that every generated scene, actor verb, dialogue line, equipment slot, and spatial witness event across 10 years of episodes mathematically conforms to declared canonical facts.

Because generative models (LLMs, neural TTS, diffusion models) are untrusted rendering workers, Downward Consistency requires that `000.goblin-lore` act as an **offline, compile-time legislative authority**. If an invalid fact, dangling reference, or anatomical contradiction reaches runtime, downstream GPU and token costs balloon. Flawless consistency requires a **hermetic, fail-closed compiler gauntlet**.

---

### II. What Has Been Implemented So Far

The core architectural boundaries and foundational compiler passes are established:

1. **Topological Domain Restructuring:**
* Transitioned from a monolithic/nested wiki structure to a **flat, top-level 5-entity domain structure**: `/characters`, `/grievances`, `/locations`, `/possessions`, and `/world`.


* Established the `_retired/` subdirectory convention (`/characters/_retired/`, etc.) ensuring entities are never deleted with raw `rm` commands, preserving historical state replay ($S_N = \text{fold}(\dots)$).




2. **Byte-0 Frontmatter & Pass 1 Bounds Validation:**
* Frontmatter extraction via `gray-matter` enforces that the opening `---` delimiter begins strictly at **Byte 0** (offset `0x00`), rejecting leading whitespace, UTF-8 BOM, and unapproved headers.


* Strict `.strict()` Zod schemas (`CharacterSchema`, `GrievanceSchema`, `LocationSchema`, `PossessionSchema`) validate scalar limits, enums, and basic structural types.


* Prohibition of raw floats in favor of **integer basis-point scalars** ($0 \le x \le 10{,}000$).




3. **Pass 2 Relational Integrity & Graph Traversal (Core Engine):**
* Built the foundational cross-corpus foreign-key resolution engine in `scripts/compile.ts` (`packages/001.lore/src/compiler.ts`).


* Active checks include:


* Spatial anchors: `character.location` $\to$ indexed node in `/locations`.


* Equipped items: `worn`, `held`, `carried`, `cached` $\to$ `/possessions`.


* Primary grievance actors: `grievance.participants_primary` $\to$ `/characters`.


* Escalation acyclicity: 3-Color Depth-First Search (`WHITE`, `GRAY`, `BLACK`) on `spawns_on_max_escalation` to detect causal recursion loops ($A \to B \to C \to A$).






4. **Deterministic Sealing & Basic Artifact Emission:**
* Compilation outputs `compiled/bible-state.json` containing the normalized world state, stamped with a 64-character SHA-256 Merkle root ($H_{\text{root}}$).




5. **Authoring CLI Scaffolding (`goblin-lore new`):**
* Interactive Node CLI wizard built with `@clack/prompts` to guide authors through domain scaffolding, auto-generating compliant Byte-0 frontmatter and auto-completing known entity IDs to prevent syntax errors at $T_{-1}$.




6. **Baseline Storyworld Seed (`under-the-floorboards`):**
* Initial seed entities authored: Bog, Spleen, The Warm Pipe, and foundational scrap currency rules (`Scrap`, `Shine`, `Weight`).





---

### III. What Still Needs to Be Created to Achieve Flawless Downward Consistency

While the conceptual foundation is solid, there are specific **relational gaps, test firewalls, sealing algorithms, and client protections** that must be constructed to guarantee zero leaks to downstream runtimes.

#### 1. Pass 2 Referential Gaps (Unvalidated Pointers)

Currently, `compiler.ts` has several unchecked relational pointers that allow "ghost" entities to slip through:

* **Epistemic Strings:** `character.epistemic.strings_held_over` and `character.epistemic.leverage_strings` keys are currently unvalidated. The compiler must verify that every string points to a real, active character ID.


* **Secondary Grievance Participants:** `grievance.participants_can_involve` is unvalidated; it must verify that optional participants exist in `/characters`.


* **Spatial Reciprocity & Adjacency:** `location.adjacent_locations` lacks reciprocal verification. If Location $A$ declares adjacency to Location $B$, Location $B$ must explicitly declare an reciprocal portal to Location $A$, with matching acoustic dampening ($cdB$) and optical opacity basis points.



#### 2. Pass 3 Combinatorial Somatic SAT Solver & Clue Disjointness

* **Hand-Saturation Satisfiability Solver:** Linear arithmetic verifying that held equipment does not exceed physical capability:



$$\sum_{s \in \text{held}} s.\text{occupies\_hands} \le 2.0 - \text{hand\_impairment\_penalty}$$



If a character dossier claims 1 functional arm but equips a two-handed weapon, the compiler must issue a fatal hard fail at $T_0$.


* **The Clue-Lemma Disjointness Theorem (`src/health/trieClueDisjointness.ts`):**
In `unresolved.json` / `unresolved.schema.ts`, narrative mysteries (e.g., `the_famine`) declare forbidden trigger lemmas ($T$) and permitted physical clues ($P_{\text{clues}}$). The compiler must evaluate:



$$P_{\text{clues}} \cap \left(\bigcup_{t \in T} t\right) = \emptyset$$



If an author accidentally includes a forbidden resolution word in a clue description, compilation must halt immediately.



#### 3. RFC 8785 Canonical JSON (JCS) & 1-Hop Local Hashing ($H_{\text{dep}}$)

* **True JCS Serialization:** Replace basic JSON serialization with strict RFC 8785 canonicalization (Unicode NFC normalization, UTF-16 code-unit key ordering, preserving array order for causal beats).


* **Atomic Sibling Rename:** Write to `.tmp_[UUIDv7]` on the same physical inode before executing `fs.renameSync` to eliminate torn reads by downstream test harnesses or watchers.


* **1-Hop Dependency Hash ($H_{\text{dep}}$):** Calculate localized entity dependency hashes:

$$H_{\text{dep}} = \text{SHA-256}\Big(\text{Sort}\big([H_{\text{target}}, H_{\text{fk\_1}}, H_{\text{fk\_2}}, \dots]\big)\Big)$$



This allows downstream editors and edge runtimes to detect localized drift without invalidating the entire multi-season corpus when an unrelated entity changes.



#### 4. The Golden-File Failure Suite (`tests/golden-failures/`)

* As surfaced during review, the current tests only verify that valid data compiles; **they do not verify that the compiler fails closed on corrupt data**.


* Must author a suite of deliberately broken markdown fixtures asserting exact non-zero exit codes:


* `dangling_item_pointer.md` (equipping non-existent item) $\to$ `RELATIONAL_INTEGRITY_FAIL`.


* `circular_grievance_dag.md` (Grievance A $\to$ B $\to$ A) $\to$ `CYCLE_DETECTED`.


* `scalar_overflow.md` (intensity $> 10{,}000$) $\to$ `ZOD_SCHEMA_FAIL`.


* `byte0_violation.md` (leading whitespace before `---`) $\to$ `BYTE_0_FAIL`.





#### 5. CI Zero-Drift Tripwire & Git Hygiene Enforcement

* **Automate `tests/ci/deletion.sh`:** CI must run `scripts/compile.ts` followed by `git diff --exit-code compiled/`. If frontmatter was changed without committing the compiled JSON, or if an agent edited `compiled/bible-state.json` directly, the build must abort.


* **Reconcile `.gitignore`:** Line 211 in `.gitignore` was found to ignore `compiled/**/*_*.json`. This must be removed so that immutable, timestamped WORM snapshot history (`bible-state_YYYY-MM-DDTHH-mm-ssZ.json`) is permanently committed to Git alongside the canonical HEAD.



#### 6. Deep-Freeze Defensive Immutability (`src/client.ts` / `bibleLoader.ts`)

* Downstream engines (`worker-sower-engine`, test suites) must not import loose JSON files directly.


* Must build `bibleLoader.ts` exporting typed accessors that wrap ingested JSON in a recursive `Object.freeze<T>()`. If a simulation worker attempts to mutate canonical memory (e.g., calling `char.logistical.held.push(...)`), the runtime must throw an unhandled `TypeError`.



#### 7. Lexical Verb Gate & Evaluation Benchmark (`src/linters/lexicalGate.ts`)

* An offline, fail-closed text linter that evaluates candidate dialogue, stage directions, and beat descriptions against a character's compiled `banned_kinetic_verbs` (e.g., asserting Bog never performs `sprint`, `vault`, or `leap`).


* Integration of `linters/evalSet.json` (a 200-sentence baseline) to measure false positives before gating downstream generation.



#### 8. Complete the Canonical Ensemble & Grievance Graph

The `under-the-floorboards` storyworld cannot currently test full combinatorial tension because it only has 2 active goblins and 1 grievance:

* Author **Wart** (`wart.md`): Guardian class, `locomotion_baseline: bipedal_hunched`, high warmth, leverage strings toward Bog.


* Author **Mum Grissel** (`mum_grissel.md`): Matriarch whose presence silences domestic squabbles.


* Expand the grievance corpus from 1 to **30–40 dual-rail grievance files** across the 6 thematic categories to achieve complete dyadic coverage ($\binom{4}{2} = 6$ fully covered channels) so the downstream simulator never starves.

### IV. Immediate Construction Blueprint (By Reversibility Cost)

To achieve downward consistency without getting stuck in horizontal design loops, tasks should proceed strictly along the **Minimum Viable Guarantee Set**:

Phase 3: Advisory Linting (Pre-Flight Execution Gating)

The mechanic you are describing—where a simulation runs autonomously, trips a brake at a specific tick, pauses execution to scaffold/generate a screenplay, and then resumes forward—is known across the system architecture as the **"Wind-Up Clockwork Machine"** and represents the core operational loop of **Cluster `02.simulation` (`worker-sower-engine`)**.

This feature is not a detached sidecar; it is the **operational bridge between the compile-time legislative authority (`goblin-lore`) and downstream media compilation (`goblin-viewport` / Remotion)**.

---

### 1. The Operational Loop: How the Simulation Pauses and Generates a Script

The simulation loop connects directly to the system layers through a seven-step sequence:

```text
 1. THE AUTONOMOUS RUN (Clockwork Ticking)
    • Scheduler advances ticks: TICK_ADVANCED(n) via append-only ledger
    • Dormant zones / regional travel evaluate analytically (0 CPU)
    • Paranoia clocks, physical hunger, and social grievance tension accumulate
                         │
                         ▼
 2. THE DRAMATIC HALT (The Boil-Over Trigger)
    • Proximity sweep (d_axial <= 1) detects collocated characters in a cell
    • Tension accumulator crosses threshold OR human showrunner hits PAUSE
    • THE CLOCK BRAKE TRIPS: World ticking halts; simulation freezes at Tick t
                         │
                         ▼
 3. MECHANICAL PRE-RESOLUTION (Stage 4 Opposed 2d6 Duel)
    • System resolves physical conflict BEFORE writing:
      Outcome = 2d6 Opposed + Social Strings - Somatic Strain
    • Evaluates who wins, who yields, what physical trauma or item loss occurs
    • Compiles intermediate contract: ScenePlan IR
                         │
                         ▼
 4. THE AUTHORING PAUSE (Fountain Screenplay Ingress)
    • Mounts /drafts/ or Writer's Desk (writer.astro)
    • Injects Spatial Stage Header (coordinates, locked portals, somatic masks)
    • Human writer or local LLM (T = 0.75) drafts dialogue to fit the mandated outcome
                         │
                         ▼
 5. TIME MEASUREMENT & QUANTIZATION (The Acoustic Clock)
    • Text-to-Speech (TTS) synthesizes audio stems for spoken lines
    • ffprobe measures waveform duration down to the millisecond
    • Master clock quantizes duration into video frames (24 fps) and simulation ticks:
      Elapsed Ticks = ceil(ms / Tick_Interval)
                         │
                         ▼
 6. ATOMIC STATE SETTLEMENT (S_t ──► S_t+1)
    • Discrete state deltas (injuries, spent items, debt shifts) committed to SQLite/D1
    • Database trigger flags stale dependent scenes (is_dirty = 1)
    • Cryptographic state seal updated: H_{t+1} = SHA-256(H_t || EventData)
                         │
                         ▼
 7. RELEASE THE BRAKE (Resume Ticking)
    • Simulation clock advances by elapsed scene interval
    • Sleeping sites apply algebraic catch-up
    • World returns to clockwork autonomous ticking until next dramatic halt

```



---

### 2. The Core Philosophical Axiom: Simulation Precedes Prose

In a naive generative system, an author (or LLM) pauses the simulation and invents what happens in the script, and the database tries to reverse-engineer what changed. In this architecture, that is strictly prohibited:

> **The Constitutional Axiom:** *Simulation determines what happens; the language model determines how it is said; the renderer determines how it is seen and heard.*
> 
> 

1. **Reality is Decided Before Writing Begins:** When the simulation pauses at Tick $t$, the characters do not enter an unconstrained improvisational space. The engine executes **Stage 4 (The Opposed $2\text{d}6$ Duel)** using character attributes, directional social leverage ("Strings"), and somatic debuffs.


2. **The Scene Plan Contract:** If the dice roll dictates a `COSTLY_WIN` for Bog over Wart, that mechanical outcome is stamped into an immutable contract (`ScenePlan IR`).


3. **The Role of the Screenplay:** The `.fountain` screenplay exists solely to **dramatize the pre-resolved physical and social outcome**. The prose cannot unilaterally alter who won, teleport an actor, or ignore a severed limb.



---

### 3. How the Pause Connects to the Legislative Canon (`goblin-lore`)

When the simulation brake trips at Tick $t$, **`goblin-lore` acts as the pre-flight legislative boundary**:

* **The Spatial Stage Header:** The engine pulls the deep-frozen topological facts from `bible-state.json` and injects an immutable metadata header directly into the top of the `.fountain` file:


```fountain
/* === SPATIAL STAGE HEADER (TICK 42) ===
   LOCATION: loc_the_warm_pipe
   COORDINATES: Bog (10, 2, -12) | Wart (11, 2, -13)
   DYNAMIC PORTALS: hatch_valve_04 [LOCKED]
   SOMATIC VECTORS: Wart arms_available = 1.0 (BANNED: ["bimanual_lift", "vault"])
   PRE-RESOLVED OUTCOME: Bog forces Wart to drop claim to scraper; Wart takes +300 bp strain
   ======================================= */

```





* **Compile-Time Invariant Gauntlet ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** As the human showrunner types in `writer.astro` (or as a local LLM streams candidate lines), a real-time linter evaluates the text against the active tick's state vectors:


* **Somatic SAT Breach:** If Wart grabs the wrench with *"both hands"*, the linter immediately highlights the line in red (`availableArms = 1.0; action violates physical limits`).


* **Epistemic Firewall (Ignorance by Omission):** If Bog mentions a secret that has not entered his `BeliefRecord`, the compiler halts the draft at $T_0$.


* Because invalid actions are rejected in local memory before reaching speech synthesis or video diffusion, continuity failures cost **exactly $\$0.00$ in GPU spend**.





---

### 4. How the Screenplay Calculates Consumed Ticks: Audio as the Clock of Time

A central question in simulation design is: *When you pause to write a scene, how much world time actually passed?*

In this system, narrative engines and generative models are forbidden from guessing how long a scene takes. Instead, **time is derived empirically from synthesized sound**:

1. **Extraction of the Machine Contract:** The written `.fountain` text is compiled into `dialogue_ast.json`, stripping away subjective prose to leave an exact sequence of spoken lines and physical reactions (Dwight Swain Motivation-Reaction Units).


2. **Acoustic Synthesis:** A local TTS service generates $48\text{ kHz}$ uncompressed `.wav` stems for every spoken line.


3. **Microsecond Measurement:** The engine spawns `ffprobe` to measure the exact waveform duration ($D_{\text{ms}}$):



$$\text{target\_frames} = \left\lceil \frac{D_{\text{ms}}}{1000} \times 24 \right\rceil$$





4. **Quantization to Simulation Ticks:** The total duration of all dialogue stems plus mandatory physical beats (e.g., $114\text{ seconds} = 2{,}736\text{ video frames}$) is converted into simulation ticks.


5. **Advancing the World:** When the pause is released, the master world clock jumps forward by that exact measured interval. Unobserved sectors sleeping in "The Void" apply their algebraic catch-up formulas ($f(\Delta_{\text{Ticks}})$) to simulate background resource decay or travel during the time the scene took place.



---

### 5. The Operational Firewalls: Protecting Causality Across Decades

Connecting a real-time clockwork simulation to human/AI screenwriting introduces two catastrophic failure modes, both neutralized by architectural firewalls:

#### A. The Forbidden Patch Loop vs. The Architectural Ratification Loop

* **The Hazard:** A showrunner or director dislikes a generated dialogue line and manually rewrites it in the screenplay editor. Because the database has no idea *why* the character said those new words, the event ledger, causal graph, and character psychology desynchronize from the media representation (Causality Drift).


* **The Solution (`SceneReviewGate.tsx`):** The interface strictly forbids direct free-text line editing. If a scene is flawed, the director selects an error code from a closed failure taxonomy (e.g., `UNEARNED_RELEASE`, `SOMATIC_CONTRADICTION`) or adjusts dynamic tension sliders (Curtness, Threat, Subtext Suppression). The engine updates the underlying state/prompt envelope and **regenerates the expression**, keeping the ledger, event log, and script 100% unified.



#### B. SIMULATION_ONLY Mode: Decoupling Truth from Hardware

Because narrative causality lives in the relational ledger rather than inside GPU memory, the simulation loop can run independently of visual rendering:

* If render nodes (Rig 2 GPUs) crash or are shut down, the engine switches to **`SIMULATION_ONLY` mode**.


* The clockwork machine continues running its ticks, pausing, resolving mechanical duels, drafting `.fountain` scripts, and committing somatic trauma directly to SQLite/Cloudflare D1 for fractions of a cent.


* The unrendered scene ASTs and frame manifests are queued in an append-only backlog; when GPUs come back online, they pull from the backlog and render missing video plates without the narrative engine having lost a single tick.



---

### Summary: The Complete Cycle

The simulation pause is **the moment where mathematical world-state materializes into human-readable drama**:

1. **The world runs autonomously** along the timeline until conflict reaches a tipping point.


2. **The machine trips its brake** and uses tabletop rules to determine physical reality and stakes.


3. **The screenplay is authored** as a constrained reflection of that state, policed by `goblin-lore`'s compile-time invariants at $T_0 = \$0.00$.


4. **Synthesized audio clocks the duration**, advancing the master tick count.


5. **The resulting state deltas are sealed into the append-only ledger**, the brake is released, and the clockwork universe ticks forward into the next epoch.


The feature colloquially referred to as the **"Wind-Up Clockwork Machine"** is the central operational loop connecting **Cluster `02.simulation` (`worker-sower-engine`)** with downstream media compilation (`goblin-viewport` / Remotion).

It operationalizes the studio’s core constitutional law:

$$\mathbf{\text{Constitutional Axiom:}}\quad \text{Simulation } \xrightarrow{\text{determines}} \text{Reality} \xrightarrow{\text{dramatized by}} \text{Prose (LLM)} \xrightarrow{\text{clocked by}} \text{Audio (TTS)} \xrightarrow{\text{projected by}} \text{Renderer (GPU)}$$



Below is the exhaustive, component-level breakdown of the seven phases comprising the Operational Loop, tracing how the clock ticks, why and how the brake trips, how screenplays are derived from tabletop mechanics, and how physical sound measures narrative time.
