### Phase 1: The Autonomous Run (Clockwork Ticking)

The simulation runs as an event-sourced, discrete-event state machine. It rejects real-time wall clocks (`Date.now()`, `performance.now()`) in favor of a strictly ordered, integer tick sequence.

#### 1. The Monotonic Tick Authority

* Time is treated as an external input, never an internal property read by the simulation.


* A central scheduler emits discrete `TICK_ADVANCED(n)` events, where time is quantized to integer **centiseconds** ($10\text{ ms}$ resolution, $100\text{ ticks} = 1.0\text{ second}$).


* Concurrency is resolved via total ordering: every actor has an integer `busyUntil: Instant` field; simultaneous actions within the same tick resolve by explicit priority followed by unsigned UTF-16 code-unit entity ID.



#### 2. Multi-Scale Spatial Partitioning

To run thousands of background characters without burning server CPU, entities stratify across three operational execution tiers:

* **Tier 1: Staged (Tactical Hot-Set):** Active characters occupying discrete axial hex coordinates `(q, r)` within an active room. Fully evaluated by `goblin-spatial` for field-of-view, acoustic propagation, and collision.


* **Tier 2: In-Transit (Warm Analytical Edge):** Characters traveling along regional corridors. They are excluded from tactical queries; their position is an analytical closed-form function of elapsed time:



$$p = \operatorname{clamp}\left(\frac{t_{\text{current}} - t_{\text{depart}}}{D_{\text{edge}}}, \, 0, \, 1\right)$$



If interrupted mid-edge (e.g., an ambush), the edge splits into two integer sub-edges joined at an ephemeral virtual node `vn:{edgeId}:{elapsedCentiseconds}` that lives only in dynamic overlay memory, leaving the sealed topology hash intact.


* **Tier 3: Cold / Dormant ("The Void"):** Unobserved sites containing zero active players or focal characters process zero ticks while empty ($0\text{ CPU}$).



#### 3. Passive State Accumulators

While ticking, entities update three internal meters on an integer basis-point ledger ($0 \text{ to } 10{,}000\text{ bp}$):

* **Metabolic Strain & Fatigue:** Accumulates based on locomotion classes (`LIMPING`, `CRAWLING`).


* **Paranoia Clocks:** Increments when acoustic transients exceed ambient room damping ($cdB$).


* **Social Debt Strings:** Interpersonal resentment registers silently increment across active dyads as grievances remain unresolved.



---

### Phase 2: The Dramatic Halt (Tripping the Clock Brake)

The simulation does not pause randomly; the clock brake trips automatically when mathematical tension exceeds systemic bounds, or deliberately when a human director intervenes.

```typescript
// Conceptual Ingress: The Brake Evaluation within worker-sower-engine
function evaluateBrakeTripPredicate(
  room: ActiveRoomState, 
  dyad: CharacterDyad, 
  grievance: GrievanceRecord
): boolean {
  // 1. Axial Proximity Invariant
  const distance = goblinSpatial.getAxialDistance(dyad.actorA.coord, dyad.actorB.coord);
  const isCollocated = distance <= 1; // Direct melee / conversational reach

  // 2. Resentment Ceiling Breach (The Boil-Over Trigger)
  const isBoilingOver = grievance.intensity_bps >= grievance.escalation_ceiling_bps;

  // 3. Channel Saturation & Cooldown Audit
  const isEligible = grievance.cooldown_ticks === 0 && dyad.boredom_score <= 6500;

  return isCollocated && isBoilingOver && isEligible;
}

```



#### 1. The Autonomous Boil-Over Trigger

During each tick, `worker-sower-engine` runs an **Ensemble Eligibility Sweep**:

* **Spatial Proximity:** Evaluates if two opposing actors occupy adjacent axial hexes ($d_{\text{axial}} \le 1$).


* **Grievance Threshold:** The engine inspects the dyad’s active `GrievanceRecord`. If the accumulated intensity reaches or breaches the escalation ceiling ($I_{\text{curr}} \ge I_{\max}$), an unavoidable dramatic threshold is crossed.


* **Procedural Boredom Filter:** The engine checks rolling Jaccard similarity across recent scene pairings ($J_{\text{boredom}} \le 0.65$). If the dyad has not appeared recently, the boil-over is cleared for dramatic execution.



#### 2. The Director Manual Interrupt ("Ghost Mode")

A showrunner observing the live simulation canvas (`HexMapView.tsx`) can click **PAUSE** or trigger a scene directly from `DirectorConsole.tsx`.

* The showrunner enters as a `ShowrunnerGhostEntity`:
```typescript
const ShowrunnerGhostEntity = {
  physics_body: null,    // 0 mass, 0 colliders; bypasses room goblin overfill limits
  visibility: "GM_ONLY", // Epistemically cloaked from character perception checks
  permissions: "ROOT",   // Live read access to in-memory state streams
};

```





#### 3. Engaging the Clock Brake

When tripped, the engine:

1. Emits a `SIMULATION_PAUSED(tick_t)` event to the internal bus.


2. Suspends the `TICK_ADVANCED` dispatch loop.


3. Clones and deep-freezes the current world snapshot $S_t$ in memory.


4. Acquires a transactional execution lease in Cloudflare D1/SQLite (`status = 'ENGAGED_IN_SCENE'`).



---

### Phase 3: Mechanical Pre-Resolution (The Opposed $2\text{d}6$ Duel)

Crucially, **prose generation does not invent the outcome of the confrontation**. Before an author opens a text editor or an LLM streams a single token, the mechanical reality of the conflict is rolled and settled in memory.

#### 1. The Tabletop Conflict Math (Stage 4)

The engine executes an opposed tabletop roll between the actors (e.g., Bog vs. Wart):

$$\text{Roll}_A = 2\text{d}6 + \text{Hegemony}_A + \text{StringsHeld}_{A \to B} - \text{SomaticStrain}_A$$



$$\text{Roll}_B = 2\text{d}6 + \text{Hegemony}_B + \text{StringsHeld}_{B \to A} - \text{SomaticStrain}_B$$



$$\Delta_{\text{margin}} = \text{Roll}_A - \text{Roll}_B$$



The resulting margin determines a discrete outcome tier from a closed taxonomy:

| Outcome Tier | Mathematical Condition | Narrative & Physical Mandate |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`DECISIVE_WIN`** | $\Delta_{\text{margin}} \ge +4$ | Instigator achieves objective with zero concession; defender takes heavy somatic strain or drops item.

 |
| **`COSTLY_WIN`** | $+1 \le \Delta_{\text{margin}} \le +3$ | Instigator achieves objective but pays a tangible price (+450 bp fatigue, damaged tool, or creates new grievance).

 |
| **`STANDOFF`** | $\Delta_{\text{margin}} = 0$ | Deadlock; physical position remains frozen, both actors accumulate +200 bp paranoia, conflict escalates.

 |
| **`HUMILIATING_LOSS`** | $\Delta_{\text{margin}} \le -1$ | Instigator is repelled, loses social leverage (`Strings`), and concedes physical territory.

 |

#### 2. Intermediate Representation: `ScenePlan IR`

The engine stamps this mechanical resolution into an immutable intermediate contract (`ScenePlan IR`):

```json
{
  "scene_id": "EP101_SC04_TICK_42",
  "tick": 42,
  "location_id": "loc_the_warm_pipe",
  "participants": ["char_bog", "char_wart"],
  "conflict_id": "grv_territorial_scrap_claim",
  "mechanical_resolution": {
    "winner": "char_bog",
    "loser": "char_wart",
    "tier": "COSTLY_WIN",
    "mandated_state_deltas": {
      "char_bog": { "fatigue_delta_bps": 450, "inventory_acquire": "item_iron_scraper" },
      "char_wart": { "inventory_drop": "item_iron_scraper", "grievance_cooldown": 3 },
      "room": { "occluder_mutate": { "portal_id": "hatch_valve_04", "state": "LOCKED" } }
    }
  },
  "required_exit_condition": "char_wart physically surrenders possession of the scraper and retreats past the seam"
}

```



---

### Phase 4: The Authoring Pause & Screenplay Ingress

Once the `ScenePlan IR` is compiled, the engine mounts the screenplay workspace. The creative text must conform to the mechanical outcome, never contradict it.

```
                     AUTHORING INGRESS PATHWAYS
                                 │
        ┌────────────────────────┴────────────────────────┐
        ▼                                                 ▼
[ PATHWAY A: HUMAN SHOWRUNNER ]               [ PATHWAY B: UNTRUSTED LLM ]
• Writer's Desk (writer.astro) or             • Qwen 2.5 14B on Rig 1 (CPU/RAM)
  Blessed TUI $EDITOR Suspension              • GBNF Context-Free Grammar
• Full creative nuance at $0.00 compute       • Tripartite Attention Sandwich
        │                                                 │
        └────────────────────────┬────────────────────────┘
                                 │
                                 ▼
         [ REAL-TIME COMPILE-TIME INVARIANT LINTER ]
         • Tier 2 Somatic SAT (Arms, Banned Verbs)
         • Tier 3 Epistemic Horizon (BeliefRecord)
                                 │ Passes Gauntlet ($0.00)
                                 ▼
                     dialogue_ast.json (Dwight Swain MRUs)

```



#### 1. The Spatial Stage Header

Before text editing begins, the engine injects an immutable, machine-parsed comment block at line 0 of the `.fountain` file:

```fountain
/* === SPATIAL STAGE HEADER (TICK 42) ===
   LOCATION: loc_the_warm_pipe
   COORDINATES: Bog (10, 2, -12) Facing: 1 | Wart (11, 2, -13) Facing: 4
   DYNAMIC PORTALS: hatch_valve_04 [LOCKED]
   CAPACITY: 2/2 Goblins (Saturated)
   LIGHTING: 0.15 lux | ACOUSTIC FLOOR: 1,500 cdB
   ACTIVE CONFLICT: grv_territorial_scrap_claim
   PRE-RESOLVED MECHANICAL OUTCOME: COSTLY_WIN (Bog over Wart)
   MANDATED EXIT: Wart drops claim to scraper; Bog accumulates +450 bp strain
   SOMATIC RESTRICTIONS: Wart arms_available = 1.0 (BANNED: ["bimanual_lift", "vault"])
   ===================================== */

INT. THE WARM PIPE - TICK 42

Bog sits across the junction box, picking at grease.

```



#### 2. Ingress Pathway A: The Human Showrunner & `$EDITOR` Suspension

If authored by a human in the Blessed TUI (`98.menu.unit`):

1. The TUI detects the drafting intent, writes the initial `.fountain` scaffold to disk, and executes `screen.leave()` to restore canonical cooked terminal mode.


2. It spawns the showrunner's local text editor via `child_process.spawnSync(process.env.EDITOR || 'code --wait', [filePath])`.


3. The server sits in zero-cost memory idle ($T_0 = \$0.00$) while the writer drafts dialogue and character beats.


4. Upon editor close (`exit code 0`), the process executes `screen.enter()`, repaints the Blessed TUI, and triggers the compile-time invariant linter.



#### 3. Ingress Pathway B: Untrusted LLM Semantic Expansion

If drafted by a local model (e.g., Qwen 2.5 14B running on Rig 1 CPU/RAM):

* **GBNF Context-Free Grammar:** The model's sampling loop is constrained by formal GBNF grammar files, forbidding chatter, markdown code blocks, or conversational preambles.


* **The Tripartite Attention Sandwich:**
* *Top 15% (Primacy Anchor):* Dialect rules, character voice exemplars, and negative lexical bans (no filter verbs, no modern therapy buzzwords).


* *Middle 70% (Passive Cadence Buffer):* Sliding window of preceding scene dialogue.


* *Bottom 15% (Recency Dominance):* The active **Spatial Stage Header**, forcing the model's immediate token probabilities to obey the pre-resolved `COSTLY_WIN` and somatic limits.





#### 4. The Live Invariant Linter ($T_0 = \$0.00$)

Whether authored by human or machine, candidate lines are parsed into an Abstract Syntax Tree and checked in memory before any external process is called:

* **Somatic SAT Violation:** If Wart *"grabs the iron wrench with both hands"*, the parser detects that Wart's `arms_available = 1.0` and immediately halts compilation:
```text
>> [SOMATIC BREACH]: Actor char_wart has availableArms = 1.0.
>> Banned Verb: "both hands". Action violates physical limits.

```





* **Epistemic Horizon Breach (Ignorance by Omission):** If Bog says *"Did you hide the siphon down in the culvert?"*, the linter checks Bog's compiled `BeliefRecord`. If `fact_culvert_siphon` is missing from Bog's knowledge set, the line is rejected with an `EPISTEMIC_LEAK` error.



#### 5. Compilation into Dwight Swain MRUs (`dialogue_ast.json`)

The validated text is compiled into a sequence of **Dwight Swain Motivation-Reaction Units (MRUs)**:

```json
{
  "beats": [
    {
      "beat_id": "beat_001",
      "type": "MOTIVATION_REACTION",
      "actor_id": "char_bog",
      "kinetic_action": { "verb": "STEP_FORWARD", "target_axial": [10, 2] },
      "dialogue": {
        "text": "Step back off the seam, Wart. It's marked.",
        "emotion_tag": "COLD_AGGRESSION",
        "curtness_words": 8
      }
    },
    {
      "beat_id": "beat_002",
      "type": "REACTION_EXIT",
      "actor_id": "char_wart",
      "kinetic_action": { "verb": "DROP_POSSESSION", "item_id": "item_iron_scraper" },
      "dialogue": {
        "text": "Take the rust, then. It won't hold edge.",
        "emotion_tag": "SULLEN_RESIGNATION",
        "curtness_words": 8
      }
    }
  ]
}

```



---

### Phase 5: Time Measurement & Acoustic Quantization (The Acoustic Clock)

In this architecture, **neither humans nor generative models are permitted to guess scene duration**. Narrative time is derived empirically from the physical sound of spoken dialogue and foley transients.

```
               THE ACOUSTIC TIMING QUANTIZATION PIPELINE
                                   │
                                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. LOCAL TTS SYNTHESIS ENGINE (Rig 1, Port 8000)                        │
│    • Routes dialogue strings through character acoustic profiles        │
│    • Injects vocal fry, gravel, and pause frequency modifiers           │
│    • Writes 48 kHz uncompressed master .wav stems to disk               │
└──────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │
                                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 2. MICROSECOND WAVEFORM PROBING (ffprobe)                               │
│    • Probes each stem: ffprobe -show_entries format=duration            │
│    • Extracts continuous millisecond duration: D_ms                     │
└──────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │
                                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 3. DISCRETE VIDEO FRAME QUANTIZATION (24 fps Master Clock)              │
│    • target_frames = ceil( (D_ms / 1000) * 24 )                         │
│    • Emits exposure_sheet.json locking every mouth viseme and camera cut│
└──────────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────┘
                                   │
                                   ▼
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 4. SIMULATION TICK CONVERSION                                           │
│    • Total scene centiseconds = sum(D_ms) / 10                          │
│    • Elapsed Ticks: Delta_ticks = ceil( Total Scene Centiseconds )       │
│    • (e.g., 114 seconds = 2,736 video frames = 11,400 simulation ticks) │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Acoustic Synthesis

The conductor on Rig 1 invokes the local TTS daemon (e.g., Parler-TTS / Chatterbox on port 8000), feeding character voice models (e.g., Bog's gravel and vocal fry). It renders uncompressed $48\text{ kHz}$ `.wav` stems for every spoken line. Silent physical beats (e.g., dropping the scraper) draw duration from fixed acoustic transients registered in `foley/registry.json`.

#### 2. Probing with `ffprobe`

The conductor spawns `ffprobe` to measure the audio waveforms to the millisecond:

```bash
ffprobe -v error -show_entries format=duration -of default=noprint_wrappers=1:nokey=1 stem_beat_001.wav

```



#### 3. Discrete Frame and Tick Derivation

Continuous acoustic milliseconds ($D_{\text{ms}}$) are quantized to discrete integer domains:

* **Video Frame Ceiling (24 FPS):**

$$\text{target\_frames} = \left\lceil \frac{D_{\text{ms}}}{1000} \times 24 \right\rceil$$





* **Simulation Tick Interval (10 ms Centiseconds):**

$$\Delta_{\text{ticks}} = \left\lceil \frac{\sum D_{\text{ms}}}{10} \right\rceil$$






If a scene’s audio stems total $114.0\text{ seconds}$, the engine locks the scene to **$2{,}736\text{ video frames}$** and calculates that **$11{,}400\text{ simulation ticks}$** have elapsed in the world.

---

### Phase 6: Atomic State Settlement ($S_t \to S_{t+1}$)

With the dialogue locked and duration measured, the physical consequences pre-resolved in Stage 4 are committed to permanent storage.

#### 1. Hard Transactional Boundary

To prevent state desynchronization, the state transition executes inside an atomic SQL transaction against SQLite / Cloudflare D1 (`CANON_DB`):

```sql
BEGIN TRANSACTION;

-- 1. Apply physical trauma and fatigue deltas to participants
UPDATE characters 
SET somatic_strain_bps = somatic_strain_bps + 450,
    busy_until_tick = 42 + 11400
WHERE id = 'char_bog';

-- 2. Execute logistical item transfers
UPDATE possessions 
SET holder_id = 'char_bog', 
    equip_slot = 'HELD_PRIMARY'
WHERE id = 'item_iron_scraper';

-- 3. Mutate environmental topology
UPDATE dynamic_portals 
SET state = 'LOCKED'
WHERE id = 'hatch_valve_04';

-- 4. Advance grievance escalation and set cooldown
UPDATE grievances 
SET intensity_bps = 0,
    cooldown_ticks = 3000,
    version = version + 1
WHERE id = 'grv_territorial_scrap_claim';

COMMIT;

```



#### 2. Downstream Invalidation Sweeps ($O(1)$ Reactive Blast-Radius)

The update to `grievances.version` fires the automated database trigger `propagate_fact_mutation`:

```sql
CREATE TRIGGER propagate_fact_mutation
AFTER UPDATE OF version ON facts
BEGIN
  UPDATE scenes 
  SET status = 'dirty', is_dirty = 1
  WHERE id IN (
    SELECT scene_id FROM scene_fact_dependencies
    WHERE fact_id = NEW.id AND pinned_version < NEW.version
  );
END;

```



Any future scene across the season that was authored against the old version of this fact is marked `is_dirty = 1`, revoking its certification before it can be rendered to video.

#### 3. Cryptographic State Hash Seal

The engine updates the Merkle state seal of the narrative universe:

$$H_{t+1} = \text{SHA-256}\Big(H_t \parallel \text{EventDigest}(\Delta_{\text{state}}) \parallel \Delta_{\text{ticks}}\Big)$$



---

### Phase 7: Releasing the Clock Brake & World Catch-Up

The pause is lifted, and the clockwork universe returns to autonomous ticking.

```
                     THE BRAKE RELEASE SEQUENCE
                                 │
                                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. ADVANCE THE MASTER CLOCK                                            │
│    • Current world tick advances by measured duration:                 │
│      t_current <- 42 + 11,400 = 11,442 ticks                          │
└────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
                                 │
                                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 2. JIT INTEGRATION FOR SLEEPING SITES (Catch-Up on Wake)               │
│    • Dormancy must never mean "the world was frozen"                   │
│    • When an unobserved zone wakes, calculate elapsed Delta_t          │
│    • State_now = State_initial + (Rate * Delta_ticks)                  │
│    • Resource decay, fuel consumption, and travel applied in <= 5ms   │
└────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────┘
                                 │
                                 ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 3. RESUME AUTONOMOUS CLOCKWORK TICKING                                 │
│    • Ticks continue advancing: TICK_ADVANCED(11443)                    │
│    • Entities resume wandering, trading, and accumulating tension      │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Advancing the Global Timeline

The master tick counter jumps forward by the exact number of ticks consumed by the audio performance:


$$t_{\text{new}} = t_{\text{pause}} + \Delta_{\text{ticks}} = 42 + 11{,}400 = 11{,}442\text{ ticks}$$



#### 2. The Lazy-Evaluation Catch-Up Protocol ("Schrödinger's Zone")

While the active scene was pausing and playing out over 11,400 ticks, unobserved rooms in "The Void" did not simulate individual cycles. When an entity later re-enters a dormant room, the engine applies **closed-form algebraic catch-up on wake**:

$$\text{State}_{\text{wake}} = \text{State}_{\text{sleep}} + \left(\text{DecayRate} \times \Delta_{\text{ticks}}\right)$$



* A cooling pipe's temperature is recalculated in a single $5\text{ ms}$ scalar operation: $T_{\text{pipe}} = \max(T_{\text{ambient}}, \, T_{\text{start}} - k \cdot \Delta_{\text{ticks}})$.


* Background travelers advance along their spline trajectories to their exact new milestones.


* **Dormancy defers simulation; it never cancels consequences.**



---

### Architectural Firewalls & Failure Topologies

Connecting a real-time state machine to human/LLM screenwriting introduces distinct failure vectors, neutralized by three explicit system firewalls:

#### 1. The Forbidden Patch Loop vs. `SceneReviewGate.tsx`

* **The Hazard:** If a showrunner dislikes a line of dialogue and directly edits the script text, the screenplay desynchronizes from the underlying event ledger (Causality Drift).


* **The Solution:** The review interface (`SceneReviewGate.tsx`) forbids freehand text editing. Instead, the director selects from a closed taxonomy of failure codes (e.g., `UNEARNED_RELEASE`, `SOMATIC_BREACH`) or adjusts dramatic dials (Curtness, Spite, Threat). The engine patches the intermediate AST parameters and **re-compiles the expression**, keeping ledger and script bit-identical.



#### 2. `SIMULATION_ONLY` Hardware Fallback Mode

* **The Hazard:** Downstream GPU rendering nodes (Rig 2 hosting ComfyUI on port 8188) crash, lose power, or overheat.


* **The Solution:** The pipeline decouples narrative simulation from pixel rendering:


```typescript
if (gpuFoundrySockets.length === 0 && payload.allowSimulationFallback) {
  // Commit narrative state transition directly into D1 without waiting for pixels
  const result = await executeNarrativeStateMutation(c.env.CANON_DB, payload.sceneAst);
  return c.json({
    status: "COMMITTED_WITHOUT_MEDIA",
    sceneId: payload.sceneId,
    stateDelta: result.delta,
    message: "Narrative state advanced in D1. Rendering queued for batch catch-up."
  });
}

```




Narrative history advances at $\approx \$0.001$ per event; unrendered ASTs queue in an append-only backlog until GPUs reconnect and drain the queue.

#### 3. Disaster Quarantine Rollback

If an experimental scene produces unresolvable narrative deadlock or schema corruption, the recovery sequence does not guess how to unwind history:

1. Delete active `bible-state.json`.


2. Restore the previous cycle backup snapshot:
```bash
cp worker-sower-engine/backups/bible-state_cycle_41.json goblin-bible/compiled/bible-state.json

```




3. Re-run `npm run compile` to re-assert the Merkle root and re-seed the PRNG lottery under clean preconditions.


### I. The Constitutional Axiom & Foundational Mandate

Across the entire studio architecture, the relation between physical reality and literary text is governed by an absolute, non-negotiable law:

$$\mathbf{\text{Constitutional Axiom:}}\quad \text{Probabilistic systems propose and execute; deterministic systems decide what is allowed to be true.}[cite: 3, 7, 23]$$

This principle is colloquially codified as **"Math Precedes Poetry"** and formalizes the operational division of labor between mechanical state simulation and natural language generation:

> **"Simulation determines what happens; the language model determines how it is said; the renderer determines how it is seen and heard."**
> 
> 

In conventional generative AI systems, a large language model (LLM) is prompted to write a scene, and the system attempts to audit, parse, or reverse-engineer the world state from the resulting text. In this architecture, that inversion of authority is treated as an architectural defect.

Generative models (LLMs, neural voice synthesizers, diffusion engines) are treated as **untrusted, headless rendering workers** with **zero authority** over world state, physical possibility, spatial positioning, or master timing. Reality—who is present, what they fight about, who wins, what trauma is inflicted, and what items change hands—is mathematically settled in an immutable relational ledger *before* a writer touches a keyboard or a language model generates a single token.

---

### II. The Cartesian Firewall: The Four Ontological Tiers and Durability Classes

To ensure that prose can never corrupt or dictate physical truth, the architecture enforces the **Cartesian Firewall**, separating operations across two orthogonal axes:

* **Axis A (Semantic Transformation / Meaning):** The deterministic progression from static canonical axioms (`01.canon` / `goblin-lore`) to tabletop RPG duel arbitration and screenplay Intermediate Representations (`02.simulation`). Axis A deals exclusively with relational truth and state mutations, remaining entirely agnostic to display frames, pixels, or waveforms.


* **Axis B (Artifact Hardening / Representation):** The downstream sensory projection of verified state, progressing from text-to-speech synthesis to GPU tensor diffusion (`03.foundry`) and optical Remotion compositing (`05.delivery`).



```text
               AXIS A: SEMANTIC TRANSFORMATION (Meaning)
┌─────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐
│ WORLD STATE │ ──► │ NARRATIVE IR │ ──► │ TEMPORAL IR  │ ──► │  VISUAL IR   │
└─────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘
       │                   │                    │                    │
       ▼                   ▼                    ▼                    ▼
┌─────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐
│  REDUCER &  │ ──► │  SOURCE CODE │ ──► │ HEADLESS RUN │ ──► │   VERIFIED   │
│  EVENT LOG  │     │   (TSX)      │     │  (Chromium)  │     │   ARTIFACT   │
└─────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘
               AXIS B: ARTIFACT HARDENING (Representation)

```



#### 1. The Four Ontological Tiers

Data is strictly segregated into four non-interchangeable tiers to prevent simulation from entangling with rendering:

* **Tier 1: Canon (Immutable Law):** Hand-authored Markdown dossiers within `000.goblin-lore`. Defines character somatic limits, inventory topologies, relationship rules, and baseline physical axioms. It mutates solely via human Git commits.


* **Tier 2: Events (The Authoritative Historical Log):** The immutable, append-only chronological log recording *why* reality changed (e.g., injuries inflicted, items traded, secrets spoken).


* **Tier 3: State (The Materialized Snapshot):** The transient representation of reality at Tick $t$, derived by folding the event log over base canon via a pure mathematical reducer:



$$\text{State}_N = \text{fold}(\text{apply}_v, \, \text{State}_0, \, [E_1, \dots, E_N], \, \sigma)$$



State is merely a disposable, fast working cache of events.


* **Tier 4: Media Projections (Downstream Presentation):** `.fountain` screenplays, exposure sheets, synthesized audio stems, and final MP4 files.



#### 2. The Law of Media Asymmetry

**Media is never state.** Modifying a media projection (such as re-recording an audio stem, adjusting a camera angle in Remotion, or polishing dialogue phrasing) mutates an editorial presentation; it **never** alters historical world state or the event log. Information flows in exactly one direction: $\text{World} \longrightarrow \text{Script} \longrightarrow \text{Media}$.

#### 3. The Two-Part Deletion Principle

* **Model-Independent World Truth (Class 1 & 2 Durability):** If every large language model weight, diffusion checkpoint, and neural vocoder is wiped from existence, the story universe survives completely intact inside the Git Markdown dossiers and relational event log.


* **Archive-Dependent Rendered Artifacts (Class 3 Durability):** Because diffusion samplers and GPU CUDA drivers exhibit non-deterministic floating-point variances, rendered media cannot be regenerated bit-for-bit from seeds alone. Rendered media are treated as *archival evidence*, requiring permanent preservation in content-addressed storage (CAS).



---

### III. The Failure Modes of Inverted Authority (Why Prose Cannot Precede Simulation)

Establishing that simulation must precede prose resolves four catastrophic failure modes inherent in unconstrained generative engines:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        FAILURE MODES OF INVERTED AUTHORITY                             │
├───────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Omniscient State Bleed        │ LLMs leak unobserved secrets across attention heads     │
│                               │ because prompt contexts contain global world bibles.   │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ The Median Regression Trap    │ Autoregressive models regress to statistical medians,  │
│ ("The Beige Plague")          │ diluting tone into polite, therapeutic concordance.    │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ The Forbidden Patch Loop      │ Human line-editing desynchronizes the causal event     │
│                               │ log from character psychology and world state.         │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Biological & Somatic Amnesia  │ Without strict invariants, models allow amputees to    │
│                               │ climb or injured characters to run without penalty.    │
└───────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



1. **Omniscient State Bleed:** When an LLM is asked to write a scene while given a full background lore bible, its attention heads inevitably leak facts. A character who should be completely ignorant of a theft drops subtle hints or prematurely reconciles, destroying dramatic irony and narrative tension.


2. **The Median Regression Trap ("The Beige Plague"):** Unconstrained language models suffer from an inherent "closure bias". When two characters engage in bitter conflict, the model naturally regresses toward the statistical median of its training corpus—smoothing over grievances, flattening idiosyncratic dialects, and forcing unearned, polite reconciliations.


3. **The Forbidden Patch Loop:** If a human director or editor dislikes a generated line of dialogue and directly rewrites the script text on the page, the relational ledger has no record of *why* the character spoke. The event log, causal graph, and psychological meters desynchronize from the script. To prevent this, the architecture bans direct text patching in favor of the **Architectural Ratification Loop**: the user adjusts the underlying mechanical constraints (e.g., raising Threat, adjusting somatic limits), and the deterministic engine regenerates the expression.


4. **Biological and Somatic Amnesia:** Open-ended prompting cannot reliably track physical debuffs. Under cognitive load, models suffer context drift: characters use broken hands, run on shattered knees, or retrieve weapons from pockets that were emptied two scenes earlier.



---

### IV. The Seven-Stage Causal Pipeline: How Reality is Determined Before Writing Begins

In **`worker-sower-engine`**, the progression from world state to finished dialogue AST executes through seven strictly ordered stages:

```text
[ STAGE 1: SPATIAL & SOMATIC INGRESS ]
  • Reads immutable canon (000.goblin-lore) via read-only boundary
  • Ingests dynamic state (hex coords, paranoia clocks, somatic trauma)
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 2: DYADIC INVARIANT MATCHING ]
  • Computes Jaccard boredom penalties across recent scene history
  • Audits Normalized Pairing Entropy (H_norm) to guarantee cast balance
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 3: CONFLICT & GRIEVANCE SELECTION ]
  • goblin-spatial computes Possibility (LoS supercover, acoustic BFS)
  • Sower selects Occurrence: Set logic matches active grievances
  • Heuristic expends social leverage tokens (Strings)
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 4: MODULAR MECHANICAL DUEL RESOLUTION ]
  • Opposed 2d6 Tabletop Roll: D = Roll_A - Roll_B across 1,296 outcomes
  • Pure Reducer commits state delta: S_{n+1} = R(S_n, E_n, σ_n)
  • Compiles immutable ScenePlan IR (Mandated exit conditions)
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 5: CONSTRAINED PROSE REALIZATION ]
  • Injects Spatial Stage Header at Line 0 of .fountain
  • Enforces Ignorance by Omission (BeliefRecord context scrubbing)
  • Tripartite Attention Sandwich guides local LLM (T = 0.75) or Human
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 6: THE COMPILE-TIME INVARIANT GAUNTLET (T_0 = $0.00) ]
  • Extracts Dual-Field AST: prose text + extracted_motor_actions
  • Asserts: extracted_motor_actions ∩ banned_kinetic_verbs == ∅
  • Linear Somatic SAT verifies hand and limb capacity limits
                    │
                    ▼
[ STAGE 7: TIME MEASUREMENT & ACOUSTIC SETTLEMENT ]
  • Local TTS synthesizes 48 kHz uncompressed .wav stems
  • ffprobe measures microsecond duration: target_frames = ceil(ms/1000 * 24)
  • Quantizes elapsed simulation ticks; releases the clock brake

```



---

### V. Mathematical Adjudication of Conflict (Stage 3 & Stage 4)

The core mechanism ensuring that simulation precedes prose is that **confrontation outcomes are settled by pure probability distributions, not semantic plausibility**.

#### 1. Possibility vs. Occurrence

The pipeline partitions spatial reality between two decoupled engines:

* **The Spatial Oracle (`goblin-spatial`):** A pure, stateless geometry kernel operating on an exact doubled integer axial hex lattice ($(4q + 2r, 6r)$). It evaluates **Possibility**: *Can Actor A see Actor B? Can sound penetrate the wall? Can an actor reach the valve?* It returns sealed mathematical receipts (integer centi-decibels, supercover raycast collisions).


* **The Sovereign Ledger (`worker-sower-engine`):** Evaluates **Occurrence**: *Because A can see B, and because their resentment accumulator has crossed its escalation ceiling, A confronts B.*



#### 2. The Grievance Set Filter

Active conflicts are selected via deterministic set logic over the authored YAML frontmatter in `000.goblin-lore`:


$$\text{Eligible} = (\text{SceneCast} \cap \text{Primary}) \setminus \text{Excluded} \quad \text{where} \quad \text{Intensity} \in [\text{Min}, \text{Max}] \land \text{Cooldown} = 0$$



#### 3. Opposed $2\text{d}6$ Duel Math

When an encounter occurs, the stakes are adjudicated through opposed $2\text{d}6$ rolls modified by character attributes, accumulated trauma, and social leverage (`Strings`). The discrete difference $D = X_1 - X_2$ across all $36 \times 36 = 1{,}296$ permutations forms a symmetric distribution centered at zero:

$$P(D = d) = \sum_{s=2}^{12} P(A = s) \cdot P(B = s - d)$$



```
                     OPPOSED 2d6 OUTCOME DISTRIBUTION (N = 1,296)
  25% ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
      │                                                                        │
  20% │                       ██████  [±1: 21.60%]                             │
      │                       ██████          ██████  [±2: 19.29%]             │
  15% │                       ██████          ██████          ██████  [±3: 16.05%]
      │       ██████ [0:11.27%]██████         ██████          ██████           │
  10% │       ██████          ██████          ██████          ██████   ░░░░░░  │
      │       ██████          ██████          ██████          ██████   ░░░░░░  │
   5% │       ██████          ██████          ██████          ██████   ░░░░░░  │
   0% └───┴───┴───────┴───────┴───────┴───────┴───────┴───────┴───────┴───┴────┘
          0      ±1      ±2      ±3      ±4      ±5      ±6      ±7...   ±10
         [     Mutual Wound / Costly Win     ]   [         Clean Win         ]
                 |D| ≤ 2: 52.16%                      |D| ≥ 4: 31.79%

```



* **Near-Parity Domination ($\vert{}D\vert{} \le 2$):** Represents $52.16\%$ of all contests. The baseline dynamic of the universe is friction, compromise, and mutual wear, preventing easy heroics.


* **The Outcome Tiers:**
* $D = 0$ ($11.27\%$): **Stalemate / Deflection**.


* $\vert{}D\vert{} = 1$ ($21.60\%$): **Mutual Wound** (both parties suffer somatic or social damage).


* $\vert{}D\vert{} = 2$ ($19.29\%$): **Costly Win / Concession** (winner gains objective but pays fatigue or item wear).


* $\vert{}D\vert{} = 3$ ($16.05\%$): **Tactical Shift**.


* $\vert{}D\vert{} \ge 4$ ($31.79\%$): **Clean Win Threshold** (decisive victory without direct concession).


* $\vert{}D\vert{} \ge 6$ ($10.80\%$): **Structural Rout / Rupture** (permanent psychological or bodily break).





#### 4. The `ScenePlan IR` Intermediate Contract

The result of this roll is stamped into a typed JSON intermediate contract (`ScenePlan IR`) before any drafting engine is invoked:

* Declares the winner, loser, and exact outcome tier.


* Mandates the exact state transitions that *must* occur upon completion.


* Establishes the non-negotiable **Required Exit Condition** (e.g., *"Wart surrenders physical possession of the iron scraper and retreats past the pipe seam"*).



---

### VI. Structural Epistemic Containment: Ignorance by Omission

When prose drafting begins (whether via a local LLM or a human showrunner), epistemic boundaries are protected by structural data engineering rather than prompt instructions:

$$\text{World Truth} \neq \text{Internal Belief} \neq \text{External Utterance}$$



* **The Inefficacy of Behavioral Prompts:** Instructing an LLM to *"act as if you do not know Bog stole the valve"* routinely fails because transformer attention heads leak latent information across the context window.


* **Ignorance by Omission:** The engine physically purges unobserved facts from the prompt envelope. If an event is not registered in a character's discrete `BeliefRecord` with valid sensory provenance (e.g., optical witness receipt or acoustic SNR margin), **the data does not exist in the character's universe**.


* **The Ambiguity Shield:** World mysteries authored in `unresolved.schema.ts` (such as the true cause of the Great Famine) are treated as non-binary epistemic constants. The engine prevents language models or duel reducers from resolving the mystery; canonical ambiguity is preserved until an explicit human Git commit ratifies a change.



---

### VII. The Compile-Time Invariant Gauntlet ($T_0 = \$0.00$)

When candidate screenplay prose is returned, it must pass through an automated, compile-time invariant linter before touching any rendering hardware:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ SOWER: PRE-GENERATION CONSTRAINTS                                      │
│ • Compiles BeliefRecord into sanitized prompt context                  │
│ • Injects hard negative motor constraints based on Somatic Vector      │
│ • Mandates Dual-Field Action JSON Schema                               │
└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ LLM Inference Call
                                    ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ UNTRUSTED GENERATIVE MODEL (e.g., Qwen / Claude / Local LLM)           │
│ • Emits: prose dialogue line                                           │
│ • Emits: extracted_motor_actions: ["LIMP", "SPEAK", "LEAN"]            │
└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ Emits Candidate AST
                                    ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ THE PANOPTICON INVARIANT GAUNTLET (T_0 = $0.00)                        │
│                                                                        │
│   extracted_motor_actions  ∩  banned_kinetic_verbs  ==  ∅              │
│                                                                        │
│ • O(1) set-intersection check against actor's Somatic Vector           │
│ • Pass: Wraps and serializes final dialogue_ast.json                   │
│ • Fail: Aborts execution immediately; zero GPU render compute billed   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



1. **The Dual-Field AST:** Instead of parsing unstructured prose with fragile regular expressions, the engine forces the authoring layer to emit a typed JSON structure pairing natural dialogue with an explicit enum array of physical movements (`extracted_motor_actions`).


2. **Zero-Token Invariant Assertion:** The linter executes an instantaneous $O(1)$ set-intersection check between the motor actions and the character's compiled `banned_kinetic_verbs`:



$$\text{extracted\_motor\_actions} \cap \text{banned\_kinetic\_verbs} = \emptyset$$



If a character with brachial nerve damage or an amputated limb emits `["GRAB", "PULL"]`, the AST is rejected immediately.


3. **The Economic Rule ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** Halting an invalid, contradictory, or anatomically impossible scene at $T_0$—in system RAM before dispatching to speech synthesizers or GPU video diffusion nodes—costs **exactly $\$0.00$**.



---

### VIII. The Acoustic Clock: Sound as the Physical Arbiter of Time

Once prose clears the invariant gauntlet, the final bridge between literary drama and simulation reality is established through physical measurement:

* **Prose Has No Inherent Time:** Neither humans nor language models can accurately predict how long a line of written dialogue takes to speak in physical space.


* **Empirical Acoustic Measurement:** The conductor dispatches dialogue lines to a local TTS service (port 8000), producing uncompressed $48\text{ kHz}$ `.wav` stems. The engine spawns `ffprobe` to measure the waveform length ($D_{\text{ms}}$) down to the microsecond.


* **Quantization into Simulation Time:** The acoustic duration derives the exact video frame budget at $24\text{ fps}$ ($\lceil D_{\text{ms}} / 1000 \times 24 \rceil$) and determines the integer simulation centisecond ticks that elapsed in the world.


* **Releasing the Clock Brake:** The master simulation clock advances by the exact measured duration, background entities catch up via closed-form algebraic integration, and the clockwork world resumes autonomous progression.



---

Under the axiom that **Simulation Precedes Prose**, the traditional role of generative AI is inverted:

| Video renderers guess pacing and frame duration.

 | Waveform measurement of spoken audio locks the master timebase.

The generative model is never the author of reality; it is a **constrained language realization engine** whose sole mandate is to make mathematically predetermined physical and social outcomes dramatically compelling.

When the autonomous simulation trips its brake at Tick $t$, the execution does not freeze in a generic vacuum. The pause initiates an **epistemological contract** between the runtime simulation engine (`worker-sower-engine`) and the offline legislative constitution (`000.goblin-lore`).

The connection between the pause and `goblin-lore` operates across **five structural interfaces**:

### I. The Ingestion Airlock: Deep-Freeze Immutability (`src/client.ts`)

`000.goblin-lore` is an offline legislative authority whose job is complete the moment `scripts/compile.ts` mints `compiled/<series-slug>/bible-state.json` stamped with its cryptographic Merkle root hash ($H_{\text{root}}$).

```text
  000.goblin-lore (Offline NVMe)
  └── compiled/under-the-floorboards/bible-state.json (H_root)
                │
                │  file:../000.goblin-lore (package.json direct link)
                ▼
  worker-sower-engine (src/client.ts)
  └── deepFreeze(bibleState) ──► In-Memory Canonical Singleton (Zero Mutation)

```



* **Zero Downstream Markdown Parsing:** When the pause occurs, `worker-sower-engine` is strictly barred from reading or parsing raw `.md` files in `/characters` or `/locations`. Downstream engines consume exclusively the pre-compiled, lexicographically sorted JSON payload.


* **Defensive Deep-Freezing:** The client wrapper (`bibleLoader.ts` / `src/client.ts`) imports the JSON artifact and immediately passes it through a recursive `deepFreeze<T>()` traversal:


```typescript
export function loadCanonicalBible(seriesSlug: string): DeepReadonly<BibleState> {
  const raw = fs.readFileSync(`node_modules/@camp_candor/001.lore/compiled/${seriesSlug}/bible-state.json`, 'utf-8');
  const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
  return deepFreeze(parsed);
}

```





* **Enforced Memory Panic:** If any transient routine within the pause handler attempts to write or alter canonical properties (e.g., executing `bible.characters['char_bog'].somatic.locomotion = 'crawling'`), the V8 runtime throws an immediate, fatal `TypeError`. The static law of the universe cannot be modified in working memory.

3. **The Grievance Spring (`grievances/*.md`):** Injects the **Machine Rail** (cooldowns, intensity limits) and the **Voice Rail** (the "Surface Argument" vs. "The Underneath"), ensuring dialogue does not descend into generic squabbling but explores the exact thematic debts defined in `goblin-lore`.

### III. Epistemic Containment: Enforcing Ignorance by Omission

A major failure mode in generative narrative pipelines is **Omniscient State Bleed**—language models leaking canonical secrets across attention heads because the entire world bible was stuffed into the prompt.

When the simulation pauses, `000.goblin-lore` governs what the authoring layer is **forbidden from knowing**:

```
                         P_clues  ∩  ( ⋃ t ∈ T )  ==  ∅

```



1. **The Unresolved Canon Registry (`ontology/unresolved.json`):**
`goblin-lore` explicitly registers existential narrative mysteries (e.g., `the_famine`, `the_vanished_sibling`, `the_pantry_watcher`). Each mystery defines two disjoint sets:


* A set of forbidden trigger lemmas ($T$).


* A set of permitted physical clue tokens ($P_{\text{clues}}$).




2. **The Clue-Lemma Disjointness Theorem:**
Pass 2 of `scripts/compile.ts` evaluates that no permitted physical clue contains any forbidden resolution lemma:

$$P_{\text{clues}} \cap \left( \bigcup_{t \in T} t \right) = \emptyset$$





3. **Context Scrubbing (Ignorance by Omission):**
During the pause, the engine cross-references the scene's participants against their subjective `BeliefRecord` in the runtime ledger. If a character does not possess sensory provenance (an optical line-of-sight raycast or an acoustic wavefront detection certified by `goblin-spatial`), **the canonical fact is physically omitted from the generation envelope**. A character cannot speak about or react to a canonical truth they have not earned.

If Wart (who has a severed left arm) is scripted performing a two-handed task, the compiler halts the pipeline immediately.

The linter sums the required hand occupancy of all held items declared in the scene against anatomical capacity:



$$\sum_{s \in \text{held\_items}} s.\text{occupies\_hands} \le \text{character.available\_arms}$$





* **Zero-Dollar Firewall ($T_0 = \$0.00$):**
Catching these narrative, anatomical, or logistical impossibilities while the simulation is paused in system memory costs **exactly $\$0.00$**. If these defects slipped downstream into neural TTS or video diffusion rendering, they would burn GPU hours and human remediation labor.

### V. The Upstream Promotion Valve: "The Human on the Handle"

A fundamental law of the architecture is that **downstream execution never directly writes back to `000.goblin-lore**`.

If the simulation duel at Tick $t$ results in an irreversible, catastrophic world event (e.g., Bog's left arm is crushed by the pipe valve, or Wart is permanently killed), **the runtime engine cannot automatically rewrite `char_bog.md` or delete `wart.md**`. Doing so would create unvetted narrative drift and break deterministic rebuilds.

Instead, the event must flow through the **Asymmetric Staging Membrane via the Human on the Handle**:

1. **State Delta Emission:** When the scene wraps and time settlement concludes, the engine writes the physical consequences to dynamic database tables (D1/SQLite) and exports an atomic `state_delta.json`.


2. **The Archival Review Gate (`SceneReviewGate.tsx`):** The consequence is quarantined in the dashboard. A human showrunner must inspect the change against the series' causal history.


3. **Physical Dossier Mutation via Git PR:** If ratified, a human author (or an authorized agent like `000.repo-bot` or `Jules`) commits a formal Pull Request to `000.goblin-lore`:


* Moves `wart.md` to `characters/_retired/wart.md` (adhering to the Deletion Principle: entities are retired, never deleted).


* Updates Bog's frontmatter to `available_arms: 1.0` and appends `bimanual_lift` to `banned_kinetic_verbs`.




4. **Re-Sealing the Universe:** The 3-pass compiler (`scripts/compile.ts`) re-runs offline on Rig 1, sweeping the updated graph for dangling pointers and minting a new SHA-256 state seal ($H_{\text{root}}$) in `bible-state.json`.


5. **Reactive Invalidation:** The updated canonical hash invalidates downstream cached scenes (`is_dirty = 1` via `scene_fact_dependencies`), ensuring future episodes compile against the new anatomical reality.
