### Comparative Architecture: Traditional Production vs. The 10-Year Deca-Spine

| Failure Vector | Traditional 10-Year Franchise (Human/Ad-hoc) | The Camp Candor Cauldron Architecture |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Somatic / Injury Drift** | Writers forget; characters use broken hands or dead eyes 3 seasons later. | **Compile-Time SAT Solver ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** Set-intersection checks against `banned_kinetic_verbs` abort impossible actions in RAM. |
| **Script Supervisor Drift** | Human line edits in NLE sever script text from character psychology. | **SceneReviewGate.tsx:** Freehand script editing is banned; closed error taxonomy and parametric dials force re-compilation of AST. |
| **Retcon Fallout** | Retcons silently break background lore, causing massive plot holes. | **Relational Dependency Pinning:** SQL triggers instantly mark affected downstream scenes as `is_dirty = 1`, blocking GPU dispatch via `HTTP 412`. |
| **Hardware / Tool Outage** | Cloud or render crashes stall writer room; mid-flight data is corrupted. | **SIMULATION_ONLY Decoupling:** Narrative engine runs at $\$0.001$/event on CPU; unrendered ASTs queue safely in append-only backlog. |
| **Epistemological Calcification** | Casual, early throwaway lines become unchangeable structural prisons. | **The "Still Good" Audit Protocol:** In-degree centrality telemetry ($k_{\text{in}}$) proactively flags calcified lore for showrunner re-affirmation or retirement. |

Through this comprehensive array of firewalls—separating meaning from representation, eliminating unvetted script patching, enforcing relational cache invalidation at the network edge, isolating simulation math from physical GPU silicon, and systematically auditing graph vitality—the architecture guarantees that **a serialized fictional universe remains 100% causal, auditable, and dramatically alive across decades of continuous automated production**.

### I. Executive Summary: The Conflict Between Writer Convenience and Causal Determinism

In algorithmic and AI-assisted screenwriting, the central operational friction occurs at the **Editorial Review Gate** (`SceneReviewGate.tsx`), where human showrunners evaluate generated scene dialogue and character blocking before downstream audio synthesis and video rendering occur.

When a human editor encounters an unsatisfactory dialogue line, an unearned emotional reconciliation, or an awkward dramatic pause, the intuitive impulse is to open the screenplay file (`.fountain`) and directly rewrite the text. In conventional filmmaking, this is standard script editing. In an event-sourced, deterministic generative pipeline governed by the ontological tuple $\langle \mathcal{S}, \mathcal{A}, \mathcal{I}, \mathcal{R}_m \rangle$, this practice is codified as **The Forbidden Patch Loop**—the single greatest point of systemic failure in the architecture.

```text
❌ THE FORBIDDEN PATCH LOOP (Systemic Causality Rot)
Human line-edits screenplay (.fountain) or NLE text directly
  │
  ▼
The relational ledger loses track of WHY the character spoke
  │
  ▼
Causality, BeliefRecords, and dynamic world state immediately desynchronize
  │
  ▼
Future episodic memory, procedural duels, and background simulation permanently drift from media

──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

✅ THE DETERMINISTIC ARCHITECTURAL RATIFICATION LOOP (Causal Alignment at T_0 = $0.00)
Human flags flaw via Closed Taxonomy (UNEARNED_RELEASE) OR tunes dramatic parameters (C, P, T, S)
  │
  ▼
Interface extracts leaked lemmas, appends to banned token masks, or updates somatic/stress ledgers
  │
  ▼
Simulation compiler regenerates the Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU) AST patch from verified preconditions
  │
  ▼
Event logged to eval/rejections.jsonl; Canonical Ledger, Event Log, and Dialogue AST remain 100% unified

```



To prevent long-term franchise decay, the pipeline replaces unstructured manual line editing with the **Architectural Ratification Loop**. Under this doctrine, **human operators never patch generated words; they mutate underlying meaning constraints, forcing the deterministic engine to re-derive the expression**.

---

### II. The Anatomy of The Forbidden Patch Loop (Systemic Causality Rot)

The prohibition against direct screenplay line editing is rooted in the mathematical separation of **Axis A (Simulation Law & Causality)** from **Axis B (Representation & Artifact Hardening)**.

```text
               AXIS A: SEMANTIC TRUTH & MEANING (Irreversible Causal Spine)
┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐
│    CANON     │ ──► │    EVENTS    │ ──► │    STATE     │ ──► │    MEDIA     │
│  (000.lore)  │     │ (event_log)  │     │ (CANON_DB)   │     │ (.fountain)  │
└──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘
                                                                       │
                               THE FORBIDDEN PATCH LOOP:               │
                       Direct Human Line-Edit Severing the Root        │
                                       │                               │
                                       ▼                               ▼
                             [ UNRECORDED PSYCHOLOGY ] ◄─── [ HAND-EDITED SCRIPT ]
                             • No Tabletop Duel Event        • Bog says forgiving line
                             • No Somatic Strain Delta       • Wart keeps rusty tool
                             • No Epistemic Transfer         • Database thinks they fought

```



#### 1. The Loss of Causal Origin

When an LLM generates a dialogue line, that line is the downstream projection of specific upstream causes: an active dyadic grievance, an opposed $2\text{d}6$ duel margin ($\Delta_{\text{margin}}$), spatial proximity, and psychological tension meters.

If an editor types a sharp retort or a tender reconciliation directly into the script file:


$$\text{Human directly edits text} \implies \text{Relational Database loses the causal origin of the utterance[cite: 3, 10, 16]}$$


The simulation engine has no record of *why* the character spoke. The utterance is an ungrounded anomaly with zero causal ancestry in the event ledger.

#### 2. The Causality Drift Spiral

The consequences of a manual line edit do not remain confined to a single scene; they metastasize forward across the multi-season catalog:

* **Psychological Desynchronization:** If the script is hand-edited so that Bog forgives Wart, but the event ledger still registers that Bog won a `DECISIVE_WIN` and holds high resentment, future autonomous simulation ticks will simulate Bog acting out of extreme spite.


* **Epistemic Horizon Corruption:** If an editor inserts a dialogue reference to a stolen key to make a scene more dramatic, the engine's `BeliefRecord` for that character is not updated. In subsequent episodes, the character will alternate between knowing and not knowing the secret, creating blatant plot holes.


* **Biological and Inventory Drift:** If an editor scripts a character picking up a lantern, but the relational ledger does not log the inventory transfer (`possessions.holder_id`), the character will possess the item visually while remaining incapable of using it during subsequent procedural duels.



---

### III. The Architecture Trap: The Vague Feedback Loop

The Forbidden Patch Loop is frequently provoked by another failure mode: **The Vague Feedback Loop**.

```text
               THE DESTRUCTIVE VAGUE FEEDBACK LOOP (TRADITIONAL)
  Human gives note: "Make it punchier" ──► Stochastic LLM re-rolls entire scene[cite: 16]
                                        ──► Model invents ungrounded facts / breaks canon[cite: 16]
                                        ──► GPU burns $15.00 on unverified video pass[cite: 16]
                                        ──► Showrunner manually patches text in NLE[cite: 16]
                                        ──► Narrative state & causality desynchronize[cite: 16]

               THE DETERMINISTIC RATIFICATION LOOP (ENFORCED)
  Human flags defect: UNEARNED_RELEASE  ──► Interface appends leaked lemma to token mask[cite: 12, 16]
  (Closed Taxonomy via SceneReviewGate) ──► Subtext Suppression clamped: S >= 0.85[cite: 12, 16]
                                        ──► MEU AST patch regenerated at T_0 = $0.00[cite: 12, 16]
                                        ──► Event logged to eval/rejections.jsonl[cite: 12, 16]
                                        ──► State, event log, and causality remain unified[cite: 12, 16]

```

In traditional writers' rooms, directors give subjective, conversational notes: *"Make it punchier"*, *"Feels a bit flat"*, or *"Needs more tension"*.

In a deterministic pipeline, **subjective feedback is an un-compilable defect**:

1. A machine cannot map *"make it punchier"* to an exact scalar delta in the state ledger.


2. If the operator re-prompts an untrusted LLM with vague instructions, the model re-rolls the entire context, altering un-flagged lines, hallucinating new facts, and breaking established canon.


3. Frustrated by stochastic re-rolls, the human editor resorts to directly line-editing the file, triggering the Forbidden Patch Loop.



To make automated compilation work, **vague feedback is structurally eradicated from the user interface**.

---

### IV. The Mechanics of The Architectural Ratification Loop

The **Architectural Ratification Loop** replaces freehand editing and conversational prompting with structured, parameter-driven re-derivation:

$$\mathbf{\text{Governing Principle:}}\quad \text{Human mutates meaning constraints} \longrightarrow \text{Deterministic engine regenerates expression[cite: 6, 10, 16]}$$

#### 1. The Gateway: `SceneReviewGate.tsx`

Mounted at `src/pages/review/animatic.astro`, the `SceneReviewGate.tsx` component acts as the sole human checkpoint governing candidate scenes before speech synthesis or video diffusion occurs. It enforces three operational constraints:

* **Strict Temporal Budget ($\le 60\text{ s}$):** The interface is designed so that a showrunner can evaluate the dramatic stakes, physical conflict, subtext, and mechanical state mutations within 60 seconds. If a scene requires longer to parse, the review artifact has failed.


* **No Raw Syntax Trees:** The human director is never exposed to raw JSON keys, AST schemas, or tensor configurations. The interface renders plain-text operational summaries and clean screenplay layouts.


* **Debugging Output Duality:** The review screen serves a dual purpose: an editorial sign-off terminal for human creators, and a forensic trace explaining *why* the engine selected this specific grievance, cast pairing, and tabletop duel outcome.



```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        SCENEREVIEWGATE.TSX (TRIAGE HUD)                                │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ SCENE: EP101_SC03_TICK_42   | LOCATION: The Warm Pipe   | CAST: Bog, Wart              │
│ MECHANICAL DUEL: COSTLY_WIN (Bog over Wart)             | MARGIN: +2                   │
│ MANDATED EXIT: Wart drops claim to iron scraper; Bog accumulates +450 bp strain        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ CANDIDATE PROSE PREVIEW:                                                               │
│   Bog: "Step back off the seam, Wart. It's marked."                                    │
│   Wart: "I found it near the drain! You don't own every scrap of rust in the floor."  │
│   Bog: "I own the pipe. I own what touches it. Drop the scraper or crawl cold."        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ [ REJECT SCENE (Select Closed Taxonomy) ]               [ ADJUST DRAMATIC DIALS ]      │
│ [x] UNEARNED_RELEASE (Characters reconciled too fast)   Threat (T):       [───●────] 82│
│ [ ] SOMATIC_BREACH (Action violates physical debuff)    Curtness (C):     [─────●──] 90│
│ [ ] EPISTEMIC_LEAK (Actor speaks unobserved secret)     Subtext Suppr (S):[──────●─] 95│
│ [ ] CADENCE_COLLAPSE (Modern therapy jargon detected)   Pacing / Hold (P):[──●─────] 40│
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                   [ EXECUTE ASYMMETRIC MEU SURGICAL RE-DERIVATION ]                    │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 2. The Closed Rejection Taxonomy

The director cannot leave open-ended text comments. Instead, rejection requires selecting an explicit failure tag from a closed, machine-readable taxonomy:

| Diagnostic Error Code | Mechanical System Failure | Automated Compiler Response |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **`UNEARNED_RELEASE`**

 | Characters smoothed over resentment without mechanical duel expenditure.

 | Clamps Subtext Suppression ($S \ge 0.85$); appends reconciling words to negative lemma mask.

 |
| **`SOMATIC_BREACH`**

 | Candidate text depicts an action violating physical body constraints.

 | Injects hard negative motor masks; recalculates linear hand-saturation SAT solver.

 |
| **`EPISTEMIC_LEAK`**

 | Character speaks about an unobserved fact or unresolved mystery.

 | Scrubs leaked fact ID from prompt context; reinforces Ignorance by Omission boundary.

 |
| **`CADENCE_COLLAPSE`**

 | Dialogue regresses to statistical median ("beige" modern therapy speak).

 | Clamps Curtness ($C \ge 0.90$); restricts max sentence length to $\le 8$ words.

 |

#### 3. Parametric Dramatic Dials ($C, P, T, S$)

Alongside diagnostic tags, the director tunes four continuous basis-point sliders governing the prompt envelope:

* **Curtness ($C$):** Controls word-count budgets per turn and dialect compression.


* **Pacing / Hold ($P$):** Adjusts the frequency and duration of Dwight Swain Motivation-Reaction Unit (MRU) reaction holds and non-verbal pauses.


* **Threat ($T$):** Modulates the explicit physical or social stakes asserted in dialogue.


* **Subtext Suppression ($S$):** Suppresses direct emotional exposition, forcing models to encode hostility into mundane logistical arguments.



#### 4. The Asymmetric Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU) Surgical Infill

When the director commands re-derivation, the system does not re-roll the entire scene. That would destroy satisfactory lines and burn unmetered compute.

The engine extracts the **Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU)**—the smallest slice of the Dialogue AST containing the failure:

* **The Forward Sweep ($I_{\text{end}}$):** The engine analyzes dependencies downstream from the rejected beat. If Beat 2 is rejected, and Beat 3 directly answers it, the MEU spans Beats 2 through 3.


* **The 40% Span Ceiling:** If the MEU sweep forces the repair window to consume **more than 40% of the scene**, automated infill aborts immediately and escalates the scene to a human Story Architect, preventing hidden scene re-writes.


* **The Seam Audit Gauntlet:** The newly generated MEU is spliced back into the scene and passed through a 4-stage boundary check (grammatical tense stability, coreference preservation, physical continuity of held items, and verbatim `.fountain`/AST parity).


* **Zero-Dollar Gating ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** Re-deriving an MEU occurs entirely in system RAM at compile time. It burns zero GPU cycles, zero audio synthesis time, and logs an audited trace into `eval/rejections.jsonl`.



---

### V. Axis A vs. Axis B: When Are Human Code Edits Permitted?

While direct freehand editing of **screenplay prose** is banned, human directors and technical artists routinely need to refine visual staging, camera composition, and timing. The architecture resolves this via the **Sealed Lifecycle Contract** in `Episode_XXX.tsx`:

```text
               THE TS-MORPH SEALED LIFECYCLE CONTRACT
               
   [ @lifecycle: generated ]  ──► Authored by generate-episode-repo.ts
              │                   Full automated overwrite authority on rerun[cite: 12, 15]
              ▼
   [ @lifecycle: patched ]    ──► Authored by Tier 3 Diagnostic Agent (Jules)
              │                   Surgically patches JSX layout props (e.g. mouth offset)[cite: 12, 15]
              │                   Blocks unstaged overwrites; enforces AST diff gates[cite: 12, 15]
              ▼
   [ @lifecycle: locked ]     ──► Authored by Human Showrunner ("The Eject Button")
                                  HARD HALT against automated overwrites[cite: 12, 15]
                                  Forks upstream reruns to .candidate.tsx[cite: 12, 15]
                                  Binds human edit as DIRECTOR_OVERRIDE event[cite: 12, 15, 39]

```

#### 1. Meaning-Altering Modifications (Routed to Axis A)

Any intervention that changes **what happened**—such as spawning an unscripted prop, modifying a character injury, or altering a grievance outcome—**cannot be patched locally in the viewport code**.

* It must route through the Architectural Ratification Loop to re-derive down the DAG, OR:


* It must execute as a cryptographically signed `DIRECTOR_OVERRIDE` event (opcode: `OP_MIRACLE`, injection type: `SPAWN_PROP`) via `overrides.astro`, appending directly to the immutable narrative audit ledger.


* Instead of an ad-hoc code change, the human edit becomes a **first-class historical event**:


```json
{
  "type": "DIRECTOR_OVERRIDE",
  "opcode": "OP_MIRACLE",
  "target": "shot_040_003",
  "action": "SPAWN_PROP",
  "item_id": "item_brass_key",
  "recipient": "char_wart",
  "reason": "Dramatic necessity for Season 2 climax",
  "author": "showrunner_01"
}

```





#### 2. Representation-Altering Modifications (Localized on Axis B)

Presentation-only polishes (camera easing curves, framing coordinates, audio gain ducking, viseme vertical offsets) remain inside `Episode_XXX.tsx`:

* **`@lifecycle: generated`:** Standard automated build artifact; cleanly overwritten on upstream reruns.


* **`@lifecycle: patched`:** Applied by automated repair agents (`Jules`) using `ts-morph` to correct layout alignment without invalidating the upstream timeline.


* **`@lifecycle: locked` ("The Eject Button"):** Stamped with the showrunner's manual signature. Triggers a hard lock against automated compilers. If upstream scripts re-run, the compiler leaves the locked file intact and writes candidate outputs to `Episode_XXX.candidate.tsx`, alerting the director.



---

### VI. Error Trajectory Analysis & The Three-Cycle Circuit Breaker

When automated agents (e.g., `Jules`) execute repairs on candidate scenes or viewport files, they must not enter an infinite loop trying to solve impossible constraints. The pipeline models repair cycles as a stateful **Error Signature Vector ($\vec{E}$)**:

$$\vec{E} = \langle \text{Domain}, \, \text{TargetEntity}, \, \text{RuleID}, \, \text{OffendingTokens} \rangle \text{[cite: 26]}$$

```text
               INVARIANT LINTER FLAGS DEFECT ON ATTEMPT 1: E_1
                                      │
                                      ▼
                        [ Jules Executes Patch Take 2 ]
                                      │
                               Did Take 2 Pass?
                               ┌──────┴──────┐
                               ▼ YES         ▼ NO
                          [ Commit PR ]      │
                                             ▼
                               [ Trajectory Analysis ]
                               Compare Error Signatures:
                               Vector E_1 vs. Vector E_2
                                             │
                       ┌─────────────────────┴─────────────────────┐
                       ▼ CONVERGENCE                               ▼ DIVERGENCE
      Domain(E_1) == Domain(E_2)                  Domain(E_1) != Domain(E_2)
      Attempt 1: 'sprint' is banned               Attempt 1: 'sprint' is banned
      Attempt 2: 'dash' is banned                 Attempt 2: 'revealed secret poison'
      Cause: Local phrasing basin                 Cause: Mutually exclusive constraints
                       │                                           │
             [ Allow Attempt 3 ]                         [ HARD CIRCUIT BREAKER ]
          Inject amplified negative                   • Abort patch loop immediately
          constraint block                            • Wipe branch to S_clean
                       │                              • Route Diagnostic Dossier to
              Did Attempt 3 pass?                       Tier 2 Story Architect
                 ┌─────┴─────┐
                 ▼ YES       ▼ NO
            [ Commit ]  [ Escalate to Writer ]

```



#### 1. Convergence vs. Divergence

Between repair attempts, the trajectory analyzer compares error signatures:

* **Convergence (Local Phrasing Basin):** $\text{Domain}(E_1) \equiv \text{Domain}(E_2)$ (e.g., swapping "sprinted" for "dashed"). The model understands the setup but is linguistically stuck. The engine injects an amplified negative prompt and permits a final Attempt 3.


* **Divergence (Structural Conflict):** $\text{Domain}(E_1) \neq \text{Domain}(E_2)$. If fixing a somatic movement causes the character to blurt out a forbidden secret, the constraints are **mutually exclusive** ($C_{\text{somatic}} \cap C_{\text{epistemic}} = \emptyset$).



#### 2. The Hard Circuit Breaker ($N = 3$)

Automated repairs are clamped to a strict maximum of **$N = 3$ iterations**:

* If Attempt 2 diverges, or if Attempt 3 fails, the circuit breaker trips immediately.


* The system executes an atomic git rollback (`git checkout -- .`) back to clean state ($S_{\text{clean}}$).


* The task status flips to `#ESCALATED_REVISION_CEILING_BREACHED`.


* A complete `DiagnosticEscalationReport` is dispatched to the human Story Architect, citing the mutually exclusive constraints and previous diffs. Compute is never burned on impossible narrative geometries.



---

### VII. Upward Scrutiny: The Rule Override Density Flywheel

The final bridge between the Forbidden Patch Loop and the Architectural Ratification Loop is **Upward Scrutiny**.

While the compiler enforces downward consistency with machine perfection, software invariants can be flawed or miscalibrated. If writers constantly hit a wall because an arbitrary rule forbids sentences longer than 12 words during emotional climaxes, the system must not force them into an endless re-derivation loop.

To detect bad rules, the engine tracks **Rule Override Density ($D_{\text{override}}$)** across all studio tasks:

$$D_{\text{override}}(\text{rule}) = \frac{\text{Overrides} + \text{CircuitBreakerTrips}}{\text{Total Task Invocations}} \text{[cite: 4, 13, 23]}$$

* **The 15% Caliber Threshold:** If $D_{\text{override}}(\text{rule}) > 0.15$ (a rule trips or requires overrides more than 15% of the time), the invariant is flagged as **obsolete, miscalibrated, or destructive to dramatic vitality**.


* **Automated Escalation to Showrunner:** An automated ticket routes to the Lead Systems Architect and Showrunner to loosen, re-calibrate, or retire the rule in `000.goblin-lore`.



The pipeline enforces physical and narrative law downward with mathematical rigor, while systematically auditing its own assumptions upward whenever dramatic truth demands evolution.

---

### Summary: The Operational Comparison

| Vector | The Forbidden Patch Loop (Banned) | The Architectural Ratification Loop (Enforced) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Primary Action** | Editor directly rewrites words in `.fountain` or NLE.

 | Editor flags defect tag in `SceneReviewGate.tsx` or adjusts dials ($C, P, T, S$).

 |
| **Causal Anchor** | **Severed:** Database has no record of why the line was spoken.

 | **Preserved:** Line is derived from updated parameters, duel outcomes, and ledger state.

 |
| **Downstream Impact** | Episodic memory desynchronizes; characters violate established psychology in future episodes.

 | World state, event log, and dialogue AST remain 100% bit-identical and unified.

 |
| **Compute Cost** | **Deceptive:** Appears free, but triggers cascading GPU/token re-renders downstream.

 | **Zero-Dollar ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** AST re-derivation runs in RAM before any GPU or audio node executes.

 |
| **Repair Scope** | Whole scenes patched manually without diff tracking.

 | Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU) surgical infill ($\le 40\%$ span) with 4-stage seam verification.

 |
| **Systemic Evolution** | Errors are hidden; tools and prompts never improve.

 | Rejections log to `eval/rejections.jsonl`; $D_{\text{override}}$ flags miscalibrated rules for upward revision.

 |### I. Architectural Mandate: The Ontological Decoupling of Truth from Silicon

In traditional generative media pipelines, narrative progression and sensory rendering are collapsed into an indivisible execution loop: a prompt is submitted, a GPU burns VRAM generating video or audio, and the resulting media artifact is inspected to determine what occurred in the story. Under that monolithic paradigm, if physical GPU hardware crashes, overheats, or loses network connectivity, the entire studio production halts.

The Camp Candor Cauldron architecture fundamentally rejects this coupling by enforcing the **Cartesian Iron Partition**:

$$\mathbf{\text{Cartesian Axiom:}}\quad \text{Axis A (Meaning \& Law)} \perp \text{Axis B (Representation \& Pixels)} \text{[cite: 3, 36, 50, 52]}$$

```text
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │                                AXIS A: SEMANTIC TRUTH & MEANING                                  │
 │ • Pure mathematical state reduction: S_{n+1} = R(S_n, E_n, \sigma_n)                             │
 │ • Evaluates physical possibility, somatic trauma, opposed 2d6 duels, and logistical inventories  │
 │ • Hardware Footprint: System RAM & Multi-core CPU threads ($0.001 / event)                       │
 │ • Storage Authority: Cloudflare D1 (CANON_DB) / Local SQLite                                      │
 └────────────────────────────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                                  │
                                   STRUCTURAL AIRLOCK: HARDWARE SEVERANCE
                                   (Is GPU Foundry Silicon Available?)
                                                  │
                        ┌─────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────┐
                        ▼                                                   ▼
         [ FOUNDRY ONLINE: Rig 2 Active ]                  [ FOUNDRY OFFLINE: Rig 2 Dead ]
                        │                                                   │
                        ▼                                                   ▼
 ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │       AXIS B: ARTIFACT HARDENING             │    │             SIMULATION_ONLY MODE             │
 │ • Headless ComfyUI (:8188) samples tensors   │    │ • Bypasses ComfyUI & Remotion entirely       │
 │ • Audio microservice (:8000) emits 48kHz WAV │    │ • Commits state transition directly to D1    │
 │ • Remotion compiles broadcast MP4s           │    │ • Returns COMMITTED_WITHOUT_MEDIA            │
 │ • Cost: Multiple dollars per finished minute │    │ • Queues AST to D1 Persistent Backlog Queue  │
 └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘    └──────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



**`SIMULATION_ONLY` mode** is the operational realization of this decoupling. It establishes that **narrative truth lives exclusively in the relational ledger, not in the physical rendering silicon**. Media (audio stems, diffusion plates, viewport compositions) is merely a heavy presentation layer painted over an immutable, verifiable mechanical core.

When physical rendering hardware (Rig 2) fails, narrative history advances unimpeded at **$\approx \$0.001$ per event**. Writers, simulation engineers, and automated procedural duels continue modifying character bodies, spending fuel, transferring items, and advancing timelines without a single GPU tensor burning.

---

### II. The Trigger Topology: Detecting Foundry Absence at the Network Edge

The routing decision between standard multi-modal production and `SIMULATION_ONLY` execution occurs at the **EdgeGateway Ingress Router** (`src/index.ts` within `edge-director`).

```text
                                  INBOUND SCENE DISPATCH
                                             │
                                             ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ EdgeGateway INGRESS ROUTER (src/index.ts)                                              │
│ • Validates Signed JWT & SpokeExecutionPayload (< 5 ms)                                │
│ • Inspects active WebSocket broker connections: brokerSockets.length                   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                             │
                       ┌─────────────────────┴─────────────────────┐
                       ▼                                           ▼
          [ brokerSockets.length > 0 ]                [ brokerSockets.length === 0 ]
                       │                                           │
                       ▼                                           ▼
            [ NORMAL FOUNDRY PATH ]                    Inspect allowSimulationFallback
            • Forward to ShotCoordinatorDO                         │
            • Acquire hardware mutex lease              ┌──────────┴──────────┐
            • Rig 2 samples diffusion                   ▼ FALSE               ▼ TRUE
            • Output stored to R2 bucket       [ HTTP 503 ERROR ]   [ TRIGGER SIMULATION_ONLY ]
                                               "FOUNDRY_OFFLINE"    • Advance narrative D1 state
                                               (Zero writes allowed)• Return COMMITTED_WITHOUT_MEDIA
                                                                    • Enqueue AST into D1 Backlog

```



#### 1. Hardware Presence Auditing (`brokerSockets.length`)

The `ShotCoordinatorDO` (Durable Object) maintains stateful WebSocket connections with local GPU foundry daemons via outbound encrypted Cloudflare Tunnels.

* When Rig 2 is active, its local daemon (`media-broker` / `rig2-broker` on port 3000) maintains an authenticated WebSocket in the coordinator's connection pool (`brokerSockets.length > 0`).


* If Rig 2 loses power, blows a power supply unit (PSU), suffers a catastrophic PCIe bus fault, trips a thermal threshold, or enters a CUDA Out-of-Memory (OOM) crash loop, the WebSocket terminates.


* The EdgeGateway detects `brokerSockets.length === 0` in $< 5\text{ ms}$ without polling or long HTTP timeouts.



#### 2. The `allowSimulationFallback` Authorization Contract

The system distinguishes between automated background operations and strict visual delivery targets via the incoming dispatch payload contract:

```typescript
// EdgeGateway Ingress Router (src/index.ts)
app.post("/dispatch", async (c) => {
  const payload: SpokeExecutionPayload = await c.req.json();
  const brokerSockets = await getActiveBrokerSockets(c.env);

  // Hardware Absence Detection
  if (brokerSockets.length === 0) {
    if (payload.allowSimulationFallback) {
      console.warn(`[EdgeDirector] Foundry offline. Triggering SIMULATION_ONLY mode for ${payload.sceneId}`);
      
      // Execute pure narrative state mutation directly against Cloudflare D1
      const result = await executeNarrativeStateMutation(c.env.CANON_DB, payload.sceneAst);
      
      // Persist unrendered scene contract into the backlog queue
      await enqueueSceneBacklog(c.env.CANON_DB, {
        sceneId: payload.sceneId,
        episodeNum: payload.episodeNum,
        sceneAst: payload.sceneAst,
        conformedFrames: payload.conformedFrames,
        targetResolution: payload.targetResolution,
        assignedLoRAs: payload.assignedLoRAs,
        prngSeed: payload.prngSeed,
        enqueuedAt: Date.now()
      });

      return c.json({
        status: "COMMITTED_WITHOUT_MEDIA",
        sceneId: payload.sceneId,
        stateDelta: result.delta,
        message: "Narrative state advanced in D1. Rendering queued for batch catch-up when Rig 2 resumes."
      }, 202);
    }

    // Fail-closed perimeter if physical media was strictly demanded
    return c.json({ 
      error: "FOUNDRY_OFFLINE", 
      message: "No active GPU spoke connected and allowSimulationFallback is false." 
    }, 503);
  }

  // Normal execution: forward payload to ShotCoordinatorDO hardware queue
  return forwardToRenderQueue(c, payload);
});

```



* **Strict Releases (`allowSimulationFallback: false`):** If an operator explicitly triggers a production master release where immediate media verification is mandatory, the gateway halts execution with `HTTP 503 Service Unavailable (FOUNDRY_OFFLINE)`. Zero writes are permitted, protecting release integrity.


* **Iterative Writing & Procedural Simulation (`allowSimulationFallback: true`):** When the writers' room is drafting, or when `worker-sower-engine` is simulating background faction movements, execution falls back seamlessly to `SIMULATION_ONLY` mode.



---

### III. Mechanical Execution: Advancing the State Space ($S_n \to S_{n+1}$) at Zero VRAM

In `SIMULATION_ONLY` mode, narrative progression is evaluated as a pure mathematical function:

$$S_{n+1} = \mathcal{R}(S_n, \, E_n, \, \sigma_n) \text{[cite: 3, 44, 52]}$$

Where:

* $S_n$ is the active state space in the relational ledger.


* $E_n$ is the discrete conflict or action event.


* $\sigma_n$ is the deterministic pseudo-random number generator (PRNG) seed.


* $\mathcal{R}$ is the pure reducer function executing in system RAM.



This calculation requires zero GPU compute, zero neural network inferences, and zero display servers.

```sql
-- ATOMIC STATE TRANSACTION EXECUTED AT ZERO VRAM (Cloudflare D1 / SQLite)
BEGIN TRANSACTION;

-- 1. Apply physical trauma and anatomical debuffs to character vectors
UPDATE entity_somatic_states
SET condition = 'FRACTURED_RIGHT_CLAVICLE',
    banned_kinetic_verbs = '["CLIMB", "VAULT", "TWO_HANDED_AIM", "BIMANUAL_LIFT"]',
    fatigue_bps = MIN(10000, fatigue_bps + 2400),
    updated_at = unixepoch()
WHERE entity_id = 'char_pilot' AND scene_context = 'EP101_SC02';

-- 2. Deduct physical logistics and transfer possessions
UPDATE possessions
SET holder_id = 'char_scavenger',
    equip_slot = 'HELD_PRIMARY',
    condition_bps = MAX(0, condition_bps - 1500)
WHERE id = 'item_welding_rig';

-- 3. Mutate spatial occluders and room topologies
UPDATE dynamic_portals
SET state = 'SEALED_WARPED',
    acoustic_damping_bp = 8500
WHERE portal_id = 'bulkhead_door_09';

-- 4. Advance relational social debt meters
UPDATE grievances
SET intensity_bps = 0,
    cooldown_ticks = 4500,
    version = version + 1
WHERE id = 'grv_salvage_theft_ep02';

-- 5. Append immutable event audit receipt to the chronological ledger
INSERT INTO event_log (
  event_id, 
  scene_id, 
  tick, 
  opcode, 
  payload_json, 
  state_hash
) VALUES (
  'evt_01j7x8...', 
  'EP101_SC02', 
  14200, 
  'OP_MUTATE_SOMATIC_AND_INVENTORY', 
  '{"winner":"char_scavenger","margin":3,"trauma":"clavicle"}', 
  'a8f5c4b2e19d...'
);

COMMIT;

```



#### 1. Tabletop Conflict Math in Memory

Before this SQL transaction is committed, `worker-sower-engine` resolves the scene’s mechanical confrontation (e.g., an opposed $2\text{d}6$ duel) in CPU memory:


$$\text{Roll}_A = 2\text{d}6 + \text{Hegemony}_A - \text{SomaticStrain}_A \text{[cite: 3]}$$

$$\text{Roll}_B = 2\text{d}6 + \text{Hegemony}_B - \text{SomaticStrain}_B \text{[cite: 3]}$$

$$\Delta_{\text{margin}} = \text{Roll}_A - \text{Roll}_B \text{[cite: 3]}$$

If the duel resolves as a `COSTLY_WIN` for Character A over Character B, the engine determines the biological, logistical, and social damages without writing prose or invoking language models.

#### 2. The $0.001 Economy

Because this transaction involves only integer arithmetic, Zod schema validation, and SQLite B-tree index updates, the execution footprint is negligible:

* **Execution Time:** $\le 8\text{ ms}$ on Cloudflare Workers / Node.js isolates.


* **VRAM Allocation:** $0\text{ MB}$.


* **Financial Marginal Cost:** $\approx \$0.000005$ in serverless CPU time, or $\$0.00$ on local hardware.



---

### IV. The Persistent Backlog Queue & The Conformed Execution Contract

Advancing narrative history without rendering pixels creates a temporary divergence between **historical state** and **media archives**. To ensure this divergence is strictly accounting debt rather than data loss, the engine writes an immutable, conformed render contract into the `d1_render_backlog` table.

```text
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │                      d1_render_backlog ROW STRUCTURE                                   │
 ├──────────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
 │ queue_id                 │ UUIDv7 (Monotonically time-sortable integer primary key)     │
 │ scene_id                 │ EP101_SC02 (Unique canonical target identifier)             │
 │ episode_num              │ 101 (Target series episode container)                       │
 │ scene_ast_json           │ Full conformed Dialogue AST (Dwight Swain MRU syntax)       │
 │ conformed_frames         │ 2,736 (Exact 24fps frame count locked by acoustic clock)    │
 │ prng_seed                │ 0x8F3A21C9 (Deterministic pseudo-random state seed)         │
 │ assigned_loras_json      │ [{"id": "char_pilot_injured", "weight": 0.85}, ...]         │
 │ target_resolution        │ 1920x1080 (Spatial pixel dimensions)                        │
 │ upstream_merkle_hash     │ H_root = a8f5c4b2e19d... (Immutable world-state anchor)     │
 │ status                   │ PENDING_GPU_CATCHUP                                         │
 │ enqueued_at              │ 2026-10-25T13:42:00.000Z                                    │
 └──────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Why the Backlog Does Not Drift

A common failure mode in deferred rendering is **context drift**: when the GPU finally boots up days later, it attempts to render the scene using the *newest* world state, producing continuity errors (e.g., rendering a character as uninjured because their wound healed in a later episode).

The `d1_render_backlog` eliminates context drift by enforcing **Source-Baking and Dependency Pinning**:

* **The AST is Frozen:** The exact dialogue, facial expressions, and spatial blocking are pre-compiled and locked in `scene_ast_json`.


* **Temporal Budgets are Locked:** The exact frame count (`conformed_frames`) was already determined by acoustic waveform analysis (`ffprobe` on synthesized `.wav` stems).


* **Cryptographic Merkle Anchor ($H_{\text{root}}$):** The render contract stores the exact hash of the world state as it existed at the moment the scene occurred.


* When the GPU eventually processes the job, it renders the exact historical slice mandated by the contract, not the ambient present state of the database.



---

### V. Inverting the Hardware Mutex & Local `flock` Sovereignty

In standard cloud computing, a central server acts as master and commands connected worker machines. In the Camp Candor Cauldron architecture, this paradigm is completely inverted to protect hardware sovereignty.

```text
 ❌ TRADITIONAL REMOTE LOCKING (Fragile / Distributed Failure)
 Cloud Edge DO ──► Acquires Remote Lock ──► Forces GPU Workstation
 (If cloud network drops, local GPU is locked in limbo; deadlock ensues)

 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

 ✅ INVERTED HARDWARE SOVEREIGNTY (Enforced by Cauldron Architecture)
 Remote DO (ShotCoordinatorDO)   ──► Passive Logical Request & Backlog Queue
                                              ▲
                                              │ (Pulls work when healthy)
 Local Daemon (media-broker)     ──► Evaluates Local Hardware & VRAM Headroom
                                              │
                                              ▼ (Enforces local OS-level lock)
 Physical Workstation Silicon    ──► OS-Level File Lock: flock(/dev/nvidia0)

```



#### 1. The Local `flock` Mutex

The local GPU workstation (Rig 2) holds absolute, un-delegated authority over its own physical silicon.

* The `media-broker` daemon enforces an operating system–level file lock (`flock`) on the GPU interfaces (e.g., `/dev/nvidia0` or `/tmp/comfy_gpu.lock`).


* The remote cloud edge cannot force work onto Rig 2.


* Rig 2 opens an **outbound-only encrypted tunnel** to the Cloudflare edge, acting as a consumer that queries for available tasks only when its local VRAM headroom is verified and its `flock` is free.


* If Rig 2 experiences a local thermal crisis, its local daemon releases the connection and shuts down. The edge simply marks `brokerSockets.length === 0` and shifts subsequent inbound requests to `SIMULATION_ONLY` mode. Neither side deadlocks.



---

### VI. Automated Batch Catch-Up & Archive Alignment

When Rig 2 resolves its hardware fault, re-establishes power, and restarts its services, the pipeline initiates the **Automated Batch Catch-Up Protocol** to align physical media archives with the relational ledger.

```text
                               THE BATCH CATCH-UP LIFECYCLE
                                             │
                                             ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 1. HARDWARE REHYDRATION & TUNNEL HANDSHAKE                                             │
│    • Rig 2 boots; ComfyUI daemon initializes on 127.0.0.1:8188                         │
│    • media-broker verifies 24 GB VRAM headroom via nvidia-smi                          │
│    • Outbound tunnel connects to Edge: brokerSockets.length: 0 ──► 1                  │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                             │
                                             ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 2. BACKLOG DRAIN LEASE ACQUISITION                                                     │
│    • media-broker claims lease from ShotCoordinatorDO:                                 │
│      SELECT * FROM d1_render_backlog WHERE status = 'PENDING_GPU_CATCHUP'              │
│      ORDER BY queue_id ASC LIMIT 1;                                                    │
│    • Status updated: 'PROCESSING_BATCH' (stamped with monotonic epoch token)           │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                             │
                                             ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 3. HEADLESS TENSOR EXECUTION & CAS EGRESS                                              │
│    • Injects conformed target_frames and seed into Wan 2.2 / ComfyUI workflow DAG      │
│    • Renders isolated character diffusion plates (#00FF00 green void)                  │
│    • Streams plates to Cloudflare R2 content-addressed storage (CAS)                   │
└────────────────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────────────┘
                                             │
                                             ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 4. RECONCILIATION & QUEUE PRUNING                                                      │
│    • media-broker emits RENDER_COMPLETE receipt with SHA-256 asset CID                 │
│    • EdgeGateway updates scenes table: media_status = 'RENDERED', is_dirty = 0         │
│    • Deletes row from d1_render_backlog; loops to next item until backlog is 0         │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



1. **Reconnection Handshake:** The moment the tunnel connects, `brokerSockets.length` transitions from `0` to `1`. The edge coordinator registers that the physical foundry is operational.


2. **Lease Acquisition:** `media-broker` issues an authenticated pull request for pending backlog items. The edge marks the oldest pending item as `PROCESSING_BATCH` and issues a short-lived monotonic lease to prevent duplicate rendering.


3. **Continuous Background Draining:** Because the upstream simulation, dialogue generation, and audio timing were already solved, the GPU operates purely as a high-throughput tensor printing press. It samples diffusion plates back-to-back without pausing for text deliberation.


4. **Final Media Convergence:** Once the plate is uploaded to Cloudflare R2 and content-addressed by its SHA-256 hash, the edge marks the scene as fully rendered and prunes the backlog entry. The media archive is once again aligned with the state ledger.

### VII. Architectural Failure Topologies: Edge-Case Protections

Operating in `SIMULATION_ONLY` mode introduces specific distributed edge cases, each governed by an explicit architectural defense:

#### 1. The Retcon Hazard During Backlog Drain

* **The Scenario:** While Scene 4 is sitting unrendered in the backlog queue, a writer in the dashboard modifies an upstream fact that invalidates Scene 4.


* **The Defense:** The database trigger `propagate_fact_mutation` sweeps `scene_fact_dependencies`. If a scene's dependency version is stale, the trigger sets `scenes.is_dirty = 1`. When `media-broker` attempts to claim the backlog item, the EdgeGateway intercepts the request and verifies the dirty bit. The dirty backlog job is cancelled and dropped into `eval/cancelled_backlog.jsonl`, preventing the GPU from combusting electricity on an obsolete scene.



#### 2. The Storage Overflow Circuit Breaker

* **The Scenario:** Rig 2 remains offline for weeks while autonomous simulation engines run millions of ticks, threatening to overflow the SQLite/D1 database with gigabytes of pending AST JSON payloads.


* **The Defense:** `d1_render_backlog` enforces a strict row-count ceiling ($N_{\max} = 500\text{ unrendered scenes}$). If the ceiling is reached, the simulation engine trips an automated brake: background ticks pause, and an administrative alert is dispatched to the showrunner’s Blessed TUI:


```text
>> [SIMULATION BRAKE]: Render backlog saturated (500 scenes pending).
>> Narrative simulation paused to prevent headless state-media divergence.
>> Reconnect GPU Foundry (Rig 2) to resume clockwork execution.

```

`SIMULATION_ONLY` mode crystallizes the fundamental philosophical thesis of the Camp Candor Cauldron pipeline:

 | Inside the relational SQL ledger (`CANON_DB`).

 | **Disposable:** If the GPU dies, storytelling advances for $\$0.001$/event.

| **Writers' Room Velocity** | Throttled by 120-second diffusion render passes.

 | Zero-latency: duels and state deltas commit in $\le 8\text{ ms}$.

 |
| **Media Status** | The irreplaceable product.

 | A discardable, reproducible projection layer.

 |

By completely severing the mathematical reality of narrative causality from the physical availability of rendering silicon, **the universe remains sovereign, permanent, and impervious to hardware catastrophe**.

It represents the **fundamental ontological airlock** where continuous, deterministic world-state transitions ($S_t \to S_{t+1}$) temporarily materialize into qualitative, human-readable drama, pass through a zero-cost invariant gauntlet, measure physical time via acoustic sound waves, and re-calcify into an immutable relational ledger before the universe is permitted to tick forward.

### Step 1: Autonomous Progression Along the Timeline to the Dramatic Tipping Point

The simulation does not begin as ambient background noise, nor is it driven by an unconstrained language model hallucinating narrative progression. It operates as an event-sourced, discrete-event state machine running on **Cluster `02.simulation` (`worker-sower-engine`)** on local CPU and system RAM.

```text
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                                 THE THREE SPATIAL EXECUTION TIERS                                │
├─────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────┤
│ TIER 1: TACTICAL (Hot-Set)      │ TIER 2: IN-TRANSIT (Splines)   │ TIER 3: COLD ("The Void")     │
├─────────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────┤
│ • Collocated in active room     │ • Traveling between hubs       │ • Unobserved dormant sites    │
│ • Doubled axial hexes (4q+2r,6r)│ • Analytical spline: p(t)      │ • 0 background threads        │
│ • goblin-spatial raycasts & cdB │ • 0 CPU pathfinding checks     │ • Process zero ticks (0 CPU)  │
└─────────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. The Monotonic Time Authority

* **Absolute Wall-Clock Independence:** Simulation kernels are strictly forbidden from reading system clocks (`Date.now()`, `performance.now()`).


* **Discrete Logical Ingestion:** Time advances exclusively through externally injected, totally ordered events: $\text{TICK\_ADVANCED}(n)$.


* **Centisecond Quantization:** The simulation grid operates on integer centiseconds ($10\text{ ms}$ resolution, where $100\text{ ticks} = 1.0\text{ second}$). All entities possess a monotonic integer field `busyUntil: Instant` to ensure deterministic execution ordering.



#### 2. Passive Accumulators and Spatial Sweeps

While the machine ticks forward autonomously, entities in the tactical hot-set ($N_{\text{hot}} \le 12$) accumulate physical and interpersonal friction governed by integer basis points ($0 \text{ to } 10{,}000\text{ bp}$):

* **Metabolic Strain & Fatigue:** Accumulates based on locomotion state (e.g., `LIMPING`, `CRAWLING`).


* **Paranoia Clocks:** Increments when environmental acoustic transients exceed the room's ambient damping threshold ($cdB$).


* **Social Debt Strings:** Interpersonal resentment registers silently tick upward across active dyads within the combinatorial incidence mesh ($\binom{N}{2} = \frac{N(N-1)}{2}$).



#### 3. The Tipping Point (The Boil-Over Trigger)

The autonomous tick loop halts when the **Ensemble Eligibility Sweep** detects that friction has crossed systemic bounds:

1. **Axial Proximity Invariant:** `goblin-spatial` confirms that two actors occupy adjacent or collocated axial coordinates ($d_{\text{axial}} \le 1$).


2. **Resentment Ceiling Breach:** The dyad’s active `GrievanceRecord` reaches its maximum escalation ceiling ($I_{\text{curr}} \ge I_{\max}$).


3. **Procedural Boredom Filter:** Rolling Jaccard similarity across recent scene pairings verifies that $J_{\text{boredom}} \le 0.65$, confirming this cast pairing has not saturated recent episodes.



When these conditions are met, the **Clock Brake** trips.

### Step 2: Tripping the Clock Brake & Tabletop Pre-Resolution of Physical Reality

The moment the brake engages, the simulation loop halts, world time freezes at Tick $t$, and the engine enforces the **Constitutional Axiom**:

$$\mathbf{\text{Constitutional Axiom:}}\quad \text{Simulation determines reality; prose merely dramatizes the outcome.}[cite: 3, 4]$$

├──────────────────────┬──────────────────────┬──────────────────────────────────────────┤

#### 1. Mechanical Arbitration (Stage 4)

Before a single word of script is generated, the conflict's outcome is resolved through opposed $2\text{d}6$ dice mechanics modified by character attributes, directional social leverage (`Strings`), and somatic trauma.

* The discrete outcome margin ($\Delta_{\text{margin}}$) determines physical reality: who wins the item, who retreats past the boundary seam, and what biological trauma is sustained.


* The outcome is deterministic and reproducible because it is driven by an explicitly recorded random seed ($\sigma_n$).

The engine stamps this mechanical resolution into an immutable intermediate representation (`ScenePlan IR`):

With the `ScenePlan IR` sealed, the pipeline mounts the authoring environment (`writer.astro` or local `$EDITOR` process suspension). The screenplay (.fountain) is authored strictly as a **constrained linguistic reflection of the pre-resolved physical state**.

#### 1. The Spatial Stage Header & Ignorance by Omission

The engine injects a metadata header directly into Line 0 of the `.fountain` file, outlining physical coordinates, lighting ($lux$), acoustic floor ($cdB$), locked doors, somatic capability masks, and the pre-resolved duel outcome.

* **The Epistemic Horizon:** World secrets and unobserved facts are physically purged from the authoring context (Ignorance by Omission). Under the **Trie Clue-Disjointness Theorem**, permitted physical clues cannot intersect with forbidden mystery trigger lemmas ($P_{\text{clues}} \cap \bigcup t \in T = \emptyset$).



#### 2. The Compile-Time Invariant Gauntlet ($T_0 = \$0.00$)

Candidate text is compiled into a typed **Dialogue Abstract Syntax Tree (`dialogue_ast.json`)** structured into Dwight Swain Motivation-Reaction Units (MRUs). Before any audio synthesis or video rendering occurs, the AST is evaluated by compile-time linters:

* **Somatic SAT Solver:** Confirms hand loads do not exceed physical capacity:



$$\sum_{s \in \text{held\_items}} s.\text{occupies\_hands} \le \text{character.available\_arms}[cite: 4, 20, 24]$$


* **Motor Verb Intersection:** Executes an instantaneous $O(1)$ set-intersection check against the actor's canonical `banned_kinetic_verbs` mask:



$$\text{extracted\_motor\_actions} \cap \text{banned\_kinetic\_verbs} = \emptyset[cite: 10]$$



If a one-armed character is scripted performing a `bimanual_lift` or an amputee performs a `vault`, the compiler fails closed immediately.


* **Zero-Dollar Principle:** Catching contradictions in RAM before dispatching to neural vocoders or GPUs costs **exactly $\$0.00$**.



#### 3. The Anti-Patch Review Gate (`SceneReviewGate.tsx`)

Human showrunners cannot directly edit screenplay text in a text editor (The Forbidden Patch Loop). Direct line-editing severs the dialogue from the causal event ledger.

* If a line is unsatisfactory, the reviewer flags an error code from a closed taxonomy (`UNEARNED_RELEASE`, `CADENCE_COLLAPSE`) or adjusts dramatic dials (Curtness, Threat, Subtext Suppression).


* The engine regenerates only the **Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU)** AST slice ($\le 40\%$ scene span) while keeping the causal ledger and script in complete alignment.

### Step 4: Acoustic Quantization & Sound as the Physical Arbiter of Time

A screenplay has **zero inherent temporal duration**. Language models and human writers cannot accurately guess physical pacing. In this architecture, **physical time is derived empirically from synthesized acoustic waveforms**.

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        THE ACOUSTIC TIMING BRIDGE                                      │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. LOCAL TTS SYNTHESIS (Port 8000)                                                     │
│    • Ingests dialogue text & modulates by character Somatic Fatigue bps (Π_eff)        │
│    • Emits 48 kHz / 24-bit linear PCM uncompressed WAV stems                           │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. MICROSECOND PROBING (ffprobe)                                                       │
│    • ffprobe -show_entries format=duration extracts continuous D_ms                   │
│    • Observation (Physical Reality) != Derivation (Presentation Grid)                  │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. DUAL-DOMAIN QUANTIZATION                                                            │
│    • Video Frame Clock (24 fps): target_frames = ceil( (D_ms / 1000) * 24 )            │
│    • 2,000-Sample Constant: S = F * (48,000 / 24) = F * 2,000 samples/frame            │
│    • Zero-Drift Cumulative Tiling: End_k + 1 == Start_{k+1} (Drift < 41.7 ms / episode)│
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 4. SIMULATION TICK DERIVATION                                                          │
│    • Total Scene Duration: D_total_ms = sum(D_audio) + sum(D_foley) + sum(D_MRU_pauses)│
│    • Consumed Simulation Ticks: Δ_ticks = ceil( D_total_ms / 10 )                      │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Biological Modulation of Acoustic Duration

Character bodies dictate the speed of speech. The conductor inspects the character’s somatic fatigue on the basis-point ledger ($0 \text{ to } 10{,}000\text{ bp}$):


$$\Pi_{\text{eff}} = \Pi_{\text{base}} \times \left(1.0 + \frac{\text{FATIGUE}_{\text{bps}}}{10{,}000} \times \kappa_{\text{exhaust}}\right)[cite: 26]$$


An exhausted or traumatized actor’s neural speech model injects labored inhalations, vocal fry, and extended inter-clause pauses, expanding the waveform’s physical length by up to $+40\%$ on disk.

#### 2. Probing and Timeline Tiling

1. **Empirical Measurement:** Native `ffprobe` probes the resulting uncompressed $48\text{ kHz}$ `.wav` files, extracting continuous duration ($D_{\text{ms}}$) to the microsecond.


2. **The 2,000-Sample Constant:** With $48{,}000\text{ samples/sec}$ and $24\text{ frames/sec}$, every video frame corresponds to exactly $2{,}000\text{ audio samples}$ ($S = F \times 2{,}000$).


3. **Cumulative Millisecond Tiling:** To avoid the "Concatenation Trap" (where rounding up individual audio files drifts multi-scene animatics by seconds), shot start/end frames are calculated from a single cumulative sum quantized once:



$$\text{Start}_k = \left\lceil \frac{\sum_{i<k} D_i}{1000} \times 24 \right\rceil, \quad \text{End}_k = \left\lceil \frac{\sum_{i \le k} D_i}{1000} \times 24 \right\rceil - 1[cite: 27]$$



Because $\text{End}_k + 1 \equiv \text{Start}_{k+1}$, consecutive shots tile the timeline with zero gaps, bounding total episodic timing drift to $< 41.67\text{ ms}$ (less than one video frame).



#### 3. Calculating Consumed Simulation Ticks ($\Delta_{\text{ticks}}$)

The total duration of all dialogue stems, ambient `[BEAT]` holds, and Swain MRU reaction cues is summed and quantized to the $10\text{ ms}$ simulation grid:


$$\Delta_{\text{ticks}} = \left\lceil \frac{\sum D_{\text{ms}}}{10} \right\rceil[cite: 3, 4]$$


(Example: A scene measuring $114.0\text{ seconds}$ consumes exactly $2{,}736\text{ video frames}$ and advances the world by $\mathbf{11{,}400\text{ simulation ticks}}$).

### Step 5: Ledger Sealing, Cache Invalidation, Brake Release, and Epoch Resumption

In the terminal phase of the cycle, the pre-resolved physical mutations and the empirically measured temporal duration are committed to permanent historical storage.

```text

 │ 1. ATOMIC TRANSACTION COMMIT (Cloudflare D1 / SQLite)                  │

 │    • Flips dynamic portal occluders (hatch_valve_04 = LOCKED)          │
 │    • Advances grievance version (v1 -> v2) & sets cooldowns            │

                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 2. REACTIVE BUILD GRAPH INVALIDATION                                   │
 │    • Trigger propagate_fact_mutation sweeps scene_fact_dependencies    │
 │    • Sets status = 'dirty', is_dirty = 1 on stale future scenes        │
 │    • EdgeGateway blocks GPU render dispatch via HTTP 412 ($0.00 spend) │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 3. CRYPTOGRAPHIC MERKLE STATE SEAL                                    │
 │    • H_{t+1} = SHA-256( H_t || EventData || D_total_ms || Δ_ticks )    │
 │    • Duration baked into event log for bit-exact historical replay     │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 4. BRAKE RELEASE & DORMANT SITE JIT CATCH-UP                           │
 │    • Master simulation clock advances: t_resumed = t_pause + Δ_ticks   │
 │    • "The Void" wakes: State_wake = State_sleep + (DecayRate * Δ_ticks)│
 │    • Simulation resumes autonomous clockwork ticking into next epoch   │
 └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Atomic Transaction & State Reduction

The state transition executes as an atomic multi-table transaction against SQLite / Cloudflare D1 (`CANON_DB`):

* Somatic trauma and fatigue deltas are written to character vectors.


* Possessions transfer between entity inventory slots under `EquipSlotSchema`.


* Spatial occluders (doors, valves) update their passage and acoustic damping states.


* The grievance intensity resets to 0, enters its cooldown window, and increments its integer `version` (`NEW.version > OLD.version`).



#### 2. Reactive Invalidation ($O(1)$ Build Graph Sweeps)

The update to the fact's version integer immediately fires the automated database trigger `propagate_fact_mutation`:


$$\text{UPDATE scenes SET status = 'dirty', is\_dirty = 1 WHERE pinned\_version < NEW.version}[cite: 2, 3]$$


Any pre-drafted future scene in the multi-season catalog that relied on the old state is instantly stripped of production certification. The edge gateway intercepts attempts to render these scenes, returning `HTTP 412 (DIRTY_SCENE_REJECTED)` in $< 5\text{ ms}$, ensuring zero GPU rendering dollars are wasted on outdated continuity.

#### 3. Cryptographic State Hash Seal

Because neural TTS synthesis can exhibit microscopic duration jitter across environments, the measured duration ($D_{\text{ms}}$) and frame count are committed directly into the event envelope:


$$H_{t+1} = \text{SHA-256}\Big(H_t \parallel \text{EventData} \parallel D_{\text{total\_ms}} \parallel \Delta_{\text{ticks}}\Big)[cite: 3, 4]$$


On future replays, the system does not re-synthesize audio; it reads the recorded duration, preserving bit-exact historical determinism across years of episode production.

#### 4. Releasing the Brake and JIT Catch-Up ("The Void")

1. **Advancing the World Clock:**

$$t_{\text{resumed}} = t_{\text{paused}} + \Delta_{\text{ticks}}[cite: 3, 4]$$


2. **Closed-Form Algebraic Catch-Up:** Unobserved sites in "The Void" processed zero ticks while the scene occurred to conserve CPU. When an actor eventually re-enters a dormant room, the engine applies closed-form integration on wake:



$$\text{State}_{\text{wake}} = \text{State}_{\text{sleep}} + \left(\text{DecayRate} \times \Delta_{\text{ticks}}\right)[cite: 3, 4]$$



Cooling pipes lose heat, ambient rust accumulates, and distant travelers progress along their spline trajectories by exactly $\Delta_{\text{ticks}}$.


3. **Resumption:** The clockwork machine re-engages its autonomous scheduler loop, advancing ticks forward (`TICK_ADVANCED(t_resumed + 1)`) until the next dramatic boil-over trips the brake.

 ### I. Executive Synthesis: The Tripartite Studio Topology

To produce serialized, multi-season narrative media across a decade without suffering either **Epistemological Calcification** (where rigid rules choke artistic surprise) or **The Hallucinatory Drift Spiral** (where generative models contradict established canon), the Camp Candor Cauldron architecture partitions studio operations into three structural pillars mediated by **`000.repo-bot`**:

```text
========================================================================================================================
                                       THE TRIPARTITE ARCHITECTURAL STUDIO TOPOLOGY
========================================================================================================================
   COLUMN 1: THE LEGISLATIVE CORE           COLUMN 2: THE SOVEREIGN LEDGER           COLUMN 3: CREATIVE DISCOVERY
     (01.canon / 000.goblin-lore)           (02.simulation / series-sower)             (00.discovery / Step 0 / ChatOps)
──────────────────────────────────────   ──────────────────────────────────────   ──────────────────────────────────────
• Axiomatic, immutable physical law      • Append-only historical event stream    • Asynchronous, conversational intake
• Byte-0 Markdown dossiers               • Continuous clockwork world simulation  • Slack channels (#creative, #writing)
• Offline 3-Pass Zod Compiler            • Opposed 2d6 Tabletop Duel Engine       • Quad-Model Divergence Fan-Out
• Cryptographic seal: H_root             • Physical Oracle: goblin-spatial        • Permissive capture ("Accept All")
• Mandate: "What CAN exist"              • Acoustic Clock (ffprobe on TTS stems)  • Unregistered Soft-Linking
                                         • Mandate: "What DID happen"             • Mandate: "What MIGHT be interesting"
========================================================================================================================
                                                           ▲
                                                           │
                                ┌──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┐
                                │             000.repo-bot (THE AIRLOCK)              │
                                │ • Edge-native control plane (Cloudflare Worker/D1)  │
                                │ • Enforces Zero Legislative Authority over chats   │
                                │ • The 4-Tier Creative Ingestion Funnel              │
                                │ • Autonomous Git PR Mechanic & Jules VM Dispatch    │
                                └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



1. **Column 1: Lore as Compiled Code (`000.goblin-lore`):** The offline, compile-time legislative authority. It treats story bibles as strictly typed Zod contracts, anatomical capability masks, and relational foreign keys compiled via `scripts/compile.ts` into an immutable, Merkle-sealed state hash ($H_{\text{root}}$). It operates at $T_0 = \$0.00$ to ensure impossible physical states never reach downstream compute.


2. **Column 2: Screenplays as an Event Ledger of Continuous Simulation (`000.series-sower-engine` + `000.goblin-spatial`):** The deterministic runtime and historical state reducer ($S_{n+1} = \mathcal{R}(S_n, E_n, \sigma_n)$). `000.series-sower-engine` advances world ticks, trips the clock brake when tension boils over, resolves physical confrontations via opposed $2\text{d}6$ dice mechanics, and measures physical time from synthesized speech waveforms ($D_{\text{ms}}$). It delegates all geometric pathfinding, line-of-sight (LoS), and acoustic decibel ($cdB$) propagation to **`000.goblin-spatial`**, a stateless mathematical oracle operating over a doubled integer axial hex lattice.


3. **Column 3: Creative Discovery (`00.discovery`):** The human-in-the-loop, probabilistic ideation sandbox. Operating across Slack channels (`#creative`, `#writing`, `#research-*`, `#ask-jules`) and multi-model exploration harnesses, this column enables artists, writers, and showrunners to brainstorm unconstrained concepts, test psychological friction, probe blind spots, and draft prospective scenes without bureaucratic data-entry overhead.



The architectural link that coordinates these three pillars is **`000.repo-bot`**. It acts as an edge-native control plane and security firewall, translating unstructured conversational ideation into deterministic compile-time canon and executable simulation events without allowing hallucinations or unvetted text to corrupt the production pipeline.

---

### II. The Core Law of Authority: "Zero Legislative Authority"

The fundamental architectural principle governing Column 3 is the **Zero Legislative Authority Mandate**:

$$\mathbf{\text{Zero Legislative Authority:}}\quad \text{Slack Drops } \cap \text{ Production Authority} = \emptyset \text{[cite: 3, 7]}$$

```text
❌ WHAT CANNOT HAPPEN (Bypassing the Airlock):
Writer in #creative: "Bog grabs the iron wrench with both hands and smashes the culvert valve."
        │
        ├──► WRONG: Directly writing to 000.goblin-lore/characters/bog.md (Violates Git Truth & T_0)
        └──► WRONG: Mutating 000.series-sower-engine D1 ledger (Bypasses Opposed 2d6 Tabletop Duel)

──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

✅ WHAT ACTUALLY HAPPENS (Routed through 000.repo-bot):
Writer in #creative: "Bog grabs the iron wrench with both hands and smashes the culvert valve."
        │
        ▼
000.repo-bot intercepts payload -> Mirrors text to vault/ingest/ with Byte-0 status: "raw"
        │
        ▼
Pass 1 Somatic Linter checks Bog's compiled Somatic Vector:
available_arms: 1.0 | banned_kinetic_verbs: ["bimanual_lift", "vault"]
        │
        ▼
000.repo-bot threads a diagnostic reply in Slack:
">> [SOMATIC INVARIANT CONFLICT]: Bog has available_arms = 1.0. 
>> Action 'both hands' violates physical canon. 
>> To proceed: Pitch a mechanical prosthetic via !PitchPossession OR use 1 arm."

```



* **Permissive Capture, Rigid Gating:** Creative ideation in Slack is intentionally permissive, unvalidated, and disposable. Writers can post wild, contradictory, or physically impossible concepts without throwing fatal syntax errors.


* **The Sovereign Artifact Seam:** Raw creative drops possess **zero authority** over production. They cannot mutate a character's state, transfer inventory, alter room occluders, or dispatch GPU renders.


* **The "Assistant Director" Model:** `000.repo-bot` enforces rigid schemas with `additionalProperties: false`. It acts strictly as a logistics coordinator and git mechanic; it lacks the API vocabulary to pass subjective, ungrounded artistic whims into the database.



---

### III. The 4-Tier Creative Ingestion Funnel

To convert unstructured Slack conversations into testable simulation code without imposing database accounting on writers, `000.repo-bot` routes incoming data through the **4-Tier Creative Ingestion Funnel**:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   THE 4-TIER INGESTION FUNNEL DASHBOARD                                │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ TIER 1: MULTI-MODAL DROP SINKS (Slack)                                                 │
│ • #research-wallpaper: Architectural decay, peeling lath/plaster, pipe rust (.png)    │
│ • #research-color:     Lighting moods, sulfur-vat chemical stains (.mp4, hex codes)    │
│ • #creative / #writing: Rough dialogue quips, insults, petty grudges, voice memos      │
│ • #ask-jules:          Dispatch bridge to trigger Jules coding agent tasks             │
├───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                   ▼ POST /api/staging/ingest (000.repo-bot HMAC)       │
│ TIER 2: NORMALIZED STAGING VAULT                                                       │
│ • Content-Addressed Storage (CAS): Binary media mirrored to vault/media/ via SHA-256   │
│ • Obsidian Staging Vault: Flat Markdown in vault/ingest/*.md with status: "raw"        │
│ • The Heuristic Soft Linker: Tags [[char_bog]] without enforcing Foreign-Key Closure   │
├───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                   ▼ Weekly Cron (0 0 * * 1) / !ResearchDigest <topic>  │
│ TIER 3: THEMATIC CLUSTERING & CANONICAL AUDITING                                       │
│ • Joint Multimodal Embedding: V_joint = α·V_text (1536-d) ⊕ β·V_vis (768-d)            │
│ • HDBSCAN Density Clustering: Groups scattered notes into recurring dramatic motifs    │
│ • Pre-Flight Audits: Screened against unresolved.json & Jaccard boredom (J <= 0.65)    │
├───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                   ▼ !PitchGrievance <Entity A> vs <Entity B> <Premise> │
│ TIER 4: THE FORMAL PROPOSAL GATE                                                       │
│ • Compiles clusters into schema-bound proposals: [I_min, I_max], cooldowns, farce caps │
│ • Routes to SceneReviewGate.tsx for human Showrunner ratification                      │
│ • 000.repo-bot commits Git PR to 000.goblin-lore & registers grievance in Sower        │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### Tier 1: Low-Friction Intake Sinks

Writers and artists interact in dedicated Slack channels without formatting constraints:

* `#research-wallpaper`: Material textures, joist rot, crawlspace debris, 3D prop meshes (`.glb`, `.gltf`).


* `#research-color`: Palette studies, ambient lighting, shadow falloff videos (`.mp4`, `.mov`).


* `#creative` / `#writing`: Spontaneous dialogue fragments, behavioral tics, unformatted arguments, voice memos (`.wav`, `.m4a`).


* `#ask-jules` (Channel ID: `C0C4M8K7LV8`): Direct operational bridge where operators commission the Jules coding agent to run refactors or test harnesses.



#### Tier 2: Normalized Persistence & The Heuristic Soft Linker

When a file or text string is posted in Slack, a signed webhook hits `000.repo-bot` (`POST /api/staging/ingest`):

1. **HMAC Signature & Idempotency:** `000.repo-bot` verifies the request signature and asserts the idempotency key to prevent duplicate ingestions.


2. **Content-Addressed Mirroring:** Binaries (images, video, audio) are streamed to Content-Addressed Storage (`vault/media/YYYY/MM/*.*`) keyed by their SHA-256 hash, eliminating broken links.


3. **The Obsidian Staging Vault:** Raw text is saved as a Markdown note (`vault/ingest/YYYY/MM/ingest_YYYYMMDD_XXX.md`) with Byte-0 YAML frontmatter (`status: "raw"`).


4. **The Soft Linker:** In `000.goblin-lore`, a dangling foreign key causes an immediate compilation crash (`Hard Fail`). In Tier 2, the **Heuristic Soft Linker** scans text for entity names and wraps them in associative wikilinks (`[[char_uncle_tallow]]`) **without verifying database closure**. Artists can freely brainstorm prospective characters, locations, or props that do not yet exist in canon.



#### Tier 3: Thematic Clustering (HDBSCAN) & Auditing Against Lore

To prevent the staging vault from rotting into an unsearchable "Dumpster Vault", `000.repo-bot` runs automated batch synthesis via weekly cron (`0 0 * * 1`) or on-demand ChatOps commands (`!ResearchDigest <topic>`):

* **Joint Multimodal Metric Space:** Text is embedded via `text-embedding-3-small` ($1{,}536\text{-d}$), and visual reference art is embedded via DINOv2 / SigLIP ($768\text{-d}$):



$$V_{\text{joint}} = \alpha \cdot V_{\text{text}} \oplus \beta \cdot V_{\text{vis}} \text{[cite: 3, 20, 26, 47]}$$


* **HDBSCAN Density Clustering:** The algorithm clusters disparate chat drops into recurring visual and thematic motifs (e.g., discovering that artists posted 8 separate notes about leaking hydraulic fluid and copper pipes).


* **Auditing Against Canon (Columns 1 & 2):**
1. *Epistemic Mystery Disjointness:* The cluster is cross-referenced against `000.goblin-lore/ontology/unresolved.json`. Under the **Trie Clue-Disjointness Theorem**, if the brainstormed ideas contain forbidden lemmas ($T$) that would prematurely resolve a world mystery (e.g., the cause of the Great Famine), an epistemic warning flags the cluster.


2. *Boredom & Repetition Sweep:* The cluster is checked against `000.series-sower-engine`'s event log using rolling Jaccard similarity ($J \le 0.65$) to ensure the writers are not pitching arguments already resolved in recent episodes.





#### Tier 4: The Formal Proposal Gate (`!PitchGrievance`)

When a creative cluster proves dramatic, a writer executes `!PitchGrievance` in Slack:

```text
!PitchGrievance Bog vs Wart "Wart suspects Bog hoarded dried tallow during the winter frost"

```



1. `000.repo-bot` routes the pitch to an LLM proxy loaded with character voice rules and tonal farce caps.


2. The proxy compiles the pitch into an **Authorized Crossing Primitive**: a dual-rail Grievance DAG proposal specifying primary participants, intensity bounds ($I_{\min}, I_{\max}$), cycle cooldowns, and Argument Score deltas.


3. The proposal is posted back to Slack as a threaded preview card with reaction controls (🔥 / 🤔 / 💀).


4. Upon human Showrunner ratification, `000.repo-bot` branches the repository, commits the file to `000.goblin-lore/grievances/`, opens a formal Pull Request, and registers the grievance in `000.series-sower-engine`.
