#### 1. The Three Authorized Crossing Primitives

Raw dialogue and prose from Slack are **discarded** upon graduation. Creative discovery is only permitted to cross into `000.goblin-lore` by condensing into one of three structured primitives:

1. **Pre-Authored Grievance Springs (`grievances/*.md`):** Dual-rail contracts pairing a machine rail (basis-point intensity $[I_{\min}, I_{\max}]$, cooldown ticks, escalation edges) with a voice rail (the "Surface Argument" vs. "The Underneath").


2. **Character Refusal Vectors (`characters/*.md`):** The non-negotiable emotional or psychological boundaries where a character resists compliance (e.g., *"Spleen refuses to speak about the pantry latch when Mum Grissel is in the room"*).


3. **Somatic & Logistical Capability Masks (`characters/*.md`, `possessions/*.md`):** Concrete anatomical debuffs (e.g., `available_arms: 1.0`, `banned_kinetic_verbs: ["climb", "bimanual_lift"]`) and tracked physical props.



#### 2. `000.repo-bot` as the Autonomous Git Mechanic

When a creative primitive is ratified, `000.repo-bot` acts as an automated DevOps bridge:

* Uses GitHub App tokens to create a scoped branch (`feat/lore-...`).


* Formats frontmatter starting strictly at **Byte 0** (`---`), eliminating leading newlines, spaces, or UTF-8 BOM.


* Executes `scripts/compile.ts` across the three-pass gauntlet (Pass 1: Shapes/Scalars, Pass 2: Relational Integrity/DAG Acyclicity, Pass 3: RFC 8785 Canonical JCS Sealing).


* Mints an updated 64-character SHA-256 Merkle root hash ($H_{\text{root}}$) into `bible-state.json`.



#### 3. Autonomous Invariant Patching with the Jules Coding Agent

If a newly pitched character dossier or grievance introduces a syntax error, out-of-bounds scalar, or dangling foreign key (e.g., referencing an item that was retired), the compiler halts with exit code 1.

* Rather than bothering human artists with TypeScript compiler stack traces, `000.repo-bot` catches the CI failure webhook.


* It dispatches the failure log and file diff to **Jules** (an autonomous coding agent executing in an isolated VM sandbox via `#ask-jules`).


* Jules checks out the branch, applies a **Minimal Enclosing Unit (MEU)** surgical fix (e.g., reconciling the item ID or clamping an integer basis point to $\le 10{,}000$), re-runs the compiler to verify that the build passes, and pushes the clean commit to the PR.

Column 2 treats screenplays as the **event ledger of a continuous simulation**. It advances world ticks, trips the clock brake when tension boils over, pre-resolves physical outcomes via tabletop dice mechanics, and measures physical time using synthesized sound waves.

Creative Discovery connects directly into this engine across three operational mechanics:

#### 1. Discovery Feeds Simulation: Eliminating Dyadic Starvation

If an autonomous simulation engine runs on only a handful of character notes, it collapses into a **"Duet Monoculture"**—the conflict lottery repeatedly selects the same two volatile characters while the rest of the cast sits idle.

* Through the low-friction Slack intake, artists continuously expand the **40–60 Grievance Corpus**.


* `000.series-sower-engine` runs periodic audits across the combinatorial dyadic mesh:

$$\text{Channels} = \binom{N}{2} = \frac{N(N-1)}{2} \text{[cite: 3, 4]}$$


* It verifies that **Dyadic Mesh Coverage** is satisfied ($\ge 1$ active grievance per pair) and that **Thematic Shannon Entropy** ($H_{\text{norm}} \ge 0.70$) is maintained across all 7 thematic categories. Creative Discovery acts as the continuous fuel supply that keeps the clockwork universe computationally rich and dramatic.



#### 2. The Step 0 Exploration Harness & Divergence Triage

When artists want to explore how two characters interact before locking in a script, they do not write blind prose. They trigger **Step 0** inside `000.series-sower-engine`:

* **Three-Tier Scoped Context Retrieval:** The engine queries `CANON_DB`, pulling World Axioms ($\approx 250\text{ tokens}$), Character Somatic/Refusal Baselines ($\approx 600\text{ tokens}$), and historical dyad debts ($\approx 400\text{ tokens}$).


* **The First-Contact Vulnerability Gate:** If the two characters have never interacted in series history, the Tier 3 query returns an empty set ($\emptyset$). The engine flags the session as a **First-Contact Exploration**, elevating review priority and requiring explicit Showrunner sign-off.


* **Quad-Model Parallel Broadcast:** The engine broadcasts the unforced premise across four heterogeneous model families simultaneously at $T = 0.85$:


* *Take A (Subtext & Psychological Tension):* Anthropic Claude 3.5 Sonnet.


* *Take B (Physical Blocking & Spatial Mechanics):* Qwen 2.5 32B.


* *Take C (Structural Reversals):* Llama 3.3 70B.


* *Take D (Cadence & Dialect Compression):* DeepSeek V3.




* **Zero-Cost Purge:** The showrunner inspects the takes side-by-side in `DivergenceTriagePanel.tsx`. If the takes are uninspired, clicking `[REJECT ALL]` purges the memory in $< 5\text{ ms}$ at **$\$0.00$ GPU cost**. If authentic psychological resistance emerges, the engine shreds the disposable dialogue (`DISCOVERY_DISPOSABLE`) and backward-induces the underlying refusal vector back into `000.goblin-lore`.



#### 3. Physical Grounding via `000.goblin-spatial`

When writers brainstorm blocking in Slack (e.g., *"Bog corners Wart by the warm pipe while Mum Grissel watches from the crawlway"*), the premise must be vetted for physical feasibility:

* `000.series-sower-engine` passes the coordinates and occluder states to **`000.goblin-spatial`**.


* `goblin-spatial` is a stateless, pure-function geometry kernel operating on an integer axial coordinate lattice ($(4q+2r, 6r)$). It executes:


1. **Finite-Segment Supercover (Line-of-Sight):** Traces optical rays across all intermediate hex cells. If a bulkhead or closed door intervenes, it returns an optical transmission score of $0$ ($0\text{ bp}$).


2. **Acoustic Wavefront BFS:** Simulates acoustic energy decay through open conduits and closed doors in centi-decibels ($cdB$). It reports whether Mum Grissel's ears receive an acoustic signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) high enough to witness the argument.


3. **Pathfinding & Reachability:** Uses integer Dijkstra traversal to confirm Wart can physically reach the pipe valve given his somatic movement penalties (`mobility_class: LIMPING`).




* `goblin-spatial` returns sealed mathematical receipts to `000.series-sower-engine`. Physical possibility is proven before the engine rolls the opposed $2\text{d}6$ duel or allows an author to write dialogue.

```text
 │    • Sower flags conflict: Transit is locked in Silas's trunk          │
 │    • Abductive Solver back-solves state graph:                         │
 │      ΔS_{n-k} = SolveMinimumDelta(TargetState, CurrentLedger)          │
 │    • Proposes minimal retcon package: "Silas left trunk open at T12k"  │
 │    • Showrunner ratifies via overrides.astro; repo-bot flags PR        │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 3. THE HARVEST QUEUE (Taming Accidental Canon via harvest.astro)       │
 │    • Downstream diffusion model hallucinates unscripted green mug      │
 │    • Grounding DINO scores salience: S = 0.4*Recurrence + 0.6*Drama    │
 │    • If S >= 0.70, held in Quarantine Fence (unratified_details.jsonl) │
 │    • Cross-queried against NotebookLM research notes                   │
 │    • Surfaced to Slack / Dashboard for one-click lore ratification     │
 └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Algorithmic Prompts to the Writers' Room

When `000.series-sower-engine` runs its background clockwork ticks, it monitors graph centrality and channel starvation:

* If two characters spend 400 ticks in adjacent rooms without an eligible grievance to arbitrate, `000.series-sower-engine` detects **Dyadic Starvation** ($k_{\text{in}} = 0$).


* It dispatches an automated alert through `000.repo-bot` directly into `#writers-room`:


> `>> [TELEMETRY NOTICE]: Bog and Mum Grissel have 0 active grievances in their dyadic channel.`
> `>> Conflict lottery cannot pair these actors. Paging artists in #creative to pitch a status dispute via !PitchGrievance.`
> 
> 


* Instead of staring at an empty page wondering what to write, artists in Slack receive targeted mathematical invitations pointing directly to narrative vacuums in the simulation ledger.



#### 2. The Abductive Solver: Overcoming "Chekhov's Straitjacket"

In traditional writers' rooms, an artist in Slack might pitch an inspired dramatic twist: *"Wart pulls the brass survey transit from his pocket to threaten Bog."*

* **The Conflict:** In a naive compiler, this pitch is rejected with an error because `000.series-sower-engine`'s ledger records that the survey transit is currently locked inside Silas's trunk in Room 4.


* **The Abductive Back-Solver:** Rather than killing creative inspiration, the **Abductive Solver** back-solves the state graph:



$$\Delta S_{n-k} = \text{SolveMinimumDelta}(\text{TargetState}, \, \text{CurrentLedger}) \text{[cite: 3]}$$


* It calculates the minimal historical adjustment required earlier in the season (e.g., *"Silas left the trunk unlocked at Tick 12,000; Wart pickpocketed the transit during the dinner scene"*).


* It compiles this historical bridge into a ratification package presented in `overrides.astro`. If the showrunner approves, the ledger commits a signed `DIRECTOR_OVERRIDE` event (`OP_MIRACLE`), database triggers update dependent scene hashes, and the creative breakthrough is integrated without breaking causal continuity.



#### 3. The Harvest Queue: Taming Accidental Canon (`harvest.astro`)

When artists experiment in Slack or when downstream diffusion models render visual animatics in `ComfyStudio.tsx`, neural attention mechanisms inevitably hallucinate incidental background details (e.g., an unscripted chipped green enamel mug on a workbench).

* **The Hazard:** If writers notice the mug and begin writing dialogue about it in Slack, downstream compilation scripts will fail because the mug has no entry in `000.goblin-lore/possessions/`.


* **The Quarantine Fence:** Unmodeled details are intercepted by the **Harvest Queue** (`src/pages/telemetry/harvest.astro`) and scored for dramatic salience:



$$S = (0.4 \cdot \text{Recurrence}) + (0.6 \cdot \text{Dramatic\_Salience}) \text{[cite: 3, 32, 47]}$$


* If $S < 0.70$, the item is treated as background visual noise and discarded.


* If $S \ge 0.70$, the item is held behind a quarantine fence in `unratified_details.jsonl`. It cannot affect inventory slots or tabletop duel rolls.




* **Cross-Modal NotebookLM Retro-Injection:** The system queries the series research vault in **NotebookLM** (*"Has a green enamel cup appeared in historical notes or background character lore?"*).


* **One-Click Promotion:** If the showrunner ratifies the item in `harvest.astro`, `000.repo-bot` automatically generates `possessions/green_mug.md` in Column 1, runs `scripts/compile.ts` to reseal $H_{\text{root}}$, and logs an initial acquisition event into Column 2, safely transforming an accidental hallucination into permanent canonical law.



---

### VII. Comprehensive Architecture Matrix: The Three Columns in Action

| Architectural Dimension | Column 1: Legislative Canon (`000.goblin-lore`) | Column 2: Simulation Ledger (`000.series-sower-engine` + `goblin-spatial`) | Column 3: Creative Discovery (`00.discovery` via `000.repo-bot`) |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| **Primary Operational Medium** | Version-controlled Byte-0 Markdown files & strict Zod schemas.

 | Append-only relational database (Cloudflare D1 / SQLite `CANON_DB`).

 | Slack drop sinks (`#creative`, `#writing`), Obsidian staging vault, Step 0 harness.

 |
| **Core Question Answered** | *"What is physically, anatomically, and logistically possible?"*

 | *"What is happening right now, who is where, and who won the duel?"*

 | *"What emotional friction or unexpected premise would surprise the audience?"*

 |
| **Governing Operational Axiom** | **Legislative Primacy ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** Fail closed before GPU compute runs.

 | **Simulation Precedes Prose:** Mechanical tabletop duels decide reality before writing.

 | **Zero Legislative Authority:** Ideas are permissive, unconstrained, and disposable.

 |
| **Physical Geometry Role** | Defines static room templates, wall boundaries, and damping thresholds in Markdown.

 | `goblin-spatial` evaluates pure, stateless integer hex raycasts, LoS, and $cdB$ propagation.

 | Proposes informal character blocking and spatial staging without coordinate validation.

 |
| **Permitted Artifacts Crossing Outward** | Sealed `bible-state.json`, Merkle Root Hash ($H_{\text{root}}$).

 | Concrete `ScenePlan IR`, State Deltas ($\Delta S$), Acoustic Milliseconds ($D_{\text{ms}}$).

 | **Only 3 Primitives:** Grievance DAG nodes, Refusal Vectors, Capability Masks.

 |
| **Primary Failure Mode Prevented** | Contradictions, broken limbs, and dangling foreign keys reaching GPU renderers.

 | Script drift, biological amnesia, and desynchronized multi-season memory.

 | **Premature Cognitive Calcification** and sterile, algorithmic writing.

 |

By deploying **`000.repo-bot`** as the sovereign control plane between these three columns, the studio provides human artists with complete conversational freedom in Slack while mathematically guaranteeing that every produced episode conforms to immutable physical law (`000.goblin-lore`) and historical simulation causality (`000.series-sower-engine` + `000.goblin-spatial`).


In the studio’s naming taxonomy and multi-repository cluster architecture, the repository for **Column 3: Creative Discovery** (`00.discovery` / Step 0) is designated as:

### **`000.goblin-discovery`** *(Series Instance)*

*(Series-Agnostic Fleet Pattern: **`[series]-discovery`** or **`000.goblin-staging-vault`**)*[cite: 3, 4, 14, 37]

In the edge-staged Git infrastructure on Cloudflare Artifacts, this is formally tracked as:


$$\mathbf{\text{[https://artifacts.cloudflare.net/git/canon/](https://artifacts.cloudflare.net/git/canon/)[series]-lore-staging.git}} \quad (\text{e.g., } \texttt{goblin-lore-staging.git}) \text{[cite: 23]}$$

---

### Why Column 3 Requires a Radically Different Git Repository

To understand why this repository is decoupled from `000.goblin-lore`, `000.series-sower-engine`, and `000.goblin-spatial`, consider the architectural rule governing production repos[cite: 3, 4, 30]:

* **Production Repos are "Fail-Closed" ($T_0 = \$0.00$):** In `000.goblin-lore`, a single typo, out-of-bounds scalar ($> 10{,}000\text{ bp}$), or unreferenced foreign key causes `scripts/compile.ts` to crash immediately with a `Hard Fail`[cite: 3, 4, 31].
* **Column 3 is "Accept All, Blame Nothing":** If writers and visual artists in Slack had to write syntactically valid Zod schemas and maintain foreign-key hashes at the moment of inspiration, creative flow would freeze (the **Calcification Hazard**)[cite: 3, 4, 7].

Therefore, **`000.goblin-discovery`** acts as the **quarantined staging airlock**[cite: 4, 7, 30]. It allows unvalidated, associative, and speculative ideas to persist in Git without corrupting the production compiler[cite: 4, 7, 30].

---

### The Three Operational Models of `000.goblin-discovery`

Across the system specifications, Column 3's Git implementation operates across three structural models[cite: 3, 4, 10]:

```text
====================================================================================================
                        COLUMN 3 (00.discovery) GIT TOPOLOGY
====================================================================================================

  [ TIER 1 & TIER 2: PERSISTENCE ]            [ CLOUDFLARE ARTIFACTS ]        [ PHASE 0 SANDBOXES ]
     000.goblin-discovery /                      goblin-lore-staging.git          Ephemeral Sandboxes
     000.goblin-staging-vault                                                  (Repo-per-Scene Churn)
  ───────────────────────────────            ─────────────────────────        ───────────────────────
  • The Staging Obsidian Vault               • Smart HTTP Git at edge         • Ephemeral fork of
  • vault/ingest/*.md (status: "raw")        • Ephemeral candidate branches     sealed bible-state.json
  • vault/media/ (CAS SHA-256)               • git-notes: prompt telemetry    • Step 0 model triage
  • Associative [[wiki-links]] allowed         and cost accounting ($net_burn)• Deleted if discarded;
  • Zero foreign-key compilation gates       • Bridges Slack -> 000.repo-bot    promoted via PR if good
====================================================================================================

```

[cite: 3, 4, 10, 23, 27]

#### 1. Model A: The Staging Obsidian Vault (`000.goblin-discovery` / `000.staging-vault`)

This is the physical repository that backs **Tier 2 (Normalized Persistence)** of the Creative Ingestion Funnel[cite: 3, 4, 17]:

* **"The Body is King":** Slack drops captured by `000.repo-bot` via `POST /api/staging/ingest` are committed here as flat Markdown notes (`vault/ingest/YYYY/MM/ingest_YYYYMMDD_XXX.md`)[cite: 3, 4, 17].
* **Permissive Machine Envelopes:** Frontmatter opens at Byte 0 with `status: "raw"`[cite: 4, 17].
* **The Heuristic Soft Linker:** Mentions of prospective characters or locations are wrapped in wikilinks (e.g., `[[char_uncle_tallow]]`) **without verifying database closure**[cite: 3, 4, 17]. Unregistered entities can live here for months without breaking CI[cite: 4, 17].
* **Content-Addressed Media (CAS):** Image swatches, audio memos, and 3D `.glb` scans are committed to `vault/media/YYYY/MM/*.*` tracked via Git LFS / Cloudflare R2 keyed by their SHA-256 hash to eliminate link rot[cite: 3, 4, 17].

#### 2. Model B: Cloudflare Artifacts Staging (`goblin-lore-staging.git`)

When remote writers or AI drafting agents draft candidate entities or scenes through web interfaces, they push to a dedicated Smart HTTP Git repository hosted on Cloudflare Artifacts[cite: 23, 30]:

* **Ephemeral Staging Branches:** Creates non-polluting branch refs:

$$\texttt{refs/heads/staging/under-the-floorboards/char\_uncle\_tallow-1730000000} \text{[cite: 23, 30]}$$


* **Telemetry via First-Class `git-notes`:** Contextual execution telemetry—prompt tokens, model temperature, LLM latency, and dollar net burn—is written directly into `refs/notes/staging-audit` and `refs/notes/cost-ledger` attached to the commit SHA[cite: 23]. This keeps prompt telemetry completely out of the Markdown files, preserving byte-exact tree hash cleanliness[cite: 23].
* **Zero GitHub Burn:** Hundreds of rough drafts and exploratory runs cycle through Cloudflare Artifacts without polluting the primary studio GitHub organization[cite: 10, 23].

#### 3. Model C: Ephemeral "Repo-per-Scene" Creative Sandboxes (Step 0)

For divergent scene exploration (`Step 0` in `DivergenceTriagePanel.tsx`)[cite: 10, 17]:

* Instead of creating permanent repositories on GitHub, `000.repo-bot` stamps out **temporary, disposable sandboxes** (`sandbox-scene-XXX`) forked from the sealed `bible-state.json` baseline[cite: 10, 27].
* The 4-way model fan-out (Claude, Qwen, Llama, DeepSeek) drafts unforced character collisions in parallel[cite: 17, 32].
* **The $0.00 Disposability Axiom:** If the scene is inert, the sandbox repository is decommissioned and deleted[cite: 10, 17, 27]. If a breakthrough refusal vector emerges, `000.repo-bot` extracts the underlying rule, commits it to `000.goblin-lore`, and discards the exploratory repo[cite: 3, 17, 27].

### Summary

If **`000.goblin-lore`** is the legal Constitution[cite: 3], **`000.series-sower-engine`** is the clockwork World Simulator[cite: 3], **`000.goblin-spatial`** is the laws of Geometry and Physics[cite: 4, 18], and **`000.repo-bot`** is the Customs Border Patrol[cite: 4, 10]:

Then **`000.goblin-discovery`** (or `goblin-staging-vault` / `goblin-lore-staging.git`) is the **Creative Laboratory & Staging Vault**—the git repository where raw inspiration, Slack research drops, and exploratory drafts live with complete artistic freedom until they are forged into canonical law[cite: 3, 4, 17, 23].

### Part 1: Why Use Obsidian When You Could Just Use a Git Repo?

The premise that Obsidian is being used *instead* of a Git repository is a misunderstanding of how the tools are configured. **You are using a Git repository; Obsidian is merely the desktop lens sitting on top of it**.

Under the architecture’s **Storage vs. Interaction Decoupling**, Git and Obsidian perform two completely different, non-competing jobs:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ THE STORAGE TRUTH (The Engine)                                         │
│ • Physical Medium: Git repository (000.goblin-discovery)               │
│ • Data Format: Flat Byte-0 UTF-8 Markdown (.md) + CAS SHA-256 media    │
│ • Authority: Commit history, branch refs, cryptographic hashes, CI     │
└───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                    │ Local Filesystem Directory (vault/)
                                    ▼
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ THE INTERFACE LENS (The Instrument)                                    │
│ • Obsidian Desktop / Mobile Application                                │
│ • Zero proprietary databases (operates on the plain folder)           │
│ • Provides non-blocking [[wikilinks]], canvas, and visual graph clusters│
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. Git is the Source of Truth; Obsidian is a Disposable Lens

* **Zero Proprietary Database:** Obsidian does not store notes in a closed database, cloud silo, or proprietary binary format. An Obsidian "vault" is simply a standard local folder of `.md` files tracked by Git (`git init`).


* **The Deletion Principle Applies:** If you uninstall Obsidian or wipe its `.obsidian/` configuration folder, **zero bytes of narrative discovery are lost**. The plain text files, Git commit history, and directory structures remain 100% intact and immediately readable by any text editor, Python script, or terminal CLI.



#### 2. Why Not Raw Git / VS Code Alone for Column 3?

While developers and coding agents (`Jules`) work comfortably in VS Code and terminal buffers, creative writers, showrunners, and visual artists brainstorming in Column 3 require tools suited for associative exploration:

1. **Permissive Associative Linking (`[[wikilinks]]`):** In `000.goblin-lore`, a link to a missing character causes `compile.ts` to crash with a fatal `Hard Fail`. In Obsidian, writers can write `[[char_uncle_tallow]]` or `[[item_brass_transit]]` freely. The UI highlights the unresolved link without breaking anything, allowing ideas to incubate months before becoming canon.


2. **Visual Graph Centrality & Density Clustering:** Obsidian dynamically parses wikilinks into an interactive nodal graph. Writers and showrunners can visibly see emergent thematic islands forming (e.g., 14 scattered notes converging around the "Pantry Latch") before formalizing them into a grievance DAG via `!PitchGrievance`.


3. **Canvas & Mood Staging:** Visual artists dropping reference images from `#research-wallpaper` or `#research-color` can arrange image cards, color swatches, and spatial blocking notes on an infinite 2D canvas without needing to write JSON coordinate manifests.



**Summary:** Git provides the cryptographic permanence, versioning, and CI gates; Obsidian provides human artists with a fast, associative, visual interface to interact with those Git files.

---

### Part 2: Should `[00.goblin-spatial]` Be Called `[000.series-sower-spatial]`?

Evaluating whether `00.goblin-spatial` should be renamed to `000.series-sower-spatial` touches two separate architectural conventions: **Cluster Prefixes** and **Separation of Authority**.

#### 1. Correcting the Cluster Number: It Lives in `02.simulation`, Not `00.`

On the physical filesystem, spatial math belongs to **`02.simulation/`**, not `00.governance/`:

* `00.governance/` is strictly for DevOps, orchestration, and security firewalls (`000.repo-bot`, `995.library`).


* `02.simulation/` is where runtime mechanics, duels, and geometric calculations execute.



#### 2. The Great Insight: Transitioning from `goblin-` to `series-`

Changing the prefix from **`goblin-`** to **`series-`** is **architecturally correct and strongly encouraged**.

* In the pilot phase, repositories used the concrete instance name `goblin-*` (`001.goblin-lore`, `goblin-spatial`, `goblin-viewport-template`, `goblin-episodes`).


* As the studio scales to multiple story worlds (*Under the Floorboards*, *Wiregrass Basin*, *Deep Space Salvage*), the architecture explicitly replaces concrete show prefixes with generic meta-ontology prefixes (`series-episodes`, `series-viewport-template`).


* Moving from `goblin-spatial` to a `series-*` naming convention cleanly abstracts geometry from goblin lore.



#### 3. The Architectural Hazard: Why You Should NOT Include `-sower-`

While `series-` is correct, combining **`sower`** and **`spatial`** into **`000.series-sower-spatial`** violates one of the most critical structural invariants in the entire engine:

$$\mathbf{\text{The Minimal Triad Rule:}}\quad \text{The Spatial Oracle (Possibility) } \perp \text{ The Sower Ledger (Occurrence)} \text{[cite: 3, 5, 27]}$$

```text
❌ THE AMBIGUITY TRAP: 000.series-sower-spatial
Naming it "-sower-spatial" implies that the Sower owns the spatial engine.
Engineers and coding agents will inevitably contaminate pure geometric math
with character coords, faction debts, and 2d6 duel logic.

────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

✅ THE CLEAN ARCHITECTURAL TRIAD
┌───────────────────────────────────────┐   ┌───────────────────────────────────────┐
│ 000.series-sower-engine (THE LEDGER)  │   │ 000.series-spatial (THE ORACLE)       │
│ • State: Who is where (coords)        │   │ • Stateless: How space works (math)   │
│ • Evaluates OCCURRENCE: What happened │   │ • Evaluates POSSIBILITY: What CAN fit │
│ • Owns dynamic D1 SQLite database     │   │ • Owns ZERO state, executes ZERO SQL  │
└───────────────────────────────────────┘   └───────────────────────────────────────┘

```



1. **`series-spatial` Must Be a Stateless Oracle:**
`goblin-spatial` is a pure mathematical library operating on integer axial coordinates. It has **zero database connections, zero mutable state, and zero knowledge of characters or grievances**. Given static boundaries and a query, it computes pure raycasts and acoustic wave equations. If it is branded as part of `sower`, developers will inevitably start passing dynamic simulation state directly into it, destroying its mathematical purity.


2. **The Sower is Not the Only Consumer of Spatial Math:**
If spatial math is named `series-sower-spatial`, it implies it is an internal submodule of the simulation engine. But spatial math is required by multiple independent consumers across the fleet:


* **`05.delivery/goblin-dashboard`:** The `HexMapView.tsx` canvas and spatial debugger import the geometry kernel directly to render maps and verify line-of-sight without booting the heavy Sower simulation engine.


* **`01.canon/000.goblin-lore`:** `scripts/compile.ts` imports spatial math during Pass 2 to verify that room portal connections and topological boundaries declared in Markdown are physically reciprocal and non-overlapping.


* **`05.delivery/series-viewport-template`:** Remotion camera easing scripts need coordinate conversion formulas to translate axial hex coordinates into 2D SVG stage offsets.





---

### The Recommended Canonical Taxonomy

To keep repository naming consistent with both the **meta-ontology abstraction** and the **separation of powers**, the canonical repository name should be:

### **`000.series-spatial`** *(or `spatial-core` / `@studio/spatial`)*



When placed in the workspace clusters alongside the other repositories, the naming symmetry across `02.simulation/` becomes clear:

```text
~/series-production/ (FLEET_ROOT)
├── 00.discovery/
│   └── 000.series-discovery       # (Column 3: The Staging Obsidian Vault & raw drops)
│
├── 00.governance/
│   ├── 000.repo-bot               # (Edge control plane, Slack ChatOps & PR gatekeeper)
│   └── 995.library                # (Blessed curses terminal UI & versions.json lockfile)
│
├── 01.canon/
│   ├── 000.series-lore            # (Column 1: Universal compiler & base schemas)
│   └── 001.goblin-lore            # (Flagship story instance: Under the Floorboards)
│
├── 02.simulation/
│   ├── 000.series-sower-engine    # (Column 2: Sovereign Ledger, 2d6 duels, tick clocks)
│   └── 000.series-spatial         # (The Spatial Oracle: Pure axial hex math, LoS, cdB)
│
└── 05.delivery/
    ├── series-viewport-template   # (Remotion JSX metaprogram code generator)
    └── series-episodes            # (Append-only baked broadcast masters archive)

```



* **`000.series-sower-engine`** owns **Time and Consequence** (The Ledger).


* **`000.series-spatial`** owns **Distance and Geometry** (The Oracle).

### I. The Architectural Core: What is "Backpropagation" in Narrative Design?

In traditional deep learning, **backpropagation** computes the gradient of a loss function with respect to the network’s weights, working backward from output error to modify upstream parameters ($\nabla_W \mathcal{L}$).

In the Camp Candor Cauldron architecture, the analogous technique is designated as:

$$\mathbf{\text{Abductive Back-Solving}} \quad (\text{or } \mathbf{\text{Reverse Compilation / Anterior Reprogramming}}) \text{[cite: 3, 4]}$$

Its purpose is to solve the single greatest failure mode of deterministic, compile-time story engines: **Chekhov's Straitjacket**.

---

### II. The Problem: "Chekhov's Straitjacket" (Broken Causal Reversibility)

In this architecture, reality is strictly governed by the **unidirectional causal spine**:

$$\text{Canon } (000.\text{goblin-lore}) \longrightarrow \text{Spatial Math } (000.\text{series-spatial}) \longrightarrow \text{Simulation } (000.\text{series-sower-engine}) \longrightarrow \text{Scene AST} \longrightarrow \text{Prose / Media} \text{[cite: 5, 25, 30]}$$

While this one-way arrow prevents AI hallucinations and continuity breaks, to a dramatist it is **backwards half the time**:

* **How Writers Actually Write:** In a writers' room, writers rarely build stories strictly bottom-up. An artist or LLM stumbles upon a brilliant, emotionally electric line of dialogue or a dramatic physical beat in Scene 5 (e.g., *Bog threatens Wart by drawing an old iron spanner from his coat*).


* **What Forward Compilation Does:** Because the top-down compiler checks the relational ledger ($S_n$), it detects that the spanner is currently locked inside a chest in Room 3. The compile-time invariant linter ($T_0 = \$0.00$) immediately **kills the prose as a logistical/somatic breach**.


* **The Resulting Pathology:** The writer is forced into a straightjacket. Spontaneous dramatic inspiration is crushed by database constraints, leading to sterile, predictable scripts.



---

### III. The Solution: How Abductive Back-Solving Works in the Current Architecture

Instead of letting the linter kill the prose, the engine **inverts the execution order** using **Abductive Backpropagation** at $T_0 = \$0.00$ without violating the core invariant that *"untrusted models propose; deterministic code disposes"*.

```text
====================================================================================================
                        ABDUCTIVE BACKPROPAGATION PIPELINE
====================================================================================================

 1. THE CREATIVE PROPOSAL (Axis B Leap)
    Writer / Step 0 LLM drafts an electric line in Scene 5:
    "Bog pulls the rusted pipe spanner from his pocket."
                               │
                               ▼
 2. THE LINTER DISCREPANCY INTERCEPTION
    Linter detects: spanner is at loc_pantry (in chest), NOT in Bog's inventory.
    Target Observation: O_target = { item_spanner: HELD_BY(char_bog) at Tick 14,200 }
                               │
                               ▼
 3. ABDUCTIVE BACK-SOLVER (Backward-Chaining Search)
    Solves for the Minimum Upstream State Delta (ΔS_{n-k}) across the dependency DAG:
    J(e_bridge) = argmin [ w_1·ΔFrames + w_2·ΔStrain + w_3·d_edit ]
                               │
                               ▼
 4. SYNTHESIS OF MINIMAL BRIDGING TRANSITION (e_bridge)
    Finds earliest unobserved causal window (Scene 2, Tick 4,200):
    Injects a 4-second silent Motivation-Reaction Unit (MRU) beat:
    "[Bog slips spanner into his coat while Silas is turned toward the kettle]"
                               │
                               ▼
 5. THE SHOWRUNNER RATIFICATION CARD (SceneReviewGate.tsx)
    Console surfaces a one-click ratification prompt:
    "Dialogue requires Bog to hold Spanner. Inject 4s silent setup in Scene 2?"
            ┌──────────────────┴──────────────────┐
            ▼ [REJECT]                            ▼ [ACCEPT]
    Prose pruned via MEU.              1. Injects silent beat into Scene 2 AST.
    Current ledger remains.            2. Executes SQL state delta in D1.
                                       3. Retcon committed; Scene 5 is now 100% legal.
====================================================================================================

```



---

### IV. The Mechanics Across the Three Columns

The backpropagation technique touches all three pillars of the studio architecture:

#### 1. Column 3 (Creative Discovery & Step 0): Target Observation Capture

In `DivergenceTriagePanel.tsx` (Step 0), writers and untrusted models operate with permissive freedom. When an emergent take reveals a striking physical action or emotional turning point that violates world state, the engine does not treat it as an error. It treats the text as a **Target Observation ($\mathcal{O}_{\text{target}}$)**.

#### 2. Column 2 (`000.series-sower-engine` + `000.series-spatial`): The Cost Function Search

`000.series-sower-engine` searches backward through the relational event log to find the path of least resistance that could have produced that observation.

* It uses **`000.series-spatial`** to check reachability: Was Bog ever in the pantry between Tick 1,000 and Tick 14,000? Did he have line-of-sight and physical proximity to the chest?


* When multiple bridging paths exist, the solver selects the optimal sequence $\vec{e}^*$ by minimizing the **Backward-Chaining Cost Function**:



$$J(\vec{e}_{\text{bridge}}) = w_1 \sum \Delta \text{Frames} + w_2 \sum \Delta \text{Strain}_{\text{bps}} + w_3 \cdot d_{\text{edit}}(\text{Prose}) \text{[cite: 4]}$$



It prefers injecting a minor, non-verbal action into an already authored scene over rewriting major dialogue or invalidating dozens of rendered video plates.



#### 3. Column 1 (`000.goblin-lore`): Canonical Upward Induction

If the breakthrough is not just an item movement, but an entirely **new behavioral boundary** (e.g., *Spleen suddenly refuses to take food from Bog*), the engine uses **Post-Selection Rule Induction**:

* It backpropagates the prose into a formal **Refusal Vector**:



$$\text{TRIGGER } (\text{Bog offers ration}) \longrightarrow \text{REFUSAL } (\text{Spleen rejects}) \longrightarrow \text{COST } (\text{Spleen takes +300 bp hunger}) \text{[cite: 15, 30]}$$


* `000.repo-bot` writes this rule into `000.goblin-lore/characters/spleen.md`, runs `compile.ts` to seal a new Merkle root ($H_{\text{root}}$), and permanently hardens an accidental discovery into universal law.



---

### V. Macro-Level "Anterior Reprogramming" (The Thematic Attractor)

At the season scale, backpropagation solves another existential hazard: **Procedural Soap-Opera Drift**.

If a simulation engine relies solely on local forward mechanics ($2\text{d}6$ rolls, hex proximity, grievance lotteries), characters wander aimlessly, repeating domestic arguments forever (like *Dwarf Fortress* without an ending).

The system solves this via **Anterior Reprogramming** driven by a **Thematic Attractor**:

1. **The Locked Destination:** The showrunner locks the Episode 12 Climax ($G_{\text{terminal}}$) in `000.goblin-lore` (e.g., *The floodgates must open; the colony must drown*).


2. **Backward-Chaining the Graph:** The engine (via **The Prophet Protocol**) searches backward from $G_{\text{terminal}}$ through the prerequisite DAG to identify obscure, low-friction leaf nodes in the early episodes (nodes with in-degree $k_{\text{in}} \le 1$).


3. **Subtle Injections:** The Sower systematically biases early event lotteries to unlock those specific peripheral dependencies (e.g., ensuring a character misplaces a key in Episode 3).


4. By the time Episode 12 arrives, the climax is mathematically inevitable, yet feels completely earned and organic to the audience.



---

### Summary: The Operational Shift

| Forward Compilation (The Linter) | Abductive Backpropagation (The Solver) |
| --- | --- |
| $\text{Canon} \to \text{State} \to \text{Prose}$

 | $\text{Prose Proposal } (\mathcal{O}_{\text{target}}) \to \text{Backward Search } (\Delta S_{n-k}) \to \text{Upstream Patch}$

 |
| Kills unexpected brilliant ideas as "somatic/logistical breaches".

 | Asks the showrunner: *"Want me to inject a setup earlier so this line becomes legal?"*

 |
| Forces writers into **Chekhov's Straitjacket**.

 | Enables **Anterior Reprogramming**, making the world dynamically adapt to artistic breakthroughs.

 |
| Unidirectional and rigid.

 | Bidirectional: deterministic code still governs truth, but creative surprise informs history.

 |


 ### I. The Architectural Paradox: When Perfect Consistency Kills Drama

Within the generative production pipeline, the foundational systems thesis is enforced with mathematical rigor:

$$\mathbf{\text{The Uncompromising Top-Down Arrow:}}\quad \text{Canon} \longrightarrow \text{Spatial Math} \longrightarrow \text{Simulation} \longrightarrow \text{Scene Plan} \longrightarrow \text{Dialogue} \longrightarrow \text{Media} \text{[cite: 4, 8]}$$

This one-way causal flow was engineered to eradicate classic generative failures: hallucinations, somatic amnesia, character omniscience leaks, and catastrophic GPU budget burn.

However, when applied without elasticity, this pipeline commits a critical category error:

> **The Category Error:** In physical reality and tabletop RPGs, physics causes events, and events cause meaning. In **narrative drama**, *thematic necessity* causes events, and physics is merely the stage dressing used to make those events appear inevitable.
> 
> 

To a software engineer, an uncompromising top-down causal arrow appears unimpeachable. To a practicing dramatist, **it is backwards half the time**.

This structural failure mode is codified across the studio architecture as **Chekhov’s Straitjacket**. It occurs when an automated, forward-only build graph treats casual, low-fidelity upstream setups as immutable physical laws, while murdering spontaneous, high-voltage dramatic breakthroughs discovered during dialogue drafting at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

---

### II. The Root Mechanism: Broken Causal Reversibility

#### 1. How Serialized Writing Actually Works vs. Forward Compilation

In high-stakes serial writing (e.g., *Breaking Bad*, *Succession*), writers rarely work as forward-marching state automata.

* **The Emergent Discovery:** A writer or language model drafting Scene 5 discovers an electric line of dialogue, an unexpected physical tic, or a startling metaphor:


> *Bog reaches into his tattered oilskin coat, pulls out a heavy rusted iron spanner, and slams it onto the copper counter.*


* **Human Writers' Room Behavior:** In a human room, the discovery of that spanner is recognized as pure dramatic gold—it sharpens the threat, anchors the subtext, and gives the actors a tactile physical prop to fight over. Writers immediately pause, look back at the whiteboard, and execute an **anterior adjustment**:


> *"Wait, does Bog have the spanner? No, Silas had it in Scene 2. Okay, let’s go back to Scene 2 and add a 4-second beat where Bog quietly pocketed it while Silas was stoking the boiler."*
> 
> 


* **The Forward Compiler's Behavior (Chekhov’s Straitjacket):** Because the engine enforces strict top-down causality ($\text{Canon} \to \text{State} \to \text{Prose}$), the compile-time invariant linter intercepts the candidate prose at $T_0$. It inspects the relational state ledger ($S_n$), evaluates the character's logistical payload (`char_bog.logistical.held`), and finds that `item_iron_spanner` is registered at `loc_pantry_wall` locked inside a chest.



Within **1.8 milliseconds**, the compiler declares a **`LOGISTICAL_BREACH`** or **`UNGROUNDED_PROP_HALLUCINATION`**. The line is murdered on the spot, discarded into the terminal scrapheap before the showrunner even sees it.

```text
❌ CHEKHOV'S STRAITJACKET (Top-Down Causal Tyranny)
Emergent, electric dialogue line drafted in Scene 5 (Stage 5)
  │
  ▼
Stage 5 Prose checked against Stage 1 State Ledger (S_5)
  │
  ▼
Linter detects: Prop not in Bog's pocket (it's in the pantry chest)
  │
  ▼
[TERMINAL FAIL @ T_0 = $0.00]: LOGISTICAL_BREACH
  │
  ▼
Result: Electric line killed; sterile, schema-compliant prose generated instead.
Story degenerates into a predictable tax-accounting exercise.

```



---

### III. The Pathological Symptoms of the Straitjacket

When causal reversibility is broken, the story engine inevitably succumbs to four systemic pathologies:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        PATHOLOGIES OF CHEKHOV'S STRAITJACKET                           │
├───────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. The Sterile Prose Hazard   │ Dialogue satisfies 100% of Zod schemas and somatic SAT  │
│    ("Tax-Accounting Fiction") │ linters, but possesses zero psychological vitality.    │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. The 3-Strike Thrashing     │ Agents thrash endlessly in scene_draft.tmp: fixing a   │
│    Circuit Breaker Trap       │ somatic verb breaks an epistemic rule (Divergence).    │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. The "Micro-Sovereignty"    │ 2d6 tabletop rolls and hex crowding dictate reality;   │
│    Procedural Soap Opera      │ stories become aimless Dwarf Fortress grudge loops.    │
├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 4. The Monolithic Spine       │ Early, casually placed props accumulate in-degree edges│
│    Lock-In                    │ (k_in >= 15); changing them threatens full-season churn│
└───────────────────────────────┴────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. The Sterile Prose Hazard ("Tax-Accounting Fiction")

If an author or generative model must clear 15 canonical invariant gates, equip-slot limits, and foreign-key verifications *before* testing a conversational exchange, creative risk-taking collapses. The system produces what the specifications diagnose as **"mathematically pristine, continuity-checked slop"**: dialogue where characters never reach for an object they don't explicitly hold, never move across an unverified threshold, and never surprise each other, resulting in flat, lifeless exposition that satisfies engineers but repels audiences.

#### 2. The 3-Strike Thrashing Circuit Breaker Trap

When an automated agent (e.g., `Jules`) or human editor attempts to fix a candidate scene trapped inside rigid, mutually exclusive upstream constraints, the system enters an **unresolvable structural paradox**:

* **Attempt 1:** The agent fixes a somatic verb (replacing a banned `sprint` with `limp`).


* **Attempt 2:** The sentence restructuring causes the character to blurt out a forbidden proposition to explain the delay, triggering an `EPISTEMIC_LEAK`.


* **Attempt 3:** The agent removes the secret, but the character now lacks motivation to enter the hex, triggering an axial pathfinding disconnect.



Because causality is strictly forward, the agent cannot adjust the upstream preconditions. On **Attempt 3**, the **Hard Circuit Breaker** trips, executing an atomic git rollback (`git checkout -- .`), dumping a diagnostic escalation dossier, and locking the file. Production halts because the machine refuses to let the script adapt the past.

#### 3. The "Micro-Sovereignty Trap" (Tabletop Simulation $\neq$ Drama)

When forward causality reigns unchecked, the system assumes that tabletop mechanics ($2\text{d}6$ opposed conflict rolls, hex-grid crowding thresholds, and Dijkstra acoustic propagation) should determine "what happens," while the language model merely determines "how it is said".

Over a 12-episode arc, this produces a **procedural soap opera** (like *Dwarf Fortress* or *The Sims*). The characters bicker endlessly over bean tallies and proximity friction, but the narrative is incapable of achieving **Ironic Thematic Closure**. It cannot craft an ending where a character's tragic blind spot snaps shut like a philosophical trap, because forward-marching dice rolls have no concept of narrative inevitability.

#### 4. The Monolithic Spine Lock-In

In long-form serials, Chekhov’s Gun dictates that setups must pay off. But in a forward-only compiler, early setups accumulate massive incoming dependency edges ($k_{\text{in}} = 1 \to 12 \to 45$).

* If a writer placed a brass key in the pantry in Episode 1 as a throwaway background note, and 20 downstream scenes across 4 episodes reference that room, the key becomes a **Monolithic Spine ($R_{\text{blast}} \ge 15$)**.


* Modifying that key to accommodate a brilliant Episode 5 twist threatens a catastrophic invalidation cascade (`is_dirty = 1`), demanding hundreds of dollars in GPU re-renders. The writer is physically trapped inside their own early, unvetted outline.



---

### IV. The Mathematical Cure: Abductive Back-Solving (Reverse Compilation)

To dismantle Chekhov’s Straitjacket without allowing models to hallucinate reality, the studio inverts the execution loop via **Abductive Back-Solving** (or **Reverse Compilation**):

$$\mathbf{\text{Forward Deduction:}}\quad \text{State } S_n + \text{Action } a \xrightarrow{\quad\mathcal{I}\quad} \text{Resulting State } S_{n+1} \text{[cite: 4]}$$

$$\mathbf{\text{Abductive Backpropagation:}}\quad \text{Target Prose } \mathcal{O}_{\text{target}} + \text{Prior State } S_n \xrightarrow{\quad\text{Solver}\quad} \text{Minimum Precursor Events } \vec{e}_{\text{bridge}} \text{[cite: 4, 8]}$$

```text
====================================================================================================
                        RESOLVING CHEKHOV'S STRAITJACKET
====================================================================================================

 [ STEP 0: UNCONSTRAINED PROPOSAL (Axis B Leap) ]
   Writer / LLM drafts Scene 5: "Bog draws the rusted iron spanner from his coat."
   Target Observation: O_target = { item_spanner: HELD_BY(char_bog) at Tick 14,200 }
                               │
                               ▼
 [ DISCREPANCY INTERCEPTION @ T_0 = $0.00 ]
   Linter detects: Pre(O_target) \ S_n != ∅ (Spanner is in pantry chest, not coat)
                               │
                               ▼
 [ THE ABDUCTIVE SOLVER: BACKWARD-CHAINING SEARCH ]
   Searches legal state-action trajectories A* between S_n-k and S_n:
   J(e_bridge) = argmin [ w_1·ΔFrames + w_2·ΔStrain + w_3·d_edit(Prose) ]
                               │
                               ▼
 [ MINIMAL BRIDGING TRANSITION SYNTHESIZED (e_bridge) ]
   Identifies Scene 2 (Tick 4,200) unobserved window:
   Injects 4-second silent Motivation-Reaction Unit (MRU):
   "[Bog slips spanner into his coat while Silas is turned toward the kettle]"
                               │
                               ▼
 [ SHOWRUNNER COCKPIT RATIFICATION (SceneReviewGate.tsx) ]
   "Dialogue requires Bog to hold Spanner. Inject 4s silent setup in Scene 2?"
               ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐
               ▼ [ACCEPT]                      ▼ [REJECT]
   • Retro-injects beat into Scene 2 AST.   Prose pruned via MEU.
   • Commits SQL state delta in D1.         Bog cannot draw spanner.
   • Scene 5 is now 100% CANONICALLY LEGAL.
====================================================================================================

```



#### 1. Why Retroactive Adaptation Does Not Violate the Causal Spine

Engineers initially fear that allowing dialogue to alter upstream history breaks the sacred rule: $\text{Canon} \to \text{Events} \to \text{State} \to \text{Media}$.

**It does not.** The breakthrough insight is that **draft prose sitting in memory is not yet committed Media**; it is an untrusted proposal residing inside the **Mutable Staging Accumulator**.

When the Abductive Solver discovers that the proposed dialogue requires an ungrounded prop or door state, it synthesizes the legal precursor events $\vec{e}_{\text{bridge}} = [e_1, e_2]$ required in the action vocabulary $\mathcal{A}$. When the Showrunner clicks `[ACCEPT]`, those bridging events are stamped into the event ledger **chronologically prior to the scene’s speech event**:

$$S'_n = \text{fold}\left(\text{apply}, \, S_n, \, [e_1, e_2, \dots, e_k]\right) \implies \text{Pre}(\mathcal{O}_{\text{target}}) \subseteq S'_n \text{[cite: 4]}$$

On the permanent, immutable ledger, **causality still flows strictly forward**. The universe remains an unbroken, deterministic chain of events, but the creative intent of the writer is preserved.

#### 2. The Hard Invariant Boundary ($\mathcal{I}_{\text{hard}}$ vs. $\mathcal{S}_{\text{elastic}}$)

To prevent the Abductive Solver from degenerating into "Miracles on Demand" or arbitrary retconning, the engine enforces a strict divide:

* **Hard Ontological Invariants ($\mathcal{I}_{\text{hard}}$ — Zero Elasticity):** The solver is **mathematically barred** from altering terminal somatic states (it cannot regrow an amputated arm), breaching the Epistemic Firewall (it cannot grant knowledge of a secret without sensory contact), or violating high-salience conservation (it cannot teleport a prop across an airtight topological air-gap). If a line requires breaking $\mathcal{I}_{\text{hard}}$, the line is permanently halted.


* **Elastic Staging ($\mathcal{S}_{\text{elastic}}$ — High Elasticity):** Intra-scene spatial blocking, unobserved background movements, minor logistical possessions, and unassigned camera frames have high elasticity. The solver freely rearranges these variables to accommodate artistic breakthroughs.



---

### Summary: From Straitjacket to Living Drama

| Dimension | The Problem: Chekhov's Straitjacket | The Solution: Abductive Back-Solving |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Causal Vector** | Strictly unidirectional forward: $\text{Canon} \to \text{State} \to \text{Prose}$.

 | Bidirectional: Forward enforcement + Backward abductive synthesis.

 |
| **Response to Great Unscripted Dialogue** | **Linter Failure:** Kills prose as a "somatic/logistical breach" at $T_0 = \$0.00$.

 | **Upstream Bridge:** Calculates the minimal upstream delta ($\Delta S_{n-k}$) to make the line true.

 |
| **Editorial Experience** | "Tax-accounting" data entry; constant compiler crashes; writer paralysis.

 | Living creative partner; asks: *"Love that line. Want me to inject a silent setup in Scene 2?"*

 |
| **Narrative Trajectory** | Procedural soap opera (*Dwarf Fortress* grudge loops; "And Then" drift).

 | Cohesive dramatic argument pulled forward by an **Ironic Thematic Attractor**.

 |
| **Systemic Health** | Epistemological Calcification (The Silent Prison of rigid early lore).

 | Continuous, audited upward vitality: canon remains mathematically pure and artistically alive.

 |


 ### I. The Tripartite Studio Topology & The Homeostatic Loop

To achieve multi-season coherence across decades without succumbing to **Epistemological Calcification** (where rigid rules choke artistic surprise) or **The Hallucinatory Drift Spiral** (where generative models hallucinate and contradict established canon), the Camp Candor Cauldron architecture organizes procedural production into three structural columns mediated by **`000.repo-bot`**:

```text
========================================================================================================================
                                     THE THREE-COLUMN ARCHITECTURAL TOPOLOGY
========================================================================================================================
   COLUMN 1: THE LEGISLATIVE CORE           COLUMN 2: THE SOVEREIGN LEDGER           COLUMN 3: CREATIVE DISCOVERY
     (01.canon / 000.goblin-lore)           (02.simulation / series-sower)             (00.discovery / Step 0 / ChatOps)
──────────────────────────────────────   ──────────────────────────────────────   ──────────────────────────────────────
• Axiomatic, immutable physical law      • Append-only historical event stream    • Asynchronous, conversational intake
• Byte-0 Markdown dossiers               • Continuous clockwork world simulation  • Slack channels (#creative, #writing)
• Offline 3-Pass Zod Compiler            • Opposed 2d6 Tabletop Duel Engine       • Quad-Model Divergence Fan-Out
• Cryptographic seal: H_root             • Physical Oracle: goblin-spatial        • Permissive capture ("Accept All")
• Mandate: "What CAN exist"              • Acoustic Clock (ffprobe on TTS stems)  • Unregistered Soft-Linking
                                         • Mandate: "What DID happen"             • Mandate: "What MIGHT be interesting"
========================================================================================================================
                                                           ▲
                                                           │
                                ┌──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┐
                                │             000.repo-bot (THE AIRLOCK)              │
                                │ • Edge-native control plane (Cloudflare Worker/D1)  │
                                │ • Enforces Zero Legislative Authority over chats   │
                                │ • The 4-Tier Creative Ingestion Funnel              │
                                │ • Autonomous Git PR Mechanic & Jules VM Dispatch    │
                                └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



The three columns do not operate in a one-way, linear sequence; they form a **continuous, bi-directional homeostatic flywheel**:

1. **Column 1 (`01.canon` / `000.goblin-lore`):** Defines the legislative boundary conditions ($T_0 = \$0.00$). It establishes somatic capability masks, physical topologies, inventory schemas, and unresolved world mysteries.


2. **Column 2 (`02.simulation` / `000.series-sower-engine` + `000.goblin-spatial`):** Computes physical possibility via stateless integer geometry, resolves encounters through tabletop dice mechanics, measures duration via acoustic synthesis, and appends state transitions ($S_n \to S_{n+1}$) to the historical relational ledger (`CANON_DB`).


3. **Column 3 (`00.discovery` / `000.series-discovery`):** Provides a permissive, unconstrained sandbox where human artists and generative models test unexpected dramatic collisions without breaking production schemas.


4. **The Sovereign Airlock (`000.repo-bot`):** Sits in `Cluster 00.governance` (*Il Sipario Tagliafuoco*). It manages data transiting between the columns, strictly enforcing the rule: **"Untrusted agents propose; deterministic code disposes"**.



---

### II. The Sovereign Artifact Seam: The Three Authorized Crossing Primitives

A foundational law of the studio is the **Monotonic Authority Boundary**: raw prose, conversational Slack chats, and downstream video plates possess **zero legislative authority** and are structurally barred from modifying canon directly:

$$\text{Slack Drops } \cap \text{ Production Authority} = \emptyset \text{[cite: 3, 7]}$$

$$\text{Rendered Media } (\mathcal{R}_m) \not\to \text{State } (\mathcal{S}) \text{[cite: 6, 14]}$$

To cross from Column 3 into Column 1, all exploratory text is discarded. The raw discovery must be distilled and formatted into one of **Three Authorized Crossing Primitives**:

```text
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                        THE THREE AUTHORIZED CROSSING PRIMITIVES                        │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 1. PRE-AUTHORED GRIEVANCE SPRINGS (grievances/*.md)                                    │
│    • Machine Rail: Byte-0 YAML frontmatter declaring intensity limits [I_min, I_max],  │
│      cooldown ticks, Argument Score deltas, and spawns_on_max_escalation DAG edges.   │
│    • Voice Rail: Markdown body declaring the petty "Surface Argument" vs. the          │
│      unspoken existential subtext ("The Underneath").                                  │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 2. CHARACTER REFUSAL VECTORS (characters/*.md)                                         │
│    • Schema: < ENTITY, TRIGGER, MANDATORY_REFUSAL, UNAVOIDABLE_COST >                  │
│    • Declares what a character categorically refuses to do under pressure.            │
│    • Governed by a Strain Threshold: holds until pressure breaches the limit,          │
│      logging a REFUSAL_STRAIN_BREAK event in the simulation ledger.                    │
├────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 3. BOUNDED SOMATIC & LOGISTICAL CAPABILITY MASKS (possessions/*.md, characters/*.md)   │
│    • Anatomical bounds: availableArms (e.g., 1.0), locomotion_baseline.                │
│    • Negative motor masks: banned_kinetic_verbs: ["climb", "bimanual_lift"].           │
│    • Inventory schemas: EquipSlotSchema (worn, held, carried, cached), weight classes. │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



---

### III. Inter-Column Transit Loop A: Forward Compilation & Downward Enforcement

The primary production pipeline flows downward from Column 1 into Column 2, enforcing physical and narrative invariants before generating media:

$$\text{Column 1: Canon Law } \xrightarrow{\text{constrains}} \text{Column 2: Simulation } \xrightarrow{\text{constrains}} \text{Prose AST } \xrightarrow{\text{projects}} \text{Downstream Media} \text{[cite: 7, 17, 23]}$$

```text
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 1. INGESTION AIRLOCK (Column 1 ──► Column 2)                           │
 │    • 000.goblin-lore runs 3-pass compiler (scripts/compile.ts)         │
 │    • Emits sealed bible-state.json with Merkle Root hash (H_root)      │
 │    • worker-sower-engine imports JSON via src/client.ts                │
 │    • Ingested payload passed through recursive deepFreeze<T>()         │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 2. THE MINIMAL TRIAD & SPATIALPORT (Within Column 2)                   │
 │    • Sower extracts localized dynamic hot-set (N_hot <= 12)            │
 │    • Queries goblin-spatial through in-process SpatialPort             │
 │    • Query scrubbed of character names, emotions, and narrative goals  │
 │    • goblin-spatial evaluates pure math (A*, Dijkstra, raycasts)       │
 │    • Emits plain data receipt: { ok: true, path, costUnits }           │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 3. MECHANICAL CONFLICT ARBITRATION (Stage 4)                           │
 │    • Sower evaluates active GrievanceRecord from Column 1              │
 │    • Tabletop Opposed 2d6 Roll: Margin Δ across 1,296 outcomes         │
 │    • Stamped into immutable ScenePlan IR (Pre-resolved exit outcome)   │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 4. LINE 0 SPATIAL STAGE HEADER & CONSTRAINED SCRIPTING                 │
 │    • Injects axial coords, portals, lux, acoustic floor (cdB)          │
 │    • Injects somatic masks: availableArms, banned_kinetic_verbs        │
 │    • Enforces Epistemic Horizon Panopticon (Ignorance by Omission)     │
 │    • Drafts Dialogue AST structured in Dwight Swain MRUs               │
 └───────────────────────────────────┬────────────────────────────────────┘
                                     │
                                     ▼
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
 │ 5. COMPILE-TIME INVARIANT GAUNTLET (T_0 = $0.00)                       │
 │    • Somatic SAT: sum(occupies_hands) <= availableArms                 │
 │    • Motor verb intersection: actions ∩ banned_verbs == ∅              │
 │    • Escalation validator: clamps intensity <= escalation_ceiling      │
 │    • Subtext attenuation scanner: flags forbidden secret lemmas        │
 └────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

```



#### 1. The Ingestion Airlock (`src/client.ts`)

* `000.goblin-lore` compiles source Markdown files into `compiled/under-the-floorboards/bible-state.json` stamped with a 64-character SHA-256 Merkle root ($H_{\text{root}}$).


* Downstream simulation code in `worker-sower-engine` imports this JSON artifact via `src/client.ts` (`bibleLoader.ts`), which wraps the payload in a recursive `deepFreeze<T>()`.


* **Zero Runtime Markdown Parsing:** Downstream consumers are barred from reading raw `.md` files; they consume only the pre-compiled, frozen JSON artifact. Any runtime code attempting to mutate canonical fields immediately throws an unhandled `TypeError`.



#### 2. The Minimal Triad & In-Process `SpatialPort` Contract

To preserve performance and eliminate split-brain state bugs, spatial reality is partitioned between the **Sovereign Ledger** (`worker-sower-engine`) and the **Stateless Physical Oracle** (`goblin-spatial`):

* **Possibility vs. Occurrence:** `goblin-spatial` determines whether an action is physically *possible* (`can_witness`, `can_intercept`, `can_reach`). The Sower determines whether an action *occurred* (`intercepted`, `ambushed`, `retreated`).


* **Scrubbed Boundary Payloads:** Sower serializes only packed integer coordinates, movement budgets, and occluder bitmasks. Character identities, narrative goals, somatic trauma text, and emotional tags are stripped before crossing the `SpatialPort` interface.


* **Zero-Network In-Process Adapter:** Rather than paying a $10\text{--}80\text{ ms}$ HTTP or RPC latency tax, `worker-sower-engine` imports `@goblin/spatial-types` and executes `@goblin/spatial-core` synchronously in-process within the same V8 isolate in sub-millisecond execution envelopes.


* **Bounded Work Envelopes:** Every spatial query enforces an explicit integer limit (`workEnvelopeLimit: 512`). If a pathfinding search expands more than 512 cells, the algorithm halts cleanly and returns `{ ok: false, reason: 'BUDGET_EXCEEDED' }`, preventing infinite loops and turning potential thread hangs into deterministic `SEARCH_ABORTED_FATIGUE` narrative events.


* **The 4-Step Deterministic Tie-Break Cascade:** To prevent identical PRNG seeds from producing divergent character positions due to memory allocation quirks, `findPath` and `resolveDisplacement` break equal-cost path ties using a strict 4-tier cascade:


1. Integer Path Traversal Cost ($g$-score ascending).


2. Exact Integer Chord Distance Squared in doubled coordinates: $(cx_{\text{curr}} - cx_{\text{dest}})^2 + (cy_{\text{curr}} - cy_{\text{dest}})^2$.


3. Pre-scrubbed Seed Tie-Break Digest: $\text{HMAC-SHA256}(\text{episodeSeed}, \text{actorId} \mathbin{\Vert} \text{tick})$.


4. Packed Cell Integer ID (numeric ascending).




* **Overcrowding Eviction (`resolveDisplacement`):** When too many characters enter a confined room, the Sower selects *who* is evicted (based on arrival recency and lowest somatic stability in basis points). `goblin-spatial` runs a budget-bounded Dijkstra flood fill outward from the congested cell to determine *where* they go, navigating open portals to find the nearest legal hex without teleporting characters through solid walls.



#### 3. Mechanical Conflict Arbitration & The Scene Plan Contract

* Before generating prose, `worker-sower-engine` matches co-present actors against Column 1 grievances.


* The conflict is resolved through an opposed $2\text{d}6$ roll modified by character Hegemony, directional leverage Strings, and somatic strain.


* The resulting margin ($\Delta_{\text{margin}}$) locks the outcome into one of four tiers (`DECISIVE_WIN`, `COSTLY_WIN`, `STANDOFF`, `HUMILIATING_LOSS`), emitting an immutable `ScenePlan IR`.



#### 4. The Epistemic Horizon Panopticon & Subtext Attenuation

* **The Hitchcock Matrix:** Mounted at `src/pages/telemetry/epistemic.astro`, the `EpistemicAuditPanel.tsx` lens evaluates the epistemic information delta:



$$\Delta I = K_{\text{audience}} \ominus K_{\text{actor}} \text{[cite: 10, 34]}$$



It audits reality across a 3-column split view: Audience Reality (objective canon in the ledger), Speaker Knowledge (subjective beliefs), and Interlocutor Knowledge (ignorance masks).


* **Ignorance by Omission:** Canonical facts not physically perceived by an actor via optical line-of-sight raycasts or acoustic wavefronts ($\ge \text{threshold } cdB$) computed by `goblin-spatial` are purged from prompt envelopes.


* **Conversational Escalation & Subtext Attenuation Scanner (`10.escalation.unit`):** Candidate lines are scanned at $T_0$ against the actor's `escalation_ceiling` (1 to 5) and forbidden `attenuation_tokens`. If an ungrounded model scripts an actor shouting existential secrets during a domestic argument, the validator flags `[SUBTEXT_ATTENUATION_LEAK]` and aborts the turn before compute is billed.

### IV. Inter-Column Transit Loop B: Creative Discovery Ingress & The 4-Tier Funnel

Column 3 operates as the permissive intake valve, allowing human artists to input raw inspiration without needing to satisfy compiler schemas:

```text
 ┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐

 │ • Binaries streamed to vault/media/ under Content-Addressed SHA-256    │

                                     ▼

 │ • Batch cron (0 0 * * 1) OR On-demand ChatOps (!ResearchDigest <topic>)│
 │ • Multi-modal projection: V_joint = α·V_text (1536-d) ⊕ β·V_vis (768-d)│

 │ • Gateless, unforced character collisions at T_0 = $0.00               │
 │ • Promiscuous generation with 100% disposability                       │

```



1. **Tier 1 (Multi-Modal Drop Sinks):** In Slack (`#creative`, `#research-*`), artists drop visual textures, field recordings, and dialogue ideas without formatting rules.


2. **Tier 2 (Normalized Staging Vault):** `000.repo-bot` captures payloads via signed webhooks, writing Markdown notes to `vault/ingest/` with `status: "raw"`. The **Heuristic Soft Linker** tags prospective entities with associative `[[wikilinks]]` without verifying database closure, allowing unindexed concepts to incubate without crashing production compilers.


3. **Tier 3 (Scheduled Thematic Clustering):** Executed via weekly cron (`0 0 * * 1`) or on-demand ChatOps (`!ResearchDigest <topic>`) to prevent the staging vault from becoming an unsearchable "Dumpster Vault". It maps text (`text-embedding-3-small`, $1{,}536\text{-d}$) and visual art (DINOv2/SigLIP, $768\text{-d}$) into a joint embedding space ($V_{\text{joint}} = \alpha V_{\text{text}} \oplus \beta V_{\text{vis}}$) and runs HDBSCAN density clustering to surface recurring motifs.


4. **Tier 4 / Step 0 (The Gateless Exploration Harness):** Mounted in `DivergenceTriagePanel.tsx` (`src/pages/director/discovery.astro`). The engine broadcasts an unforced character collision across four heterogeneous LLM families simultaneously (Claude 3.5 Sonnet, Qwen 2.5 32B, Llama 3.3 70B, DeepSeek V3) at high temperature ($T = 0.85$) with zero plot stakes. The models act as promiscuous branch generators; the human showrunner acts as the selector of authentic psychological friction.

### V. Inter-Column Transit Loop C: Backward Induction & Abductive Backpropagation

When an unconstrained take in Step 0 or a candidate scene draft produces an electric dramatic moment that violates current ledger state (e.g., *Bog pulls an un-inventoried spanner from his coat to threaten Wart*), the system avoids **Chekhov’s Straitjacket** by executing **Abductive Backpropagation** at $T_0 = \$0.00$:

```text
====================================================================================================
                        ABDUCTIVE BACKPROPAGATION TRANSIT FLOW
====================================================================================================

 [ COLUMN 3: STOCHASTIC PROPOSAL (Axis B Leap) ]
   Writer / LLM drafts Scene 5: "Bog draws the rusted pipe spanner from his coat."
   Target Observation: O_target = { item_spanner: HELD_BY(char_bog) at Tick 14,200 }
                               │
                               ▼
 [ DISCREPANCY INTERCEPTION @ T_0 = $0.00 ]
   Linter detects: Pre(O_target) \ S_n != ∅ (Spanner is in pantry chest, not coat)
                               │
                               ▼
 [ COLUMN 2: ABDUCTIVE SOLVER & BACKWARD-CHAINING SEARCH ]
   worker-sower-engine searches legal precursor trajectories in Action Vocabulary A*:
   J(e_bridge) = argmin [ w_1·ΔFrames + w_2·ΔStrain + w_3·Count(UnratifiedProps) + w_4·d_edit ]
   goblin-spatial verifies reachability and LoS in Scene 2 (Tick 4,200)
                               │
                               ▼
 [ SYNTHESIS OF MINIMAL BRIDGING TRANSITION (e_bridge) ]
   Synthesizes 4-second silent Motivation-Reaction Unit (MRU):
   "[Bog slips spanner into his coat while Silas is turned toward the kettle]"
                               │
                               ▼
 [ HUMAN RATIFICATION GATE (SceneReviewGate.tsx) ]
   "Dialogue requires Bog to hold Spanner. Inject 4s silent setup in Scene 2?"
               ┌───────────────┴───────────────┐
               ▼ [ACCEPT]                      ▼ [REJECT]
   • Retro-injects beat into Scene 2 AST.   Prose pruned via MEU.
   • Commits SQL state delta in D1.         Current ledger preserved.
   • Scene 5 is now 100% CANONICAL.
                               │
                               ▼
 [ COLUMN 1: CANONICAL RULE INDUCTION (000.goblin-lore) ]
   Candidate Invariant Extraction Parser extracts Refusal Vector:
   RefusalRule = < char_wart, "acoustic_pipe_tapping", "refuse_to_admit_deficit", "volume_escalates" >
   000.repo-bot commits characters/wart.md PR -> scripts/compile.ts reseals H_root
====================================================================================================

```



#### 1. Backward-Chaining Search in Column 2

* The engine treats the candidate text as a **Target Observation ($\mathcal{O}_{\text{target}}$)** whose physical preconditions are unsatisfied by current state ($S_n$).


* The Abductive Solver searches the bounded Action Vocabulary $\mathcal{A}$ for a sequence of precursor micro-transitions ($\vec{e}_{\text{bridge}} = \langle e_1, \dots, e_k \rangle$) that satisfies $\text{Pre}(\mathcal{O}_{\text{target}}) \subseteq S'_n$.


* It evaluates candidate solutions by minimizing the **Backward-Chaining Cost Function**:

$$J(\vec{e}_{\text{bridge}}) = w_1 \sum_{j=1}^{k} \Delta F(e_j) + w_2 \sum_{j=1}^{k} \Delta \text{Strain}_{\text{bps}}(e_j) + w_3 \cdot \text{Count}(\text{UnratifiedProps}) + w_4 \cdot d_{\text{edit}}(\text{Prose}) \text{[cite: 6, 40]}$$



The solver prefers injecting a brief silent beat into an unobserved scene window over rewriting dialogue or triggering massive video re-renders.



#### 2. Post-Selection Rule Induction into Column 1

When a dramatic take reveals an authentic character boundary, the system executes **Post-Selection Grounding**:

* The engine enforces a **Negative Extraction Filter**: it never extracts rules prior to human selection, preventing human operators from skimming summaries rather than reading scenes.


* Working backward from the winning dialogue, the extraction parser isolates the psychological turning point and constructs a formal **Refusal Vector** bound to the canonical schema:

$$\text{RefusalRule} = \langle \text{ENTITY}, \, \text{TRIGGER}, \, \text{MANDATORY\_REFUSAL}, \, \text{UNAVOIDABLE\_COST} \rangle \text{[cite: 10, 38, 43]}$$


* The raw exploratory dialogue is discarded. `000.repo-bot` branches the repository, appends the refusal vector to `000.goblin-lore/characters/*.md`, runs `scripts/compile.ts` to reseal $H_{\text{root}}$, and establishes the discovered trait as permanent universe law.



#### 3. Trajectory Routing & The 3-Cycle Escalation Matrix

When automated repair agents (e.g., `Jules`) resolve rule conflicts during backpropagation, the system audits the **Error Trajectory Vector ($\vec{E}$)** across attempts ($N \le 3$) to prevent infinite repair loops:

```typescript
export interface DiagnosticEscalationReport {
  escalation_id: string;
  scene_id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  classification: 'LOCAL_STYLE_FAILURE' | 'CONSTRAINT_CONFLICT' | 'UPSTREAM_STRUCTURAL';
  assigned_target_role: 'BEAT_WRITER' | 'SCENE_ARCHITECT' | 'SHOWRUNNER';
  attempt_history: Array<{
    attempt_number: number;
    generated_text: string;
    violation_category: 'SOMATIC' | 'LOGISTICAL' | 'EPISTEMIC';
    violation_message: string;
  }>;
  conflict_root_cause: {
    conflicting_invariants: string[];
    constraint_provenance: Array<{
      invariant_key: string;
      originating_scene_id: string;
      originating_commit_hash: string;
    }>;
  };
  recommended_remediation: string;
}

```



| Observed Trajectory | Diagnostic Meaning | Deterministic Action & Target Assignee |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Oscillation ($R_A \to R_B \to R_A$)**

 | Mutually exclusive constraints ($C_{\text{somatic}} \cap C_{\text{epistemic}} = \emptyset$).

 | **Trip Circuit Breaker Immediately:** Abort at $T_0$; route `DiagnosticEscalationReport` to **Scene Architect**.

 |
| **Progressive Unmasking ($R_A \to R_B$)**

 | Fixing Defect $A$ unmasked downstream Defect $B$; rules are not proven disjoint.

 | **Authorize Combined Retry:** Inject combined constraints ($R_A \land R_B$) into the prompt rail.

 |
| **Convergence / Phrasing Basin ($R_A \to R_A$)**

 | Model trapped in repetitive phrasing basin; rules are satisfiable.

 | Apply amplified negative token mask; route to **Beat Writer** for 30s cockpit edit.

 |
| **Ontological Impasse (Cycle 3 Exceeded)**

 | Foundational lore contradicts active arc.

 | Reset to $S_{\text{clean}}$; escalate to **Showrunner** for scoped exemption or retcon.

 |

The pipeline does not require centralized status meetings; it coordinates through **environmental stigmergy**—where autonomous subsystems leave machine-readable traces in shared storage (`CANON_DB`), triggering work across adjacent columns:

```text
 │    • harvest.astro computes Salience: S = 0.4·Recurrence + 0.6·Drama   │

 │    • Surfaces audit brief to Showrunner: "Does this rule still serve?" │
 │    • Moves fact through Tri-State Lifecycle: ACTIVE ──► FLAGGED ──► RET│

```



#### 1. Algorithmic Starvation Prompts

When `000.series-sower-engine` runs background simulation cycles, it computes **Normalized Pairing Entropy ($H_{\text{norm}}$)** across all $\binom{N}{2}$ dyadic channels:

* If two characters spend multiple ticks in adjacent rooms without an eligible grievance to arbitrate, the engine detects **Dyadic Starvation** ($k_{\text{in}} = 0$).


* `000.repo-bot` dispatches an automated prompt to `#writers-room` in Slack, challenging writers to pitch a new grievance via `!PitchGrievance` to populate the narrative vacuum.



#### 2. The Harvest Queue & NotebookLM Retro-Injection (`harvest.astro`)

When downstream rendering models hallucinate unscripted visual props (e.g., an un-modeled green enamel cup on a bench), the system navigates between the **Radical Omission Trap** (discarding all incidental world texture) and **Accidental Canon via Model Mimicry** (allowing hallucinations to break inventory schemas):

* The **Harvest Queue** (`src/pages/telemetry/harvest.astro`) intercepts emergent props and evaluates their **Salience Score**:



$$S = (0.4 \cdot \text{Recurrence}) + (0.6 \cdot \text{Dramatic\_Salience}) \text{[cite: 7, 27]}$$


* If $S \ge 0.70$, the item is quarantined in `unratified_details.jsonl`.


* The system cross-queries the series research vault in **NotebookLM** to discover if the prop has latent narrative provenance.


* If the showrunner ratifies the item, `000.repo-bot` commits `possessions/green_mug.md` to Column 1, runs `compile.ts` to update $H_{\text{root}}$, and logs an initial acquisition event in Column 2, safely integrating the emergent prop into canon.



#### 3. The "Still Good" Protocol

To protect long-running series from **The Silent Prison** (where early, casual worldbuilding notes permanently calcify future storytelling), the engine monitors graph centrality:

* When an early fact's in-degree centrality reaches $k_{\text{in}} \ge 12$ and has not been reviewed by a human in $> 60\text{ days}$, the **Auteur Reconsideration Interface** surfaces the **"Still Good" Audit Brief**.


* Facts move through an explicit **Tri-State Standing Lifecycle** (`ACTIVE_ENDORSED`, `FLAGGED_FOR_REVIEW`, `DEPRECATED`), allowing writers to debate and retcon lore in staging branches without triggering immediate invalidation cascades across active production lines.

 |
